# BABA ANANDAMURTIJI'S LIFE – A MYSTERY

Shrii Prabhat Rainjan Sarkar was BABA's worldly name and SHRII SHRII ANANDAMURTI is HIS spiritual one. HIS devotees call HIM as simply BABA (the dearest one). BABA was born in the month of May, 1921 on Vaishaki Purnima full-moon day (Buddha Purnima day) at Jamalpur, Bihar state, in Eastern India.

Once HIS mother came with a bottle of milk to feed HIM, while HE was still a baby of a few weeks old. Even though the bottle was kept a little away, she was astonished to find the bottle to be empty. The little baby had taken the milk from the bottle just by holding it with HIS tender hands. The mother was utterly surprised and in amazement called him "Burha" - an old man. She later used to call him "Bubu" – a loving baby.

Once as a schoolboy, BABA's elder sister scolded HIM and asked HIM to devote more time in reading and learning. She was astonished to see that BABA had written her name 'Abha' in eight different scripts. She then realized that her brother Bubu was not an ordinary child.

When HE was very young, he was found doing meditations in lonely and solitary places for fairly long time by HIMSELF. The village woodcutters used to claim that they sometimes saw BABA deep in the woods riding on a tiger.

While very young, HE fluently recited Dyana Mantra of Lord Shiva in Sanskrit language in a local temple in Jamalpur. The priests of the temple were amazed to listen to HIM reciting such difficult Sanskrit slokas. The local astrological priests had predicted seeing HIS talents that the child would be a very great personality and would establish one Universal Religion, One Human Society by dispelling all narrow sentiments like caste, creed, colour, religion and even the barrier of national feeling. They also predicted that HIS name would be known all over the world and HIS ideas would guide humanity for thousand years to come.

HE was considered as an usual "college-going boy of Ishvarchandra Vidyasagar college in Calcutta. There HE would go for long walks by the Ganges. One night a notorious dacoit by the name of Kalicharan approached BABA with the intent to rob HIM. Instead, BABA called Kalicharan over by name and initiated him onto the spiritual path. This dacoit in one night was transformed into a great spiritualist and was later known by the name Kalikananda.

Throughout the 40's and 50's, HE led life of a normal householder, working at a railway workshop in Jamalpur as an accountant. HE was punctual, sincere, and respected for HIS work, but behind the scenes, BABA was secretly initiating more and more people into spiritual sadhana.

Once an Englishman wanted to go to England, as his seriously ill wife was hospitalized. BABA advised him that there was no need for him to travel to England. Later when the Englishman's wife came to Jamalpur, she was surprised to see BABA. She said that HE was the same doctor at the hospital in England, who in fact saved her life.

Finally in 1955 at request of HIS devotees, BABA started Ananda Marga, a socio-spiritual relief organization currently working in more than 200 countries as United Nations International Non Governmental Organisation - NGO (AMURT).

Devotees once requested BABA, to write HIS autobiography. In response to this, BABA called a worker to have HIS autobiography dictated. The worker came with voluminous notebook to take down what BABA said, but all BABA said was "I am a mystery, I was a mystery, and I will forever remain a mystery."

With my limited intellect it is impossible to explain such a great personality. All that I can do is relate to the readers my own personal experiences with BABA, my Master. If this book succeeds in even remotely arousing a feeling of devotion, I will consider that I am able to achieve something in my efforts. Here are the stories of my personal experiences with BABA.

-- ACARYA SAVITANANDA AVADHUTA

# PART- I

## POLARISATION

### 1. BABA THE DEAREST

All the languages of the world usually start with the letter A. Why is this? Why not the letter B or C, for example? What is the reason behind it? It is an interesting question. In order to understand this point, one has to trace the original source from which all sounds have evolved.

One million years ago there were no humans on Earth and several millions of years ago there were no mammals and even further back, hundreds of millions years ago there were only fish and amphibians swimming in the sea. Amphibians used to come up from the water to the surface of the sea and breathe out air, thus making the sound "A." This is how the sound A originated from the amphibians, continued through reptiles, mammals, and monkeys until reaching its final destination with human beings. Hence, humans use the letter and sound A at the very beginning of all existing languages of the world.

Who was there at that time to watch those first primordial amphibians come up from the sea, gasping for breath? Who was there to witness that first exhalation of exhalations, that first "A" of all A's? None other than the Supreme Witness Himself, naturally.

This was the gist of a discourse given by the great Spiritual Master Shrii Shrii Anandamurti, an all-knowing Entity. Ordinary spiritual masters have to spend years upon torturous years performing intense spiritual practices and penance in order to gain any sort of spiritual realization - from Ramakrishna to Yogananda to Buddha and back again. Shrii Shrii Anandamurti is different in that respect. HE established HIMSELF as all-knowing Pure Consciousness right from birth, thus exhibiting status of omniscience, as it was most natural for HIM.

The universe is full of energy. The Supreme Consciousness controls the energy of the universe. Consciousness and Energy always remain together; they can never be separated. They always exist together, just like two sides of a piece of paper or two sides of a coin. The combined name of both Consciousness and Energy is called BRAHMA (INFINITY). This is a new concept given by the Great Spiritual Master Shrii Shrii Anandamurti, affectionately called BABA by HIS devotees (which means dearest, nearest).

Amongst many other things, BABA was the profounder of a new scientific theory known as MICROVITA. According to this new theory, BABA states that every electron is further sub-divided into many smaller, minute particles. Modern science has no means of measuring it. But in the future, science will discover the truth; that an electron is made of millions of subtler particles, which BABA calls Microvita. This Microvitum particle (called here in its singular form) is the fundamental unit of Energy.

Mind is a creation of matter, but BABA said furthermore that matter is nothing but a crudified form of Consciousness and also the creation of Consciousness Itself. The universe is full of Energy but the source of Energy and the source of Microvita is Pure Consciousness. Therefore, everything in its essence is nothing but an expression of the Supreme Consciousness because after all, Consciousness is everything.

Pure Consciousness is the subject, which gets crudified due to the influence of Energy and Microvita into matter and mind, and matter and mind itself becomes the object of Consciousness.

I was discussing this once with the top Indian nuclear scientists of Trombay Atomic Research Center in Mumbai, western part of India, and came upon the subject of the source of the enormous energy released from atoms during nuclear fissions or nuclear fusions. The weight that is lost from atoms is negligible compared to the tremendous amount of energy released from atoms during these processes.

$$\text{(E = MC}^2\text{) (Energy = Mass }\times\text{ (Speed of Light)}^2\text{)}$$

Energy is a blind force and to function properly it requires control and proper direction. Thus, Consciousness is playing the role of both controller and director; only HE finally controls all the energy of the universe. The source of Energy released from atoms is Pure Consciousness according to the Microvita Theory of BABA.

A human being is composed of three bodies. They are:

(1) The Physical Body - made of the five fundamental factors i.e., solid, liquid, luminous, aerial and ethereal

(2) The psychic body, which is composed of three parts: existential mind ('I' feeling), doer or planner mind (Doer I) and the objective mind (Done I)

(3) The Spiritual body; the soul; the Unit Consciousness; the reflection of Pure Consciousness.

The Sleeping Divinity or Kulakundalini (coiled serpentine) is stationed at the lowest plexus (Muladhara Cakra) at the bottom of the spine. As one develops spiritually, one's kundalini starts its journey towards higher cakras further along the spinal cord up towards the head. As one moves and establishes oneself in the higher plexuses or cakras, one becomes a Master of a certain degree; they can guide others. Thousands of Masters

came to help and guide humanity over the course of our long history. Highest is the One who has established HIMSELF in the Sahasrara Cakra (located at the top of Head) like Lord Shiva, Lord Krishna and BABA. It is they who are the perfect Masters, the Masters of all Masters. It is they who are fully established in Pure Consciousness. Such Great Masters are called Liberators or the Taraka Brahma.

Seven thousand years ago Lord Shiva made his advent upon this dusty Earth already established in that highest Sahasrara Cakra. Born perfect, liberated and omniscient, Lord Shiva came to this Earth to save humanity from the crisis of the period.

Three thousand and five hundred years ago, Lord Krishna came again to establish Dharma (morality). And on the historic fields of Kurukshetra, Krishna proclaimed to Arjuna, "From age to age I come again and again for the protection of the virtuous and the destruction of the wicked. When adharma (immorality) reigns and dharma is descendent, I create MYSELF out of MY own maya."

Now that same perfect Master has taken advent again as Lord Shrii Shrii Anandamurti, as BABA. Merely thinking of HIM can give one perfect peace of mind. BABA is the giver of Bliss, (eternal happiness) hence HIS name - ANANDAMURTI (embodiment of Bliss). BABA is a devotional personality. One can understand HIM only through devotion, and not in the dusty pages of books, this one included.

The more we trust and depend on HIM, the more one realizes HIM. BABA is the ancient Guru of all Gurus, the Master of all Masters. All masters refer to HIM as the final Master. BABA is that same Shiva of the Himalayas, that same Krishna of the Mahabharata. Even now, people who have faith in Shiva and Krishna realise BABA as their Supreme Guide.

"The Supreme Spirit within humanity, neglected for ages, has awakened today. This awakening will initiate a new chapter in human history. You will all be the pioneers of the new trend." - BABA

## 2. LORD SHIVA - KRISHNA - BABA ANANDAMURTI

Here follows a popular story as told in South India about Lord Shiva. There was once a cowherd boy meditating on Lord Shiva every day. While doing his meditation, he would leave his cattle at the altar of his Lord. He thought, "Let me meditate on Lord Shiva and let Lord Shiva take care of my cows and buffaloes." As long as the boy was meditating on Shiva, Lord Shiva HIMSELF would appear as a human being to take care of the cattle. The more one trusts deeply in the Lord, the more the Lord also responds accordingly.

The same was the case with Lord Krishna. Everyday a boy was going to school with his friends, and they would cross a dense forest along the way. One day he had to go alone as all the boys already left for school without him. He requested his mother to accompany him, as he was afraid of crossing the dense forest alone, but his mother told him, "Just call your elder brother Lord Krishna. HE is sure to cross the forest with you." The boy, simple as he was, believed what his mother said to him. He left home and entered the forest.

"Oh, Krishna!" He called loudly into the woods. "Oh, Krishna! My elder brother! Come and take me through the forest." Lord Krishna appeared in front of the boy as an elder brother and escorted him through the forest just as the boy's mother had said would happen. When they approached the school, Lord Krishna told the boy that HE would appear whenever he needed HIM. Saying this, HE disappeared. And every day following, Lord Krishna would respond to the calls of the boy, his "younger brother." The pure and sincere calls of the boy simply melted Krishna's heart.

One day the boy's teacher asked him how he was coming all alone through the dense forest every day. "Lord Krishna, my elder brother escorts me!" The boy replied. The teacher was

surprised. He believed that Lord Krishna was omnipresent, but never did he see Lord Krishna's actual physical form. The teacher also accompanied the boy just to see his Lord Krishna. The boy called for Lord Krishna in the forest but as the teacher was escorting him, Lord Krishna did not appear. The teacher came just to test if it was true or not, but not really with the firm faith to see Lord Krishna. FAITH IS EVERYTHING for the spiritual progress.

In order to realise and to visualise the Lord, one needs firm faith, full surrender and complete dependence on HIM. Only then does the Lord respond accordingly. The same is the case with Lord Anandamurti, BABA. HE is attending to and helping all those who are totally surrender to HIM.

It is an interesting fact that Lord Shiva was born on Amavasya (new-moon day), Lord Krishna was born on the eighth day of the lunar cycle and BABA was born at dawn during Purnima (full-moon day), this completes the cycle Amavasya, Astami - eighth day and Purnima.

All my life, I was worshiping the Lord in one form or another. I was really willing to talk to the Lord. Sometimes HE appears to me in different forms. HE appears in my thoughts and guides me when I need HIM. In this book I will be narrating in my stories how I have been feeling BABA all my life. I think, rather I know, that every person can feel BABA. HIS love and care is always there to help one and all forever.

> "The Supreme Being is calling you in the roar of the ocean, in the thunder of the clouds, in the speed of the lightning, in the meteor's flaming fires." – BABA

# 3. POLARISATION AT THE TIME OF LORD SHIVA

Seven thousand years ago, when Lord Shiva came, the majority of the population of the world was living in the area of India due to its congenial climatic conditions. Even birds migrated to India. So humans also followed the routes of the birds and the animals and ended up in India.

At this time, the White Aryan race had come into India and started exploiting all the non-Aryans. The Aryans had a Vedic culture (involving rituals and Yajinas, sacred fires) from Europe whence they came. The indigenous people of India possessed a Tantric culture that emphasized on mental purification, which led to simple-hearted and good-natured temperaments. It placed no importance on rites or rituals. This, amongst other things, created a lot of clash amongst the Aryans and Non-Aryans.

Thus, India became a melting pot of many races, four in particular. There were the Aryans, white people, who were well organized in war. They arrived in India from Europe and Central Asia and eventually conquered North India. The Mongolians, of yellow complexion, who settled in the East of India; the Austerics, brown colored, who settled in Central India; and black colored, Negroes (Dravidians), who settled in the South of India.

Lord Shiva was the first manifestation of Taraka Brahma (liberator) on this earth, and it was because of HIM that India was saved from the destruction of racial and inter-cultural wars. He taught yoga, tantra, meditation, music, dances, herbal medicine, etc., but perhaps one of the most important things He did was He brought unity amongst the races by introducing for the first time the system of marriages; marriages between people of different races, which brought about racial and cultural blending and also made men take responsibility for their offspring. Before this goal could be achieved, however, there was polarization, as there always is during Taraka Brahma's advent.

Lord Shiva married Gaorii, also called Parvati (mountain girl) or Hemavati (snow girl) from the Aryan race, Kali from the Negro race, and Ganga from the Mongolians; Lord Shiva Himself was a blend of Aryan and Austeric. At that time it was common for men to live with more than one woman, especially because there were many more women than the number of men. Lord Shiva married one woman from each of these three different races just to bring unity amongst all the four races. Lord Shiva taught spirituality to unite all, and traveled vast distances across the world to spread the message of Universalism.

Daksha was an Aryan King and also the father of Gaorii. He did not like that his daughter had married a nomad and he especially felt threatened by Lord Shiva's progressive ideas. For Daksha, the Aryans were supreme, but for his daughter Gaorii, Shiva was the Lord of the Universe, everything.

Daksha held a meeting of all the different Aryan kings to mobilize the "superior" Aryan race against this Shiva, this nomad who was compromising their racial exploitation by preaching and bringing about unity. And so all the Aryan kings assembled at a place in the North in the Himalayas to conduct a Yaiina, a sacrificial fire. This Yaiina they used as a symbol of their Vedic culture to oppose Shiva's simplistic Tantric culture.

Gaorii thought that she should go to this Yaiina, but Lord Shiva advised her that one should not go to a place or Yaiina uninvited. Gaorii did not listen to Him, thinking that a daughter did not need permission to visit her father. Family sentiment influenced her, and because of this, she did not heed the advice of Lord Shiva; she went with her son last to attend Daksha's Yaiina, which had already started and had been going on for last few days.

Gaorii and Bhaerava entered the assembly. Her parents and all the others ignored her, as they were completely full of their own egos, their own superiority complexes. When Gaorii tried to reach out to them, they only humiliated her and insulted her and Lord Shiva. Only then Gaorii could understand why Lord Shiva had told her not to go.

Gaorii could not tolerate the insults had meted to her and her Lord Shiva. Not being able to bear it anymore, she walked over to the fireplace, the Yaiina, their sacrificial fire, and jumped in it. Bhaerava, her son, saw how his mother was insulted, how the exploiters led his dear mother to death, but more than that, he also saw how all the Cruel Aryan exploiters were all rounded up in one place; he took an oath that he would kill all who were present there and burn all their heads in the same fire that had just engulfed his mother.

Bhaerava was a great Tantrik, initiated into the process of Tantra Sadhana by the Lord Shiva Himself. He gathered up Shiva's gana (Shiva's group) right away and killed all the narrow-minded Aryan kings assembled there and burnt their heads in that same Yaiina. They had built the fire to worship but now they were consumed by their own Vedic dogma. In one fell swoop; all the evil forces were wiped out.

It looked as though the Aryans, all mobilized in one place, would certainly decide to unite and attempt to kill Lord Shiva for His Universalistic actions, but instead, all the immoral forces were wiped out in one clear swipe at the sacrifice of Lord Shiva's own wife. This was the polarization at the time of Lord Shiva. After the divisive forces of the society were decimated, Lord Shiva could now freely blend the races, and humanity advanced further. As a result, the Great Indus River valley civilization and Chinese civilization rose to glory 5000 years ago, 2000 years after Shiva. Shiva is the father of the Human civilization.

> "Shiva looked upon all the living beings of the universe as HIS loving children. He raised them with loving care and, at the end of their physical existence, pulled them unto HIS affectionate lap." - BABA

## 4. POLARISATION AT THE TIME OF LORD KRISHNA

Lord Krishna, the second advent of Taraka Brahma, came around 3500 years ago. The immoral forces, working under the influence of negative forces (Negative Microvita - crude force) were powerful even before the birth of Lord Krishna. Kamsa, who was the evil king ruling the area at the time, was told in a prophecy that Lord Krishna, the eighth child of his own sister, would some day rise up and kill him. Hearing this, Kamsa ordered the extermination of all of Krishna's brothers and sisters. Lord Krishna, who was born in jail, was removed secretly in the dead of night to a safe location. All (but one of) Krishna's seven elder siblings were killed by their own uncle, Kamsa.

Lord Krishna grew up in the village of Vrindavan as a cowherd boy. Many agents and spies were sent by Kamsa to find and kill Him, but Lord Krishna was able to kill them all. At last when He grew to the age of adolescence, Lord Krishna rose up and killed Kamsa. All the immoral kings of India were infuriated to hear that Kamsa had been killed by a mere cowherd boy and united together to attack Lord Krishna. Lord Krishna retreated from His uncle's capital, Mathura and crossed a thousand miles of desert, leaving India before settling on an island in the Arabian Sea. There He built a town on the island called Dwaraka, organizing it into one of the most powerful, attractive centers of the world. He trained the Yadava Sena (force) to fight against the immoralists of India.

The cruel Indian kings felt jealous of Lord Krishna. Here was a simple cowherd boy, and now He was growing richer day by day. All the kings of India chose one representative, Shishupal and told him to attack Dwaraka and kill Lord Krishna. They gave him an army and full support. Shishupal crossed the desert, came to the sea and with the help of boats reached the island of Dwaraka.

Lord Krishna and the Yadavas destroyed the entire attacking army and spared only one i.e., Shishupal. This was done at Lord Krishna's own strict instructions. Shishupal went back to India alone and contacted all the same immoral kings; they gave him a new army to attack Krishna with, and with this, Shishupal returned to Dwaraka. Lord Krishna and His army destroyed the attacking army once again; Shishupal was once again spared.

The Yadavas asked Lord Krishna, why was he sparing Shishupal, who was his main enemy. "I have no time to go to India and find all my enemies myself; Shishupal is collecting them for me. Shishupal is doing my work only!"

This went on eighteen times, the attack by the Indian immoral kings through Shishupal. When they got exhausted and completely ruined, finally Lord Krishna told the Yadava Sena to invade India. "India is waiting for us," Lord Krishna told them. The Yadava Sena became soft from years of prosperity, and refused Lord Krishna's order. Hearing this, Lord Krishna left His own army and went to India to defeat the immoralists Himself.

In India, about 150 kilometers south of Delhi (the present capital of India), there was one kingdom, the capital city of which was called Hastinapur. There lived two princes' families, one was called the Kauravas and another called the Pandavas. The Pandavas were five simple-hearted brothers who became devotees of Lord Krishna, learning His meditation and believing Him to be God. Their stepbrothers, the Kauravas, who had grabbed power, put these Pandavas through a lot of difficulties, exploiting them at every opportunity.

The Kauravas tried to kill all the five Pandavas by burning their house, but Lord Krishna saved them through the minister Vidura, a devotee of Krishna's. The Pandavas eventually won the support of a king by marrying his daughter Draupadi, winning her marriage through an archery competition. The Pandavas agreed to keep Draupadi as a wife amongst themselves. This arrangement served to keep unity, so that they could collectively fight against the powerful and immoral Kauravas.

After the Pandavas won the princess, Lord Krishna guided them to get half of the kingdom, but still the Kauravas only gave

them land filled with impenetrable forests. Still, the Pandavas, under the guidance of Lord Krishna built a great city, Indraprastha, located at present Delhi, the capital of India.

The Kauravas felt jealous of the Pandavas' prosperity and popularity, so they used a black magician to defeat the Pandavas in gambling and in that way won the whole kingdom from them. That still wasn't enough for the Kauravas; they tried to make the Pandavas their slaves and tried to disrobe their wife, Draupadi. Lord Krishna paralyzed the hands of the wicked persons trying to disrobe her and rescued Draupadi. She surrendered to Lord Krishna and Lord Krishna saved her honour and prestige.

Later on the Pandavas were gambling again, and this time they lost their entire kingdom and had to go to the forest in exile for thirteen years. Thirteen years came and went, but still the Kauravas refused to return their kingdom. Hearing this, Lord Krishna prepared the Pandavas for a final battle against the Kauravas. All the immoralist Kings of India and beyond came with their armies to finish off Lord Krishna and His Pandavas. Even the Yadavas, Krishna's own army, fought against Lord Krishna in the end.

The final polarization happened between the Kauravas, the immoralists, and the Pandavas, the moralists, the devotees of Lord Krishna. This event divided all of society into two opposing camps. Even the kings of the Philippines and the Middle East took part in this Great War, this Mahabharata.

None of the immoralist forces thought they would lose. They had a vast army and the Pandavas' forces were so small in comparison. Still the Pandavas never lost faith; Arjuna had Lord Krishna Himself was His chariot-driver. The great battle continued on for eighteen days.

The immoralist forces were defeated by the Pandavas, thus setting an example for all time that the evil forces, however powerful they might be, would certainly one day meet their end, would perish. This was the lesson taught by the Mahabharata war.

After the war, Lord Krishna and the Pandavas worked very hard for twenty years more to build up every village of India. All the villages were marching towards spiritual growth, developing materially and mentally as well. Peace and prosperity continued in the society due to the efforts of Lord Krishna and the Pandavas, under His guidance.

Whenever Taraka Brahma comes, whether in the form of Lord Shiva, Lord Krishna, or BABA, HE polarizes humanity into two opposing camps and brings about enormous clash and cohesion in society that will eventually serve to establish HIS Ideology. When such things happened in Lord Shiva's and Lord Krishna's lifetime, then it is only natural that similar clashes would occur with us. Ananda Marga is the organization that BABA founded to carry out and establish HIS Ideology. It represents Universalism and Cosmic Fraternity in its purest form. When such an organization comes with such an Ideology, then this forces people to polarize into one of the two opposing camps – one for the Universalism and the other against the Universalism. When I narrate my stories, only keep in mind that Ananda Marga is not an ordinary organization, but an organization founded with the purpose to establish the True Ideology – the Ideology of Universalism, of Cosmic Fraternity.

At the time of Lord Shiva narrow-minded kings came together but were finally eliminated. Lord Krishna started a war and wiped out all the immoral forces Himself. These clashes may have been great, but the results of those clashes were greater; it was only because of these clashes that Lord Shiva was able to blend the races and unite all culturally, that Lord Krishna was able to unite all of South Asia under Dharma. BABA'S life is very significant in establishing One, Universal and integrated Human Society.

"Lord Krishna gave assurance by proclaiming, 'I incarnate Myself in this world from age to age for the annihilation of the wicked and the protection of virtuous, I will liberate you from all sins. You have no cause to worry.' Krishna has no parallel or equal. Krishna is *Parama Purusa* (The Supreme Consciousness) HIMSELF." - BABA

## 5. POLARISATION NOW AT THE TIME OF BABA

One million years ago, there was a polarization between some monkeys on one side and some more intelligent, slightly more evolved human-tending monkeys on the other. They fought during one day, and by evening, man had made its final separation with apes, and thus humans made the leap from simian to sapiens, leaving the monkeys behind for caves and trees before evolving into the present day humans. This historic polarization took only one day. Though humans evolved one million years ago, the present human civilization, we notice, is only 15,000 years old. The past one million years was a long, drawn out history consisting of not much more than ice ages, fights and negative influences of nature etc.

About seven thousand years ago, Lord Shiva made His advent on this earth to give a galloping jump to human evolution, to make humans truly human. He came with Universal, progressive ideas that were far ahead of His time. It is only natural that coming with such ideas would polarise all the negative forces against Him as they did. This polarisation was settled in one night, when His son, Bhaerava, killed all the Aryan kings.

Three thousand and five hundred years ago Lord Krishna came to unite all of India under the banner of Dharma. It took sixty years to completely polarise all the vast forces, and this time the polarisation was settled in eighteen days – the eighteen days of the battle of Kuruksetra.

Today, the polarisation that BABA has created is between ideas and ideologies, between mass movements and sentiments rather than between kings, armies and brute forces. BABA came on this earth to increase this process of polarisation, to speed up our collective spiritual evolution, but because so many ideas are involved, because every human and every aspect of human life across the globe rests in the balance, then this process is taking longer than one lifetime to settle. Hence, BABA's physical life of seventy years was not enough to see through the entire process of polarisation. It is still continuing, and in fact is gaining momentum and will continue to accelerate until that final culminating point - the apex of polarisation.

BABA said that there are four major types of philosophies, ideas or motives that are currently at work on this earth. The first one is Matter-centered ideology, like Communism, which as you will read in the story, BABA spent HIS entire life fighting against all alone. The second one is Self-centered ideology, like present-day capitalism, which many economists say is soon to meet its doomsday. The third one is Dogma-centered ideology, like religion and narrow sentiments based on race, language, nation, region etc., which is now being challenged by Universalist warriors. The fourth one is God-centered ideology – PROUT (BABA's socio-economic theory) and Ananda Marga. The polarisation that BABA has sent into motion and is still setting into motion will eventually establish the Genuine Ideology – God-centered Ideology. It is the Sadvipras (spiritual moralists) who will win the day, and the creator of the Sadvipras is the Maha Sadvipra, BABA HIMSELF. This is the polarization going on now at the time of BABA.

> "Now humanity bleeds. The future is dark. So we have come here to do something. I have come here to do something, and you have also come here to do something. My coming is significant, and your coming is not less significant. We have come with a mission; and our lives, singularly and collectively, are a mission. Not missions - ours is a collective mission. Here we all are one. We have come to do something. And that is the causal factor. And what will be the effect? The effect will be that the world will realize that humanity is one and indivisible, and no power in heaven or on earth can destroy this glorious humanity. We have come here to save humanity, and we will save humanity." - BABA

# PART - II

## MY FIRST DARSHAN WITH BABA

### 6. INITIATION - BABA'S SPECIAL BLESSING

Its all began with me in 1966 when I was studying to become a doctor (Allopathic, Western medicine) at Gandhi Medical College, in Hyderabad, Andhra Pradesh, India. One fateful day an orange-clad swami came to my medical college to deliver a lecture on the subject 'Concentration of Mind.' I felt that sanyasiis (renunciate monks) were misguiding the simple people. There was no need to become a sanyasii or a monk to realize GOD. Anyhow, I attended the Swami's lecture, but I did not get initiated (that is, I didn't learn meditation from him). I felt that if I were to learn and practice meditation, I might also become a monk (sanyasi). I thought it was better to serve the suffering humanity as a doctor rather than as a monk.

All my friends learned meditation from this Swamiji; Dada was what they called him. Still I hesitated to learn meditation. I felt that sitting down with one's eyes closed would do little more than to make one lazy. I warned my friends about my feelings regarding meditation, but Dada on the other hand was visiting every week to inspire them to meditate regularly. They were confused between the guidance of Dada and my advice. Hence they all took me to Dada to convince me about the benefits of meditation. I agreed to attend weekly meetings but I firmly decided not to learn meditation at any cost. I still had the unusual fear that if I learned meditation the Mantra might influence or compel me to become a monk. So I was scared to learn meditation.

I remember I had one question in my mind about strange diseases that we were facing in the hospitals. We believed that these unusual diseases were caused by black magicians (Avidya Tantrics), and they were extremely difficult to cure in a conventional hospital. I wanted to know from this Dada, Acarya Asiimananda Avadhuta, how these black magicians (Avidya Tantrics) could create such dangerous diseases and what was the remedy for such diseases. Dada replied that black magicians used their mental power against weak-minded persons. This was called Avidya Tantra, which he explained to me as a materially-oriented mental science that takes one further away from the Lord. He said that he teaches only Vidya Tantra, spiritually oriented intuitional science, which helps one in moving towards the Lord; he also told me that no Avidya Tantric could influence the practitioner of Vidya Tantra because Vidya Tantra is far more powerful. I felt it was better to learn meditation just to protect myself from the influence of Avidya Tantra (destructive forces).

I wanted to protect my-self from the influence of Avidya, so I took Diksha with the firm determination not to become a monk. "What if after practicing meditation for a while I feel compelled to become a sanyasi (monk)?" I asked Dada fearfully. Dada smiled and assured me that he would not allow me to become a monk even though I wanted to. It was then that I finally decided to learn meditation. And so I sat in front of him and he taught me

meditation. After initiation into science of meditation (Ananda Marga Sadhana), I started to practice.

He made it clear that my Guru was Shrii Shrii Anandamurtii (BABA) and he (Dada) was only the media of the Guru. I offered everything to my Guru mentally. After this, I felt so light, just in my very first sitting! For the first time I started to feel that presence for which I had longed for all my life – the presence of BRAHMA (Infinite Consciousness). Just on that very first day I started to realize that I am Brahma. This was the grace of BABA.

I have never discontinued meditating since that day. I was very serious and sincere in my sadhana (meditation), sitting and practicing twice a day regularly. I asked all my friends to also be regular in their spiritual practices. Earlier I was stopping them, but now I was advising them to do sadhana regularly. I started to contact many people, especially students, and I would bring all of them to Dada Asiimananda for initiation into meditation. I became active in spreading Ananda Marga meditation to many students in Hyderabad as well as in the various other cities of Andhra Pradesh (a state in the South of India). Just see how BABA changes us according to HIS will!

> "Knowing oneself is the real knowledge; *serving* all with the ideation of Narayana, the real action; and the vow to please *Parama Purusa*, the real devotion." - BABA

---

# 7. BABA IN HYDERABAD

In 1966, BABA Anandamurti, my Guru (Master), came to Hyderabad, Andhra Pradesh, in South India. We were hundreds of students all clad in our volunteers' uniforms. The entire Hyderabad airport was filled with our volunteers, waiting eagerly for our Guru's arrival. At last BABA arrived. We did pranam (salutations) to HIM and HE responded to all of us and smiled.

The moment I saw BABA coming out of the plane, I instantly felt that BABA was Shiva, that BABA was Krishna, the One whom I was searching for all my life. It is impossible to describe what I realized. It's beyond words. Even when I was a mere child of four years, even then I used to sit by myself and meditate on my Lord. I worshiped Lord Shiva and Lord Krishna. Nobody taught me the mode of worship; I simply worshiped. I was told that I was a quiet boy, never crying, all the time happy by myself. I read many stories of Lord Shiva, of Lord Krishna and the Mahabharata, of the Ramayana, the Gita, the Upanishads and many other spiritual books, still just as a young, growing boy. What I really wished for was to have been born at the time of Lord Shiva or Lord Krishna. Needless to say, when I saw BABA for the first time, receiving HIM at that airport, I was elated beyond imagination. I was seeing my Lord Shiva, my Lord Krishna in the form of my BABA. It only took one look of HIM to convince me of this.

BABA had come to Hyderabad in year 1966 to give Dharma Maha Cakra (DMC, a special spiritual gathering). Mr. Ramchandra Reddy, the State Chief-in-Engineer (retired) was the key person to arrange this Dharma Maha Cakra for BABA. Acarya Asiimananda Avadhuta, my same Dada, had initiated him. He was a very influential person in Andhra Pradesh. He arranged BABA's program in Reddy's Women's College, in Narayanguda, Hyderabad. BABA stayed in Mr. Reddy's house, which was opposite the women's college. He was also the hostel warden of both the boys and girls hostels. He was an ardent

devotee of BABA, and was instrumental in getting Ananda Marga established in Hyderabad. There he established an Ananda Marga School, a jagriti, a yoga center, destitute children's home and a medical dispensary. All of them are still running very well.

The DMC was a three-day program in Hyderabad. Margiis (practitioners of Ananda Marga meditation) from Maharashtra, Andhra, Karnataka, and Tamil Nadu came to Hyderabad to have darshan of BABA during DMC. Now it was time for BABA's first General Darshan (sight of the Guru).

BABA's entry into the DMC hall created a big devotional flow in the entire atmosphere. All were in a jubilant mood, loudly proclaiming, 'Parampita BABA ki jai!' (Victory to the Supreme Father)

As BABA sat on the dais, all maintained silence. That is, all except for one particular margii who was sitting in the middle of the hall - out of nowhere he stood up and screamed loudly, "BABA! BABA!" The volunteers rushed to take him out of the hall, but before they could even reach him he had already fallen down unconscious and went into Samadhi.

In BABA's presence, bhajans (devotional songs) were sung in Hindi and in the local language, Telugu. Hearing BABA's pravacans (discourses) was a novel experience. The margii who was screaming earlier, meanwhile, in Samadhi during entire period of BABA's discourse. After BABA's pravacan (discourse), some more bhajans were sung. BABA enjoyed these very much, especially the local folk songs sung in Telugu. Afterwards BABA left the hall.

The margii, who was in Samadhi (a state of trance) got back to normalcy after several hours. We all approached him and kept asking him what had happened when BABA came and what feeling he had on seeing BABA. He told us that he had just come out of samadhi (deep Spiritual realization) i.e., union with Brahma. He said that when BABA entered the hall, he saw brilliant light and when BABA sat, the light was so bright that he then started screaming, as he could not control himself. He also said that two Great personalities whom he believed in very much in his life - Lord Buddha and Ramakrishna Paramahamsa - were also seen sitting on either side of BABA. From BABA both were receiving a beam of light and BABA was in the center as the source of all light. Then the margii saw that both Buddha and Ramakrishna were merging into BABA. BABA had become so full of light that it was as if millions of dazzling Suns were all shining together in HIM. The margii could not bear the overwhelming intensity of the light so he screamed loudly and finally went into deep samadhi – the state of union with Brahma.

On the second day, I had my personal contact with BABA. Personal contacts were something BABA would grant out to all the margiis at least once, during which they would be able to meet BABA alone and BABA would often explain many different things personally and guide in spiritual sadhana. I was waiting in queue with other students for my personal contact with BABA before finally getting my turn. Before I entered BABA's room, one Dada told me to ask BABA for Moksha (permanent liberation). I went inside BABA's room and did sastaunga pranam (prostration - lying down on one's chest with both the hands and legs stretched fully on the floor). BABA asked me to get up calling me, "My little boy." BABA was beaming with his charming, smiling face.

Suddenly BABA's mood changed and he started asking me very seriously, "Why were you thinking such and such things in the past." Actually I was thinking of those things while I was a boy. BABA pointed these things out to me and nobody, not one person in the world could have known these particular things, as I had never disclosed them to any one else. BABA said that HE would give me severe punishment for my past mistakes. I agreed for any punishment, so BABA took out HIS stick. BABA asked me to raise my hands and to keep my eyes closed. I was expecting heavy beatings from BABA and did as HE said, but BABA just touched my waist lightly with HIS stick and said, "Now you are free from all sins of the past. From this moment onwards you are to lead a new life."

BABA gave me fatherly affection. It made me immensely happy. Finally BABA gave me some very personal instructions

and told me to keep them secret. Then I remembered how the Dada had told me to ask for Moksha earlier, so I asked BABA about Moksha and how I could get it. BABA just smiled and said, "Don't worry about Moksha. I am here to give it to you whenever you want. It is in your pocket." BABA is the granter of Moksha; HE can liberate anybody at anytime, for HE is Taraka Brahma (Liberating Brahma). This I realized from my personal contact.

On the final day of Dharma Maha Cakra, a marriage ceremony as per the Ánanda Marga system was conducted, but BABA was not present. The couple was from Bombay. Once the marriage had been completed, only then did BABA come out and sit on the dais. BABA's Personal Assistant mentioned to BABA that a marriage was conducted as per the Ananda Marga system and that the couple was eagerly waiting for HIS blessings.

"Were the oaths taken in their mother tongue?" BABA asked.

"No," said BABA's Personal Assistant. "The couple is Maharashtrian – the oaths were taken in Hindi."

"Then the marriage cannot be recognized." BABA had the whole marriage re-conducted in front of HIM; this time the oaths were taken in their mother tongue, Marathi. After the marriage ceremony, BABA smiled and gave the newly wed couple HIS blessings.

After thirty years, I happened to meet the same couple again in Bombay (Mumbai). They have grown up children now. I mentioned to them how their marriage had to be conducted twice on that day in 1966. Then the wife told me the secret that was lying behind that thirty-year old incident. Apparently she had a strong desire to have her marriage conducted in front of BABA. The first time the marriage was conducted, this was not so, and she was weeping internally. She did not feel like the marriage ceremony was complete without BABA in front of her. BABA listened to her inner voice and orchestrated that drama just to fulfill her inner desire and got the marriage re-conducted in front of HIM on some pretext. BABA listens to and fulfills the desire of each and everyone all the time and responds accordingly.

On the final day of Dharma Maha Cakra BABA gave Varabhaya Mudra (special blessings) to all of us. HE repeated a short Sanskrit mantra and then blessed us all with the special mudra; a vibrant flow of spiritual energy was generated from BABA like a massive wave that literally overwhelmed and everyone could experience. One and all felt immense joy, euphoric ecstasy. The explanation of the feeling generated by HIM is really something beyond words to express.

Following HIS mudra, we all did Guru puja (offering one's everything to the Guru) in front of BABA. Then BABA gave HIS special blessings by reciting the following words in Sanskrit and in English:-

"Let everybody be happy. Let everybody be free from all diseases. Let everybody see only the bright side of everything. Let nobody have to suffer under pressure of circumstances."

Then HE asked us to utilize our time and energy to serve the suffering humanity.

The next morning, we all went to the airport to see BABA off. It was so unbearable to bid HIM a touching farewell. We had experienced such blissful, beautiful days with BABA and now HE was leaving us. I wept as HIS plane took off, but BABA's thought remained in my mind deep rooted and this proved me that actually HE did not leave me at all mentally may be only physically. I became more regular in my meditation, and now that I had had BABA's darshan, now that I had gotten personal contact, I found my feeling in meditation to have undergone dramatic change. It became more intense, deep and blissful.

> "*Parama Puru\d{0,1}sa* (the Supreme Consciousness) has blessed you with hands to work and legs to move; has infused you with the stamina to act; has endowed you with practical intelligence, so make the best use of them in the fight against the demons. You must not sit idle relying on fate. Be vigorously active." - BABA

## 8. BABA IN BOMBAY

Medical studies need lot of time, but I spent far more time in meditation and spreading the ideas of yoga amongst other students, along with my Acarya Asiimananda Avadhuta. Even so, I was still amongst the top ten students in the University, regularly receiving the Andhra Public Service Commission Merit Scholarship. When I started practicing meditation my friends thought that I took my studies too lightly and that the Ananda Marga yogic life had spoiled me. Anyhow, an especially tough exam was looming ahead on the horizon, one that took most students at least six months to a year to prepare for it. All students studied and had midnight oil but my routine was different; I slept at 10 PM, woke up at 4 AM, meditated for two hours, and only then would go for studies.

Although none of my fellow students could understand it, learning medical science was very clear and very easy for me, and it was not because of my small, limited intellect, but because of the intense meditation I had been performing. I felt as if I knew the entire subject already, and was very confident. In anatomy, in dissection of different parts of body, we had to remember the relation of all the countless arteries, veins, nerves, muscles, and their names and their positions as well. I used to enjoy thinking about how BABA arranged all these parts in our body so perfectly, how carefully HE had fixed everything. I could understand the secrets of HIS arrangements – why should I be wasting all my time reading and studying when I could be meditating instead? And so with little effort I could understand the difficult medical sciences like anatomy, physiology, microbiology, pathology etc. While all my friends slaved away their hours over textbooks, I was outside playing volleyball, cricket and table tennis. While my friends memorized terms into the wee-hours of the night, I would enjoy my sleep, and as they were resuming their studies in the day, I was representing Andhra state in the All India Junior National Tournament in volleyball. All of these extracurricular activities may have taken up much of my time, but on top of all that were Yoga, meditation and pracar (propagating spiritual philosophy and meditation). They were the most important things in my life.

In 1968, just as that tough medical exam was peeping around the corner, news arrived that BABA was coming to Bombay. Only one month was left to study for the exam. I had the choice then either to proceed to Bombay to have BABA'S darshan, or to stay in Andhra and study. This wasn't very much of a decision at all; I immediately decided to go to Bombay (Mumbai) and have BABA'S darshan with the other devotees.

Bombay was an overnight journey from Hyderabad and we had no train reservations. When the train arrived at the platform, it was nearly impossible even to get in any of the coaches even the platform was so swarmed with people. I remembered BABA and pushed myself into the compartment through the crowd and then entered the next compartment, finding it to be empty (it was reserved for the next station). I occupied the berths and then called all my friends. We traveled happily to Bombay and slept soundly all the night. It was for BABA'S grace only. It may have been just a small incident, but for me it was a big realization because I realized how BABA was with us all the time, helping us when we needed it.

In Mumbai there were a good number of devotees. A few local margiis had arranged the DMC in a very suitable place. The food was arranged for free by the devotees of Bombay.

DMC was held in Mumbai for three days. Each day both in the mornings and evenings BABA used to go for field walks to various interesting places. Only those devotees who were chosen for field contact were allowed to accompany HIM. I was lucky enough to get the chance to walk with BABA.

I was in BABA'S car as we drove to the beach for field walk, BABA in the back seat and me in the front. BABA narrated to me about different subjects during the car ride; Later on, as we were walking quietly along the beach, I got the urge to ask BABA

about Lord Venkateswara; I wanted to know if he was God or not. Many people go to see the idol of Lord Venkateswara in Tirupati in Andhra Pradesh, South India. Lord Venkateswara was the richest God in India. His idol was on the top of seven hills, standing in Varabhaya mudra. There was a belief that Lord Vishnu who lived in Vaekuntha had become an idol to give boons whenever one asked anything from Him. People from all over India offered properties, money, gold, and tonsured their heads and offered their hair to Lord Venkateshwara in the hope of him bestowing his blessings. I was also worshiping Lord Venkateswara. I believed him to be God. Now I met BABA who was the living God. So naturally, I wanted to know from BABA whether Lord Venkateswara was God or not. I was doing sadhana (meditation) and I was also worshiping Lord Venkateswara as well, and I felt as though they perhaps conflicted. I felt that if BABA told me that Lord Venkateswara wasn't God, then I would stop worshiping him and I would only do the sadhana of BABA.

When BABA was quiet for some time, I asked BABA if Lord Venkateswara was God or not. "I will explain later," HE replied. We continued walking silently, and then BABA started speaking about other matters. I wondered whether BABA would answer my question about Lord Venkateswara, and I did not know when I would get an opportunity to talk to BABA again on this matter.

We came back to the car. Just as before, BABA sat in the back seat and I sat in the front seat. Then BABA started speaking about Lord Venkateswara. BABA said, "You mean the Lord Venkateswara where people tonsure their heads and offer their hair?"

"Yes, BABA!" I said. Actually a few months before I met BABA at Hyderabad, I visited Tirupati along with my relatives and tonsured my head and offered my hair to Lord Venkateswara. Only later did I understand BABA's indications.

BABA said, "You see, nowadays people buy God with money. The Ananda Marga God is for every-one, irrespective of anything rich or poor. BABA did not hurt my sentiment about Lord Venkateswara. Whatever BABA said I would have accepted, but the manner in which BABA psychologically handled the delicate matter was really amazing. Later on as I continued doing sadhana more and more intensively, I gave up the idol worship of Lord Venkateswara, especially after reading one of BABA's books, which mentioned that idol worship was hypocrisy when you limit the Infinite Consciousness to a limited form or figure. Then finally I gave up all idol worships and started performing only the sadhana of Ananda Marga.

Following DMC, BABA remained in Mumbai, but we all had to return to Hyderabad. On the return journey we had no reservations again, just as before. I just took again the name of BABA and entered the full packed compartments with confidence. Just as last time, I found a way through the compartment into an empty one (reserved for next station) and occupied berths to sleep. Then I called all my friends. We all reached Hyderabad, sleeping soundly as we did before. It was again BABA's grace. Both departure and return we had very comfortable journey, even in spite of planning our travel program at the last moment.

> "The one *formless*, beginning less and infinite *Parama Brahma* (Supreme Consciousness) is the only Entity to be attained by living beings - That alone is *Jagat* Guru (the Supreme Preceptor), That alone has revealed Brahma Vidya' (intuitional science) to us through the medium of the name and the form of Anandamurti (*Embodiment* of *Bliss*). *Unit Beings* must be made to appreciate its Majesty." - BABA

## 9. BABA'S GRACE IN MEDICAL EXAM

When I reached Hyderabad my friends who were seriously preparing for the examination thought for sure that this time Ananda Marga really had spoiled my career. How could I possibly prepare for this examination when I only had three weeks, while they had been preparing for the past one year. Anyhow, with only these three weeks left I decided to concentrate fully on my studies.

There were ten enormously voluminous books I had to read and memorize. I decided I would begin reading them after morning sadhana every day until 10 PM. I took the name of BABA and began reading. To my utmost surprise, I realized as I read the books that it was as if I knew the entire subject already—that I was merely turning the pages. I read all ten books in the next fifteen days like a story, remembering everything. It was a big surprise for me. With only one week left for my examination, again I read the ten books. It took me only four days. The third time it took me only two days.

Only one day was left before I had to take the exam. I did lot of sadhana and made sure I was well rested. Finally, the next day I sat for the exam. If I had spent the entirety of the past year studying as my friends did, the test could not have been any easier; I answered all the questions like a storyteller in my own way. This was like a research study for me because I could write all the answers in my own style without having to regurgitate word-for-word from the books. In this way, I finished the examination. I only had this thorough knowledge of medicine by the grace of BABA. When the results came a month later, only 35 students cleared the exam out of 200. 17.5% cleared the exam and 82.5% failed. It was a grim enough result, but by the grace of BABA, I cleared the exam with good marks.

All my friends thought that Ananda Marga had spoiled my career, but still I remained amongst the top ten students of the University. All were surprised by how I could secure such good ranks in spite of touring Bombay, not to mention all the volleyball and cricket I had been playing.

BABA is the final controller of everything. When we depend on BABA, BABA does everything that is good for us. We just have to depend on HIM and then it is easy to be carefree. The more burdens we carry on ourselves, the more difficult our lives will be and the more we depend on HIM and surrender to HIM all our big or small problems, the easier and smoother it will be. This was what I realised then, and I still have full conviction on it.

> "People have expectations from their fellow human beings. In order to fulfill those expectations, go on working without the least respite. Remember that a healthy human society, free from exploitation, hatred and malice, must be built. In this great task the blessings of *Parama Purusa* (the Supreme Consciousness) are sure to be with you." - BABA

## 10. BABA SAVES ME IN RIVER GODAVARI

In spite of all the experiences I had, even after having BABA's Darshan, even after receiving personal contact, I was still set on not becoming a monk, no matter what. I could serve humanity as a doctor – why should I become a renunciate when I could give a soothing touch to the suffering through medical services? This was how I was thinking at the time.

One day I went to a marriage party with my friends. It was held in a remote village just beside the River Godavari (other than the Ganges, this is the largest river in India). The marriage ceremony went on and on all throughout the night as we celebrated and enjoyed sumptuous dishes. The next morning we all went for a swim in the River Godavari.

The River Godavari normally flows from west to east but at this particular place in India it twists and starts flowing from north to south, thus narrowing the river, creating many a whirlpool. Many go there to swim, considering this spot of the river as sacred as the river Ganges of North India. That morning we all got in the river and decided to swim across to the other side. They all went in a group, playing and splashing water everywhere. I decided to swim slowly to avoid getting water in my face. So I was left behind.

Suddenly I found myself being dragged away by a powerful current of water that I did not anticipate. I tried to swim fast but in this process some water went into my mouth through the nose and I ended up swallowing it. A little water got inside my throat but this water didn't reach the stomach due to the dryness from all the excessive sweets I had eaten the night before. Thus, the water got stuck in my esophagus (pipe connecting the mouth and stomach), which in turn closed up my trachea (wind pipe). The lid over my windpipe at the entrance of my lungs shut; my breath stopped and air could no longer pass into my lungs.

Once breath had stopped, under this situation, instantaneously death could occur. Even experienced swimmers could die from this. The only way to avoid this situation was to drink a glass or half a glass of water before diving into the water. Drinking this water would make the esophagus (stomach pipe) wet, so that if any water entered again while swimming, the water would slip down to the stomach without any hindrance. In my case the water got lodged in the stomach pipe, which closed the air passage to my lungs thus making me breathless. I could not stay above water any longer, nor could I shout for help because air was needed from my lungs to produce any sound. My friends were swimming ahead of me and I was far behind. I was alone and helpless.

The intense current of the river Godavari dragged me down towards the whirlpools. Many people and animals like cows and buffaloes had lost their lives in those whirlpools over the years, and now I was heading towards that same fate. I thought that the last moments of my life had surely come. I thought, "Let me remember BABA before I die." And so I just thought of BABA, looked all around one last time and then plunged myself into the current of the river; I could not manage above water, especially when my breathing had ceased completely. I did not request BABA to save me, nor did I pray to BABA for help. I simply remembered HIM. Sure enough, I was sucked into the whirlpool and was pulled down.

Deep down in the depths of the currents, I suddenly felt that some special divine force had entered into my body and was carrying me against the current of mighty Godavari River with a tremendous force to some unknown destination. It was truly a miracle of BABA that I survived the ordeal. I was breathless for over fifteen minutes and still, after all that time I was able to find a place where my feet could touch the river ground. Fifteen minutes of breathlessness, and now I could feel the cool touch of sand at the soles of my feet. I stood on the sand, which was

on the other side of river, and stood in the neck deep water. It was only now for the first time since I had gone under that my face was above the water once again. I tried to breathe, and by the Divine grace of BABA, I could breathe normally again. It was only BABA'S grace that no water had entered my lungs. I thought that I was dying - that my life had come to an END – and now I was standing on the other side of the Godavari River, breathing again as if nothing had happened.

I felt, rather I realised that BABA had saved my life. Standing there in the neck-deep river water, I decided to dedicate my life to BABA's Mission. My life belonged to HIM because it was HE who saved me. If BABA wanted it to be so, then I would even become a monk. It was all up to BABA. I was thinking all these things standing neck-deep in water. Still, I could not walk to shore. as I had not even the strength to walk, exhausted as I was from having not breathed for so long. Thankfully, my friends who had reached the other side of the river were looking for me, as I was the last swimmer. When they didn't find me they thought that I had drowned in the whirlpools of the river and might have died. They were all very sad and even in tears.

After some time they saw me down the river on their side. They came running to me and carried me back to shore. After resting for a while we swam back to our village's side. They were escorting me all along the river while swimming back across. I never discussed with any of them my recent decision to work as a monk. I merely remained silent. They were inquiring many things but I did not disclose anything. This was how BABA extended my life and granted me a choice to serve the humanity. I, the person who neither intended to learn the process of meditation nor inclined to become a monk was a changed person now. My thoughts radically changed and I decided to serve HIS Mission. Man proposes and GOD disposes.

"You are never alone or helpless. The force that guides the stars guides you too." BABA

# PART - III

## MY LIFE FOR BABA

### 11. BABA IN MANDYA, KARNATAKA

After BABA extended my life by saving me from drowning in the river, I was eager to meet HIM again. I was only waiting for the great opportunity. It was in 1969. I came to know that DMC (spiritual congregation where BABA would give special blessing) would be held in Mandya, Karnataka, in a southern state of India. Immediately, I decided to attend Mandya DMC. I made my trip all alone to meet BABA there.

BABA's program was only for three days, and because of that I could hardly get a chance to speak to HIM. In Karnataka the devotees were seeing BABA for the first time and I had already seen HIM in Hyderabad and Bombay, so my chances of being able to speak personally with HIM were very bleak.

One evening I was guarding BABA's room as a volunteer. One Dada came out from BABA's room and asked about me before going back inside. After some time the same Dada came back out again. I thought BABA might decide to call me inside or perhaps might have given this Dada some instructions for me

as to what I should do with my life. HE didn't tell this Dada either of these things, but the Dada did let me know that BABA was appreciating me - I was not allowed in. This didn't matter very much to me anyway; I only wanted to know if BABA wanted me as a sanyasi (monk) or not.

The three-day DMC was coming to conclude. I enjoyed the morning and evening Darshans and Discourses of BABA, but my question still remained unanswered. Now it was the final day and BABA was leaving Mandya. Margiis were standing in front of BABA's quarters with garlands in their hands, waiting to see HIM off. I was there too. There was only one thought in my mind: why would BABA not answer my question? Why didn't HE say anything to me?

At last BABA came out from HIS room. HE did namaskar (salutations) to one and all then started towards the car that would take HIM to the airport. Along the path HE walked margiis stood in two rows - I was standing in the front of the row, thought in my mind to ask BABA about my becoming a monk. Slowly BABA moved closer to me, the margiis garlanding HIM one after another, HIS whole face was just smothered in these garlands. Then BABA came very close to me, but rather than stopping and talking to me, HE looked the other way and continued towards the car. HE moved further leaving me behind. Now I could not communicate with HIM anymore. HE started entering HIS car. My mind became utterly restless. What would I do now? I thought and spoke to BABA in my mind, "Oh, BABA! YOU did not tell me whether I should become a monk or not, YOU didn't respond. YOU didn't even look at me and now what should I do. YOU are leaving me unanswered."

Suddenly, BABA stopped at the door of the car, turned back and walked directly to me, stood in my front. BABA removed all the garlands and placed them in my hands. My heart leaped. Would HE answer my question now? Would HE speak to me a word? BABA just smiled; HE walked back to HIS car and finally the car left for the airport.

When BABA left, the margiis came to me for the flowers from HIS garlands. Even after all this, still I was disappointed - BABA might have given me HIS garlands, but still didn't give me HIS instructions. But I engaged myself in distributing the flowers to the margiis. None could understand the mental agony I was going through. Reluctantly, I decided to return to Hyderabad, Andhra to continue studying medicine.

> "It is action that makes a person great. Be great by your sadhana, by your service, by your sacrifice." - BABA

## 12.1 DECIDE TO BECOME A MONK - BABA'S GRACE

I returned to Hyderabad to study medicine again. Every morning I used to sit for two to three hours in sadhana, and because of this I would invariably arrive at the hospital around 8 AM – about one or two hours late. Thankfully, the professors didn't object as I had thorough knowledge of the medical subjects. It was only by the grace of BABA that medical studies were not a problem for me.

I thought that I might have to lead a family life, as BABA never mentioned anything to me about becoming a monk. Anyway, I thought that since BABA never mentioned anything to me, that maybe HE wanted me to lead a family rather than a renunciate one.

In this way relations with my family were strained, and now it was clear to me that they were not prepared to accept my Ananda Marga lifestyle, neither the meditation, the vegetarian food habits nor the Ananda Marga. The family people knew that the astrologers had predicted that I would become a monk so they wanted me not to continue with sadhana.

I gave them the impression that I was not doing sadhana, but actually I was doing even more intensive meditation and was enjoying BABA'S grace and bliss. For me, the path to GOD and HIS mission to serve one and all was much more important than the mundane things, so I never felt the least bit guilty in hiding my practices from them. I changed my decision to continue studying medicine and finally I decided to become a monk; a social doctor to establish BABA'S ideology in order to free the human society from sufferings and exploitations. I preferred to be a social doctor rather than a medical doctor because to be a social doctor would serve a greater cause.

Finally I decided to become a monk. It was all BABA'S blessings, I strongly felt.

> "Those who can dedicate their all to the thought of the great and the inspiration of the Supreme are verily the most heroic. Indeed, they are the virtuous, and they alone are capable of taking human history from darkness to light." - BABA

### 13. MEETING BABA IN PATNA

One day in late 1969, a Dada came from Ranchi to Hyderabad to conduct a spiritual seminar for the youths and students. We all gathered at a farmhouse outside Hyderabad to attend. The farm was at the footsteps of a hill. At fall of night we would walk to the top of the hill and shared with Dada the spiritual atmosphere and enjoyed BABA'S stories. At one point, Dada told us that just before he left Ranchi he had met BABA and had mentioned to HIM that he was going to Hyderabad to conduct special classes for the youths there.

"Ah," BABA told him. "There is one boy in Hyderabad waiting to speak with ME. You should send him to ME right away." Dada simply mentioned this to us but didn't pay much attention to it, telling more stories about BABA. Dada did not know what BABA meant. Even my friends didn't guess what BABA meant. But my inner self could understand fully what BABA had meant.

Early the next morning I decided to take a train to Ranchi along with the Dada to see BABA. The only problem was that that very same day I was supposed to receive money as my Merit Scholarship, good enough for a year's expenses, so I asked Dada to wait just one more day so that I could collect the money and accompany him to Ranchi.

"Money is not the most important thing in life," he replied. "Money comes and money goes but the Ideology and the goal of life, to attain Brahma (Infinity) are more important than anything else." Hearing this, I decided not to wait and went with Dada that day to Ranchi to have BABA's darshan. We took the train to Ranchi as planned; but by that I missed out a huge amount of money — money that could have been utilized for better purposes.

After a few days of traveling we reached Ranchi, but BABA had already left Ranchi for Patna, the capital of Bihar (a state in Eastern India) for New Year's DMC. There was still some time, so in the meantime Dada took me to a Relief Camp. That Camp was for a week and then we proceeded to Patna to see BABA. This was a special DMC of New Years day in 1970.

Devotees from all over India and from some parts of the world assembled to have HIS Darshan and Blessings. More than one hundred thousand (one lakh) margiis attended the DMC program. It was held in a big ground in Patna. A huge tent had been installed for the program. It goes without saying that in such circumstances it was extremely difficult for me to see BABA personally. I was like a drop in the sea of devotees. Still I sent a message through BABA'S PA (Personal Assistant) for a favour to grant me chance to see HIM in person.

And then BABA came out to give darshan. Dadas (monks) and Didis (nuns), all clad in saffron sat in the front close to the dais and all the margiis and myself sat behind them. Sisters were on one side and brothers on the other. It was truly a wonderful sight.

During the discourse, BABA asked a Brahamcharii Dada to stand up. BABA asked him to look at the crowd behind and tell HIM what he saw. Dada looked behind and said, "BABA, I am looking at the multi-colored poles holding the tent, and also the faces of many many devotees." BABA asked him to do Dhyana in a standing position. Dhyana is to think and meditate on BABA in Varabhaya Mudra. BABA gave this special mudra at the end of every DMC. It is a mudra of Divine blessings, giving boons and making one fearless to march on the spiritual path.) Dada did this dhyana, meditating on this Varabhaya mudra of BABA in standing position. BABA asked the Dada to open his eyes and look at the crowd again. When Dada opened his eyes, he said, "BABA! I am seeing only BABA sitting in Varabhaya mudra everywhere!" BABA then did a special mudra, interlocking HIS index fingers; Dada fell unconscious into samadhi (a spiritual trance), becoming one with the Supreme, the Pure Consciousness. Dada remained in that blissful state for hours. Meanwhile, BABA continued HIS discourse as if nothing unusual

had happened and afterwards BABA blessed us with HIS Varabhayamudra.

After the DMC program BABA left for HIS quarters in Patna, but I had a strong desire that HE would call me soon. Sure enough, I was right – the final call came from HIM, calling me late at night to meet HIM at the residence. As I entered BABA's room, BABA called me to come near to HIM. I was overwhelmed to have HIS close proximity.

During the period, I only had one question in my mind. Earlier in BABA's discourse, BABA advised the youth either to become Whole-Timer (sanyasiis, monks or nuns) or choose the life of a householder. It was easier for me to choose to become a monk. After all, my mind had already been changed in that regard. But the question was that if I were to become a monk, then I should remain a monk all my life - if I couldn't remain a monk all my life, then it would be better to not become one in the first place. So I wanted an assurance from BABA that if I were to become a monk, then I would ever remain a monk. If I were to choose that path, then not even my dead corporal body would return home. Was it possible for me? This was the question in my mind.

BABA knew everything. There was no need to ask HIM any question. BABA ordained me to undergo whole-timer training and gave me the strength all my life when HE said, "The little boy has the capacity to face all challenges." BABA blessed me by reciting special Mantras. I felt so happy that finally BABA helped me indirectly to decide - now I was put on the track of monkshood, to serve the suffering humanity. The next morning I proceeded to Varanasi (North India) for whole timer training to become a monk. I joined the training center at Varanasi.

> "Fight for your ideology. Be one with your ideology Live for your ideology. Die for your ideology" - BABA

## 14. JUMPING OUT OF RUNNING TRAIN - BABA SAVES

Family ties are strong in India. It was not easy to break the family relationship. When I decided to become a monk, none of my friends or relatives supported my decision. I was alone in my determination,-and they were doing all they could to bring me back. With this in mind, a team of relatives and friends came to Varanasi to take me back from the training center. To make things even worse, by support of a sitting member of the Indian Parliament, so not only did friends and relatives came, but some of the politicians of Varanasi were mobilized against me at the training center to take me back.

A huge crowd of several hundreds of people assembled to take me out of the Varanasi training center. The trainer Dada was afraid to keep me there. "Leave the training center and come back later, once things had cooled down," he told me. "If I keep you here, that mob might ransack the building."

It was agonizing to go back, but I had no other choice. As I did Sastaunga Pranam to BABA's Pratikriti (photo) before leaving, however, I knew that my decision had not changed – I would become a monk no matter what it comes. Perhaps they could compel me to temporarily leave, but in the end, I would not allow them to stand in my way. I was determined to become a monk at any cost, nothing could stand in my way, I was sure.

As they brought me to the train, however, I felt depressed and silently cried while pretending to be normal. It was too much. Pretending proved to be too difficult, so I went to the toilet just to be alone with my grief. They even guarded the bathroom door. I wept there in the train bathroom, while silently appealing to BABA. "Oh, BABA!" I thought. "How could this happen? How can I leave you? Please grant me strength and composure."

Suddenly, BABA appeared in my mind. "No use weeping" HE consoled me. "Use your mind and tactfully get out of this

situation." Hearing BABA's voice, I felt strong again and happy. I had to plan an escape. I was sure that the plan was coming from BABA.

With this strong inspiration in my heart, I left the bathroom much unlike I had entered it. I went and sat with my captors. And you know what? I managed to apply a trick on them.

I said, "You all did a good thing by coming to Varanasi. Now I am far away from the magic power of that training center. Now I am normal. It is a good thing that you came and saved my life. I was having a bright career as a medical doctor, and I was going to throw that all away. I am returning to Hyderabad to continue my as a student." Hearing these words, all felt reassured. We even ate food together. They trusted my words. It was only until four hours had passed, when we edged near Allahabad that I decided that the time had come – the time for my escape.

The train stopped at Allahabad station and I did not move. The green signal was given for the train to move. Now it picked up the speed. "I have to go to the toilet," I told them. "I'll be back in a few minutes." I had earned their trust, and they sent no escort to go with me. Seeing my chance, I ran to the door just near the toilet and looked outside. The train had already sped up to a good speed. It was going to be dangerous. Still I took the name of BABA and jumped out of the running train.

The train was moving fast and steep on a high slope, and I flew through the air until slowly landing on the ground and rolling down to bottom of the slope. I landed on the earth safely without hurting myself and started to run. It was all the grace of BABA that I was safe and unhurt.

After I picked myself I walked up the slope to the meter gauge station of Allahabad and boarded a train back to Varanasi with the help of the Railway guard, even in spite of having no money. I reached the training center at midnight. The gate was closed so I had to scale the high wall and jump inside in the dead of night. Nobody knew of my arrival, and the next morning when everybody woke up to start the day; the trainees were utterly surprised to find me there with them.

"How could you come back?" They asked me.

"BABA saved me," I told them, and then went on to narrate the entire story.

Only a few days after yet another team of those people arrived at the training center – and this time they were even more bent upon taking me back home. "I can't control this huge crowd!" The trainer Dada said again. "Please go back with them."

"Dadaji," I asked him, an idea suddenly struck my mind "Please give me the address of another place I can undergo my training. Those people won't be able to find me in another place." Dada gave me the address of the Delhi training center.

"Dadaji," I continued. "Could you give me some money perhaps I could escape once again before reaching Hyderabad?"

But the luck didn't favour this time. “I'm sorry, brother," he told me. "I can't give you any money." But it was all right. It was okay. BABA had given me another chance to continue my training in another place.
