Keeping in mind a feeling of joy, I did Guru puja and sastaung pranam in front of BABA's Pratikriti (photo) and for the second time I left training center.

Just as I was leaving, the trainer Dada called me back. "Wait," he asked. "Here, take this." And he gave me twenty-five Rupees (about half a dollar). Perhaps not a fortune, but I was still happy that he could help me – may be it would be enough.

This time I traveled with the captors for a whole day, going all the way to Nagpur the next day with them. Enough time had passed – they assumed I was really with them this time. After leaving Nagpur, the train stopped in some small, unknown station. Then, just as the train started to move, I ran for the door and jumped off the train before they could even realize what I was doing.

This time around the train was moving slower, so I was able to stay on my feet and run right off – a train was just entering the station in the opposite direction. I managed to get into it just in time. They stood at the door of the train, looking for me. But they were too late; I had already escaped.

After all the struggle and clash, it was this occasion that finally broke all my relations with worldly family. Only by the grace of BABA could I do it. I had to make a strong determination, a willful sacrifice to break all the ties to my previous ties, to leave behind my medical prospects. My determination was becoming further firmer to become a monk of Ananda Marga.

The train took me back to Nagpur. There I got off and looked to take a train to Delhi, to get to the training center Dada had suggested to me. I didn't have enough money, but despite this, I boarded the train with no ticket. At midnight, I arrived at Bina station. Thinking that perhaps I was close enough to Delhi to buy a ticket with the twenty-five rupees Dada had given me, I got off the train.

Just then a ticket collector came up to me and stopped me and asked to check my ticket.

“I'm going to buy it just now," I told him.

"No, I saw you getting off the train just now," he said. He threatened me to book me to have traveled without a ticket. I was afraid of being sent back to Hyderabad, as the Government of Andhra was looking for me. A fear crept in my mind whether they would send me to Hyderabad.

"I only have twenty-five rupees," I told him. "That's all I have."

"Give me ten," he said. "And I'll permit you to get on the Delhi train." Breathing a sigh of relief, I agreed and once again set off for Delhi.

I traveled all night and reached Agra early in the morning, thinking by this time that I was safe, but just then another ticket collector approached me.

"Ticket, please," he said.

Very innocently, I said, "I already gave another ticket collector ten rupees – he assured me that that would be enough to get me to Delhi."

Some of the other passengers overheard what I said and laughed. The ticket collector frowned in embarrassment at me blaming his colleague for being a bribe collector. "You have to pay a penalty for traveling without a ticket," he told me.

I searched through my pockets for money I had left. "I have fifteen rupees," I said.

"That is not enough," he replied. The only thing I had was the Swiss watch I was wearing, so he told me to go and sell the watch so I could pay for the ticket as well as the penalty fee. “If you don't," he said, “I'll book a case against you." Once again fearing arrest (and more than that, being sent back to Hyderabad), I sold the watch on the train and paid the ticket collector.

Finally I reached Delhi. In Delhi, I could easily find D 41, South Extension, Part I. Office – the trainer Dada there had already been informed of my arrival there. Finally, I was back again in the flow of my newly chosen monk life. My new trainer Dada was an editor of a newspaper; he also was in-charge of a printing press. Almost my entire training period I spent in his office doing sadhana and reading BABA's books.

> "Thorns on the path cannot deter those who have assembled with the determination to march in unison. They are blowing the trumpets of victory in an effort to conquer the universe of a timid mind. O human beings, march ahead - let the song of march be your only slogan." - BABA

### 15. BABA VISITS DELHI

I was overjoyed to hear the surprising news that BABA had already arrived in Delhi, but was unhappy to hear that I was not allowed to see HIM while under training. I was told that BABA only liked to see and talk to a trainee until after training was already completed. Even the Varanasi trainer Dada came to Delhi and instructed me not to go to see BABA. This was very painful news for me. I asked the Delhi Editor Dada to convey a message to BABA that I was eager to meet HIM. Later on this Dada went in front of BABA and before he could even mention my name, BABA asked him about me HIMSELF. Dada then conveyed my message to HIM, about my inner wish to see HIM. BABA was happy to hear about me and immediately gave HIS special permission to come and see HIM even though I was still under training. How fortunate I was to get love, attention and care from BABA, which I really think I hardly deserved. Now none could stop me from having BABA's darshan.

BABA stayed in Delhi in HIS own quarters. Everyday in the mornings and evenings devotees would come there to see HIM. There was only a small room to accommodate everybody, but somehow people managed to squeeze themselves in there, another miracle of BABA. I went to see BABA for HIS morning Darshan and sat just in front of HIM. There were other devotees as well but BABA was mostly speaking to me personally.

HIS Darshan was a training class for me. BABA said, "There are two paths - one path looks from the outside to be a very happy path. One feels that one can enjoy in that path so one follows that path. But in reality one gets into more complications and problems by following that path as it leads to sorrow, pain and suffering. There is another path, which from the outside looks like the most difficult one to follow because it is full of struggles; but when one follows this path it secures happiness and peace. The first one is the path of worldly pleasures, the materialistic path and the second one is the path of spirituality, the path to the Supreme Consciousness." BABA concluded by saying that one should follow the path of spirituality in spite of the obstacles one has to face. This is the path of Bliss, the path of eternal happiness, the path of Anandam - Ananda Marga.

The next day BABA gave another class. BABA said, "There was one train moving from Howrah (Calcutta) to Peshawar (Pakistan). A passenger took the train and arrived at Peshawar after traveling for many days. People say such a person reach at Peshawar, but actually it was not by his own efforts that he was ever able to reach; it was only because the train took that he was able to reach his final destination at Peshawar. No one speaks about the train. Similarly, when one embraces the right ideology, the ideology makes one Great. People say that such a person is great but they never speak about the ideology that made him Great; that brought him to that final destination, the realization of the Supreme."

BABA continued, "If one takes a train going in another direction or even a nearby destination, then one cannot reach Peshawar in that way. One should choose the right train and get onto it. Even if one just eats and sleeps all the time on the train, or argues with others while on the train, still the train will surely take him to Peshawar. Similarly, when one takes up the right ideology then one will surely attain the Supreme Goal (Brahma)."

BABA meant that when one takes up Ananda Marga, the path of Brahma, then this path would make one great - others would call such a person great, but wouldn't speak about what made him or her great. No one gives any credit to the Paths or the Masters who shape the disciples and guide him to the goal, just as no one remembers the train carrying a man to Peshawar. This is the inner meaning of BABA's Pravacan given that day.

Another day in Delhi, BABA performed many demonstrations. I was sitting close to HIM in the front row. BABA called me to sit even closer to HIM. BABA called one Dada and asked him to sit facing me. BABA asked the Dada to see my hand, and then with HIS stick BABA changed the vibration of my hand into a book. BABA asked the Dada to see my hand again. Dada

said that he was seeing a book. I was still only seeing my hand but this Dada was seeing a book, and I wondered how this was possible. BABA then said just as I thought this, “If I let it remain in this state for a while then all would see the book.” Then BABA changed the book vibration into a pen, then into a lotus flower, then into a swan (bird), then into a sweet (a Rasagolla) and into many other objects. Then BABA said, “One should never run after these worldly objects or its vibrations. One should only run after the creator of these vibrations, i.e. Parama Purusa. HE is the most loving Entity, most beloved. HE is always with every one. HIS love and affection is with everyone. HE is the loving father of all. One should fully depend on HIM only, and then life becomes enjoying and smooth.”

The next day there was another demonstration. BABA called me to sit close to HIM. BABA called one Dada and asked him to sit in front of me and meditate. Then BABA took out HIS stick and started making large circular motions with it around my body. BABA asked Dada to open his eyes and look at me. Dada said that he was seeing me as a point; BABA had compressed all my vibrations into a single point. BABA smiled and said, “Such a big boy has now become such a small point.” This demonstration was to show us to withdraw all our different expressions and vibrations and get it all focused down to one point. Such a pointed mind can easily merge into the Supreme Consciousness. Point is called Bindu - we practice bindu sadhana. For ten days BABA continued giving different fascinating discourses, often supplemented with demonstrations.

When BABA was about to leave Delhi, both my Varanasi and Delhi trainer Dadas told me that they were planning on asking BABA if I could be allowed to stay in Delhi at the office for another one or two years for training. I wondered what I would do there at the office. I could serve people better if I got trained. So they approached BABA for extending my stay in Delhi. BABA got angry and told them to complete my training within seventy-two hours. In addition to this, BABA also gave a special SPT (special physical training) training system just for me. I had to go around Delhi doing pracar (propagating Ananda Marga), wearing only one cloth like a dhoti folded all around the body. I also had to keep the office clean. At the Benaras (Varanasi) training center, the usual SPT system was to collect ones own food as alms and cook for oneself in the open in a pot, eating only once a day. Along with that the SPT trainees had to maintain strict silence for all seven days. But in my case BABA relaxed all the rules.

I was considered a sensitive caseworker because the Government of Andhra and the Government of India were still trying to locate my whereabouts. Here was the conflict between Universal ideology and the family sentiment. The Governments did not appreciate universal ideology for which I had dedicated my life.

I finished my training in seventy-two hours. Then I went to Varanasi for the final exam, cleared it, and then went on from there to Jamalpur for Acarya (meditation teacher) training for a week under Dada Dasarathji. BABA often used to reveal to different people their past lives, but Dada Dasarathji wanted to see BABA’s past lives. So BABA had Dada meditate and then HE showed Dada HIS past lives – Dada told me that he saw Lord KRISHNA first and then was taken back further and saw Lord SHIVA. Dada said that Lord Krishna was very, very handsome, having fair-complexioned skin and a thick beard. He also said that Lord Shiva was very tall and muscular, with thick mats of hair. He said that Lord Shiva wore tiger skin and unlike Lord Krishna, did not have a beard as He was from the Himalayas. It was a great a privilege to be trained by such a devotee of BABA.

My acarya training was completed and I was posted to work in Ludhiana (Punjab), India as a Principal of Ananda Marga School and Superintendent of Children Home.

> "Those who have the correct philosophy and a correct spiritual sa'dhana' based on the principles of Yama and Niyama (Ten cardinal moral principles) will be the guiding personalities of the society tomorrow.” - BABA

## 16. BABA - FIRST ANANDA MARGII IN THE UNIVERSE

Until late 1970 I was working in Ludhiana (North India) as the principal of the Ludhiana Ananda Marga School. I was also the Diocese Secretary of Ludhiana Diocese. This was my first posting as a worker of Ananda Marga. I mobilised all the Ludhiana margiis to raise funds for our children's home there. Within a month the financial position of my school and home had become very healthy. I started doing pracar in the nearby cities, blocks and villages. Margiis were spirited and we worked as a team, rendering services and doing pracar (spreading advanced spiritual ideas).

Whenever BABA toured other parts of India, HE used to pass through Delhi. Once I proceeded to Delhi to have HIS Darshan. I went to HIS quarters in Delhi and told HIS Personal Assistant (PA) to convey that I had not seen HIM since becoming an Acarya so I would like to see HIM. At that time I was a Brahmacarii. So PA Dada went inside BABA's room, asked permission, which HE gracefully granted.

It was evening time and BABA was sitting on a chair in HIS room. I did sastaunga (prostration) pranam to BABA and then I sat near HIS feet and began to massage them. HIS feet were so soft, just like flowers. There was a special fragrance of sandalwood as well. BABA spoke to me, "Oh, now Acarya Kamal Kumar has come." BABA said this to me because I was seeing BABA for the first time since becoming an Acarya.

BABA continued, "You have become an Acarya but you know that I am not even an Acarya." BABA smiled.

I said to BABA, "Oh! No BABA, YOU are far above Acaryas. YOU are the creator of Acaryas. YOU are everything."

Then BABA smiled and said, "You know, I am just an Ananda Margii - but I am the first Ananda Margii in the entire Universe." When BABA said this, I felt that Ananda Marga's Universal Ideology was working all over the universe on various planets and galaxies. I also felt that BABA is our father – not just my father, but also everyone's father. All human beings of this earth and even the human beings of other planets far away in distant space are all HIS children. BABA is the Supreme Father and the entire cosmos is HIS planning. All are HIS children, so HE is guiding one and all to come closer to HIM. One has to follow HIM, as HE is the only one to be followed.

> "No power in heaven or on earth can separate me from my children. Even if this universe comes to an end, I will be with them in the expressionless cosmos." - BABA

## 17. BABA IN MEERUT, (UP) INDIA

According to the Ananda Marga organizational setup, India is divided into seven regions. A region is further divided into dioceses and diocese into districts, a district into blocks and then a block into Panchayats or villages. A few months into working as a Dada in Ludhiana diocese (Punjab), I came to know that BABA had a program to come to Meerut, U.P., in North India to conduct a Dharma Maha Cakra. Meerut was not my diocese, nor was it in my region.

For smooth functioning, certain organisational rules and regulations were designed for the workers. One of these rules was that the workers of one region should not enter another region without prior permission of the organisation; Thus, I was not allowed to attend the Meerut DMC. I was encouraging the local margiis to go to Meerut and have BABA's darshan, but the margiis were insisting that I should also accompany them to Meerut because we were all working like a team, doing pracar, relief work, fund raising etc. So I planned to attend Meerut DMC along with the margiis to see BABA as a margii but not as an Acarya or as a worker.

Along with the margiis from Ludhiana I took a night train and reached Meerut early in the morning. We took bath, performed sadhana and proceeded to the site (pandal) where DMC would be held. Senior Dadas were also there. They were surprised to see me in Meerut, as I belonged to another region. One senior Dada objected to my presence and told me to return to Ludhiana before BABA's arrival.

I was hurt. I was a new worker, unaware of the rules and regulations of the organization. I did not know how to face the situation. "Ok," I told the Dada. "I will go back, but let me see BABA at least once, as I came all the way from Ludhiana to see HIM." He agreed after much persuasion, but told me to see BABA from a distance and not to go close. I agreed. When BABA arrived, there was so much enthusiasm all over. I didn't listen to what the Dada had said and decided to see BABA from a close distance. So when BABA came out of HIS car I was standing almost directly in front of HIM. BABA did long namaskar to all, looked at me, smiled, and went inside HIS quarters.

After a few minutes the senior Dada who had objected to my presence in Meerut, came out of BABA's quarters and started searching for me. He was calling me by my name. Dada said, "Oh! It's good that you are here. BABA wants to see you, come inside quickly." I was overjoyed to know that BABA was so kind to call me.

I went inside HIS room and did sastaunga pranam. HE was happy to see me. Then BABA asked the Central workers, "Why did only one worker come from the other regions? DMC is such an important programme. Why didn't the workers from the other regions had come?" BABA then started asking the workers how many workers had come from other regions. None had come uninvited except me.

One Central worker in order to save himself replied, "Yes, BABA! But at least this one worker came." BABA knew that I was asked to leave Meerut by this same Central Dada. BABA created this whole drama just for me.

The Central Dada who had asked me to come inside was charged for having failed to ask all the workers from the other regions and so BABA ordered him to handover his Central posting to me. Now he would work as the Diocese Secretary of Ludhiana, Punjab and I would become the Central worker. It was an inter change of posting.

Suddenly BABA's Personal Assistant told respectfully. "Only an Avadhuta (who practices special tantrik sadhana) is eligible for a Central posting, BABA," as if BABA didn't already know.

"Then make him an Avadhuta immediately," BABA replied.

"There's no time during DMC for Avadhuta Initiation!" He requested.

This bothered me, but not in the sense that he was objecting to me, but that he was objecting to BABA. I could feel that PA Dada, even after being so close to BABA was still not a good devotee. Rather he was just bringing up such objections. How could he do that? Later on, in only another year, it would all become very clear to me.

BABA paid little attention to PA Dada and merely said, "Then send him to Ranchi to get initiation there." Ranchi was where our Central office was functioning. Now I could see that BABA made all this drama just so that I could stay with HIM, now in Meerut and then in Ranchi where BABA was spending most of HIS time. Hearing this, I was able to happily enjoy HIS Darshans for all three days during Meerut DMC.

Before leaving Meerut, BABA called for me once again. All the Central workers were there. BABA said to me, "On account of certain formalities, the new posting order as a Central worker is not getting materialised. You just remain as the Diocese Secretary of Ludhiana for the time being. Remember one thing that your spiritual development has nothing to do with your posting." If some one has a very high posting, it does not mean that one is spiritually developed. One might even remain in a low posting and be very high spiritually. This was what BABA meant and taught us as a lesson to remember in the future. It turned out later to be a much more important lesson than I previously had realized.

"Those who are devotees are workers. They will never be afraid of work. They will do maximum work." - BABA

## 18. BABA SUMMONS ME TO RANCHI

In December 1970 BABA summoned me from Ludhiana, Punjab for Avadhuta Diiksa (initiation) at Ranchi (Central Office). As a junior worker, I was wondering what was more important in my life - organisational work or sadhana (meditation). This was a big question in my mind. With this question, I went to see BABA in Ranchi.

BABA called me to HIS room, so I got to spend time with HIM alone. Of course BABA already knew everything that was going in my mind, so I did not have to ask HIM anything. BABA said, "Your first and the foremost duty is to do sadhana. If you still have time then do your duties, your organisational work, and if you still have time then bathe, and if you still have time left then you can eat some food." BABA's reply cleared my doubts about what should hold importance. BABA gave General Darshan in the Ranchi jagriti right after speaking to me. In General Darshan also BABA was emphasising these same points - first sadhana, second duties, then bath and then food. BABA continued, "One should not reverse the order and say 'Oh! Now it is time for food so let me do a few minutes of sadhana and then take food.' One should give sufficient time for sadhana."

Everyday I was enjoying BABA's mornings and evenings general Darshans in HIS small room in Ranchi's jagriti. BABA wanted to see us all too, just as we wanted to see HIM. HE used to say, "Come close, come close - make space for all!" It was a wonder how so many devotees used to get accommodated in such a small room for HIS Darshans. There was a big hall outside BABA's room as well. Sometimes BABA used to give Darshan in the big hall on Sundays and on certain other important occasions when there were bigger gatherings.

Very close to the Ranchi jagriti there was a separate Central Office. BABA used to visit that office as well. Then nearby the Central Office was BABA's quarters. Every morning BABA would come to the Central Office to review reports (BABA would often

review reports from workers worldwide concerning the progress of the different spiritual and social service programs we were running), and then proceed to the jagriti (spiritual center) to give Darshan to the workers and margiis, then would return to HIS Quarters.

Later in the day in the evening the same routine was followed. In the evening after reviewing the workers' reports, BABA would sit in the garden of the Central Office. There we would dance and sing kiirtan (BABA NAM KEVALAM - BABA's name – the name of the dearest one - is everything). Kiirtan had newly introduced. BABA created BABA NAM KEVALAM as a Siddha Mantra (a few words in which tremendous spiritual power was surcharged by the Sadguru – the perfect Guru). One can get liberation by singing BABA NAM KEVALAM. This was the first time that a Siddha Mantra was permitted for reciting aloud by Taraka Brahma - Liberating Brahma. At the time of Lord Shiva and Lord Krishna humanity was not yet prepared for the open recital of a Siddha Mantra. The present society had evolved in the intellectual sphere and is heading towards collective spiritual growth. Hence BABA gave BABA NAM KEVALAM, a siddha mantra, for one and all.

So on that day we were doing Kiirtan around BABA as HE sat in a chair in the garden. There was one Dada, a Central worker, beating the drums. We were all in ecstasy, singing and dancing. However, there were a few workers who did not accept Kiirtan wholeheartedly. Their outlook was more intellectual than devotional.

BABA gave an indication of the incidents that were about to occur in the near future. When I first heard it I did not understand it quite clearly, but later on everything became clear, as did the incident with BABA's PA Dada. As the Central Dada was beating at the drums BABA said, "You see the weight bars? The twenty kilo heavy weight is at the bottom, then on top of that is a ten kilo weight, on top of that a five kilo weight then one kilo, then 500 grams and so and on so and on until finally at the top comes just 10 grams. This Central Dada is like the weight on the top, so small, so weightless."

BABA told me that development in spirituality had nothing to do with the posting one had. Someone could have a very high posting but might be a big zero in spirituality, just like a 10 mg weight. BABA made everything clear with that statement HE made, but I could not understand the significance and inner meaning of it at the time.

There was a big lake behind the Ranchi Jagriti, only about one kilometer away. I was walking alone with all the frustration I had my decision to leave my physical body. I reached the pond, thinking I would end my life this way, drowning myself. I thought, "Let me do the last sadhana of my life." I sat and began my meditation.

Meanwhile, BABA came to the Central Office and started asking about me. BABA wanted to see me right away, and demanded that the Central Dadas should produce me. HE expressed HIS displeasure over the delay. So the Central Dadas sent volunteers and messengers out, searching for me at the railway station, at the airport, at the bus station and even in the homes of some local margiis.

I performed long sadhana for about an hour or so, and then I started doing Guru Puja. This would be the last Guru Puja of my life. I thought. After finishing Guru Puja, I did Sashtaunga Pranam and got up. Suddenly BABA appeared and said, "Why did you decide to end your life, you are such a brave boy, you have the capacity to establish MY Mission single handed."

"Yes, BABA!" I promptly replied. I did Sastaunga Pranam again. When I got up, BABA disappeared. The voice and message were very distinct. BABA'S words were ringing in my ears. Then I thought, "Why should I leave this physical body?" I decided to materialise BABA's mission all alone. I thought, "Why should I depend on anybody?" I alone would materialise BABA's Mission. With this firm determination, I got up and returned to the Central Office, Ranchi.

The Dadas were happy to see me. They were curious to know 'where I had been all the while.' "We were being repeatedly

asked to find you out. HE loves you so much" they told me. I remained silent. They did not know what was going on in my mind - The only aim in my life is to establish HIS Mission, the Dharma on this earth.

"Despite it's advent onto this earth many thousands years ago, humanity is not yet capable of building a well-integrated and universal human society. This is in no way indicative of the glory of human intellect and erudition. You, who have understood the predicament, realized *the* urgency, seen the naked dance of evil and heard the hypocritical and raucous laughter of the divisive forces, should throw yourself into this noble task without further delay. When the ends are just and noble, success is inevitable."- BABA

## 19. BABA INITIATES ME INTO TANTRA SADHANA

I was taught Tantra Sadhana by BABA at Ranchi. It was a great experience to learn this special Tantra Sadhana directly from HIM. It was a mystical experience. Words cannot express it. "BABA is SHIVA," I felt.

Lord Shiva was the first to teach Tantra Sadhana, 7000 years ago. Lord Sadashiva taught Tantra Sadhana to fourteen persons; amongst them were HIS son, Bhaarava and HIS daughter, Bhaeravii. Lord KRISHNA, 3500 years later taught Tantra Sadhana to fifteen persons, amongst them were the five Pandavas.

BABA ANANDAMURTI initiated several thousands into Tantra Sadhana. The entire humanity will gradually be brought into a spiritual flow. The future of humanity is great and bright with the advent of BABA ANANDAMURTI on this planet.

The first time I practiced Tantra Sadhana in the midnight all alone was very very powerful. I was ordained Avadhuta the next night (Sanyasii or Monk). It was the beginning of a new life for me. Now I was more equipped and eager than ever to spread BABA's Universal Ideology all over the planet.

The next morning I was walking with BABA on HIS daily field walk, now clad all in orange with a new name and new posting as well. I would be working as the Diocese Secretary of Srinagar, Jammu Kashmir State, which is in the north of India in the Himalayas. BABA instructed me like a Father taking loving care to HIS child, "You should always wear well-pressed clothes." I was negligent towards clothes, thinking that it was not so important. But as a social worker it was very important to wear clean and well-pressed clothes.

Walking there with BABA, I did not know at the time that very soon BABA's Mission would take me far outside of India's. BABA was telling me all about the spreading of Ananda Marga

in Manila, Philippines, North America, U.S.A., etc. I was thinking, "Oh, what do I have to do with other countries? I have to think about working in Jammu Kashmir."

BABA was talking about the seminars being conducted in America. Many new margiis were being initiated every day, and so on and so forth. I was listening to BABA, but not so attentively, as my mind was occupied on Kashmir. I did not understand that the real reason BABA was sending me to Kashmir was to get adjusted to the cold climate so that I could work in cold countries without any health problems in the future. That was my last field walk with BABA for a long time. In fact, I would not see HIM for years.

All the workers, both Dadas and Didis, were sitting on the lawn in front of BABA's garden in Ranchi. BABA came out of HIS quarters and sat in front of us in a chair. HE surprised us that day, saying, "You all must decide now when you want the entire planet to be brought under Ananda Marga, under the care of Sadvipras (spiritual moralists)." It wasn't what we expected. BABA gave us one day for all of us to think, discuss, decide and give HIM the final date by which Ananda Marga would be established on this planet. "You set the date," HE said, "And I will change the world by that time." It was something new for me, as I was more interested in realising HIM. About changing the world, I thought it would happen when HE really wanted it to happen. Of course, we have to make sincere efforts and BABA would Grace upon this planet to make it a Sadvipra Samaja.

The next day again we had a general meeting with all the workers and BABA. BABA asked us about our final decision. So BABA asked us the time needed for establishing Sadvipra Samaj. The Departmental Secretaries collectively told BABA that within the span of three years, Ananda Marga should get established all over the globe. BABA then said, "Ok, that's good. It will be done, but on the condition that you raise (10,000) ten thousand workers." This was BABA's direction to those Departmental secretaries - to engage themselves in creating more workers.

BABA made an interesting point in the general meeting. BABA said, "No group or organisation will support you because all are working for limited, finite objects and goals. You are all working for Infinity, and that contradicts what others are working for. Your ideology, your goal is to attain Infinity – the Infinite goal, Brahma. Even the social ideas like Universalism, the cosmic brotherhood and PROUT (The Progressive Utilization Theory, BABA's socio-economic theory) are all based on Infinity. How can others, whose goal is for limited objects, how can they support you? Both your directions and movements are opposite, different. So don't depend on anybody. Don't expect any support from any corner. You should move ahead, taking the help only from those who agree with your Ideology. Remember two things in your life: be regular in practicing all your lessons (i.e., our different sadhana lessons) four times a day and organise yourselves so that you are self-sufficient economically and financially. Follow these two things. Victory is yours!"

"Marching ahead is Jiivan *Dharma* (the characteristic wonts of life). To march ahead carrying all, viewing the whole humanity as an integral entity, is the principal aspect of Jiivan Dharma. *Resolve* courageously to observe this *Jiivan Dharma*. Remember:

'Yato *dharmah tato is't'ah*, yato is't'ah *tato jayah*.' Where there is Dharma, there is *Is't'a* (the Goal) and where there is Is't'a there is Victory." - BABA

## 20. BABA INDICATED OF MY ARREST IN SRINAGAR

I left Ranchi and proceeded to Srinagar to start work at my new posting. I could not attend the 1971 New Year's DMC, as my position was very delicate. There was a search for me. Even in 1971, after all this time, the Government of Andhra was still looking for me. The Government of India was also cooperating in their efforts to take me away from Ananda Marga. Thus, I had to miss the annual DMC and instead went straight to Srinagar from Ranchi (Bihar). The journey took a few days. First I reached Jammu and met the margiis there, who were all happy to see me now as a new Dada, a new Avadhuta, and the next day I proceeded for Srinagar. It was a whole day journey.

Srinagar city with its beautiful Dal Lake is in the lap of Himalayas, about 5000 feet above sea level. It is a gorgeous valley, considered by many to be a paradise on earth.

I heard that Jesus Christ was once there in Kashmir. I saw a place in Kashmir where Jesus did his sadhana (meditation) - Lord Shiva's system of sadhana - here in the Kashmir valley around 2000 years ago. He realised that he and Lord Shiva were so close. I was told that Jesus considered Lord Shiva as his Father. Jesus did not mention Lord Shiva's name because then the Romans who kept Palestinians and Jews as slaves would understand that Jesus was in India, in hiding - that he came from India. Indians refer to Lord Shiva as "BABA," which means Father. Once I mentioned this point to a priest in Europe. He was surprised. Anyway, this was the information I gathered here in Srinagar.

I started doing pracar in Srinagar and initiated many including a medical student. I explained the philosophy of Ananda Marga to him for three days. He was impressed by BABA's ideas and wanted to see HIM right away, so I had him attend the Annual DMC (New Year) held in Ranchi. He was inspired after seeing BABA. He had only been a margii for a few days, he went on his own to the whole-timer training center in Varanasi to become a sanyasi (Monk). Once the medical student's father - a high ranking officer of the Jammu and Kashmir Government - came to know that his son had gone for training to become a monk in Varanasi, he contacted the local police in Srinagar and got my teacher Baburam Manhas and myself put behind bars.

We were both arrested and kept in Srinagar jail under what was called the Preventive Detention Act. Under this Act anybody could be arrested without any reason or charge and could be kept in jail for two years. The Government's plan was to close the Ananda Marga School we had there. Our school was in Karan Nagar, with one hundred students on roll. The school was a prestigious one for its high standard of teaching. I used to get English books all the way from England. Out of the one thousand applications we used to get, only fifty would be selected.

The Government of Kashmir was jealous of our popularity, so they manipulated to close down the school. After our arrest the same day a new Dada came to replace me. What happened was that I had already been transferred to a new posting out of India, as the Sectorial Secretary (SS) of Sydney Sector (which includes Australia and other South Pacific countries). But by the time I was arrested, I was already on my way out - to Ranchi to see BABA before going abroad - and this new Dada was already on his way in.

BABA took the decision to transfer me a week earlier and sent another Dada to Srinagar to replace me. BABA knew that I would be arrested and took these steps to protect our school. And it worked - the Dada who arrived following my arrest took over the charge of the school the very next day. The government's scheme had failed; the school children were not affected by my arrest. BABA's liila - HIS Cosmic Play - is really wonderful!

The guardian who got me arrested then went to the Varanasi training center and brought his son back to Srinagar. The father and son contacted the media, the press and all the newspapers and gave a statement full of lies. He told them all that that he was kidnapped in a Military vehicle and forcibly brought from

Srinagar to Varanasi. The medical student had a bus and train journey reservation from Srinagar to Varanasi as proof that he had traveled by public transport, but of course this was paid a deaf ear. In Varanasi all we had was an Ashram Centre where Spiritual and Social teachings, Bhajans (Songs) and Kiirtans (BABA NAM KEVALAM) were taught.

> "The very import of the history of human welfare is the history of struggle and strife. Even the sweet gospels of peace could not be preached in an environment of peace and composure. Devils did not allow the apostles of peace to work peacefully - that's why I say that peace is the outcome of fight.
>
> This Endeavour at the wellbeing of the human race concerns everyone - it is yours, mine and ours. We may afford to ignore our rights, but we must not forget our responsibilities. Forgetting the responsibility implies the humiliation of the human race.
>
> In order to march ahead on the road of human welfare, we all have to strengthen ourselves in all the arena of life. The complete seeds of welfare in all the spheres - physical, mental, moral, social and spiritual - are embedded in sixteen points (the main social and spiritual practices of Ananda Marga). Hence be firm on sixteen points." - BABA

## 21. BABA'S SPECIAL BLESSINGS IN JAIL

There was no case against Baburam Manhas, the school teacher or me. None of that mattered – things like legality and justice carried very little weight in India in those days; we were arrested under the arbitrary Preventive Detention Act and were not charged on any ground.

We had the special grace of BABA. We would sit for several hours in deep meditation everyday. We were spending most of our time - sixteen hours a day - in meditation. On fasting day we were meditating twenty hours a day, sleeping for only two to three hours. I understood why BABA once told me personally that the first and foremost duty for me was to do sadhana; in jail there was no other work to do but sadhana!

Once, a Dada came to see me. I told him about my health condition and about fasting. He went back to BABA and told HIM of my situation.

In jail I started to initiate the prisoners into Ananda Marga meditation. We were two in the beginning and after only a short while we were over one hundred margiis. The report went to the government that they had arrested two Ananda Margiis but now they had become one hundred. They were not happy, but we in the jail were; we celebrated all the major Ananda Marga festivals together. On the occasion of BABA'S ANANDA PURNIMA, BABA's birthday, we conducted twelve hours of Akhanda Kiirtan (Singing BABA NAM KEVALAM around a circle). We served good food to all one thousand prisoners in the jail that day. All were happy.

> "Be firm on sixteen points. Unite all the righteous forces. All the tall talks of the evil forces will be silenced." - BABA

# PART - IV

## BABA'S JAIL DRAMA

### 22. MID-NIGHT COURT HELD FOR BABA

Some of the anti-social elements in connivance with the police got BABA arrested in false and fabricated case. It was also a miracle that the District Judge ordered to hold a special court at mid-night on June 27ᵗʰ, 1971. BABA was released right away by granting bail at the mid-night on the spot. This was big news, which was well published in all the news dailies of India. I was thrilled to go through this news while in jail custody in Kashmir. Never such thing happened anywhere in the world so far.

Great is HIS Cosmic Game!

> "History bears testimony that whenever a person states the absolute truth in any sphere of life, whether it is spiritual, social, economic, or otherwise; sought clarification of doubts or protested against injustice and wrongs, the evil forces forthwith plotted against the person, administered poison, slandered and assaulted that person with rage, misused authority and mercilessly dealt blow after blow, but the blows boomeranged and ultimately the evil forces were annihilated by those very blows, Remember, by an unalterable decree of history, the evil forces are destined to meet their doomsday." - BABA

---

### 23. BABA IN JAIL A MIRACLE OF HIS FASTING

Workers of Ananda Marga are dedicated workers who volunteered themselves for the upliftment of humanity. Some of them who were not sincere in spiritual practices (Sadhana) and were not committed to this Mission couldn't continue to carry on the work of the organization. So they left on their own. From amongst them, some even tried to harm the organization in different ways. The police and the intelligence department of the Indian government took advantage of the situation and tried their best to completely finish social and spiritual activities of the holy organization. But the wishes of Almighty were different.

Though they harass workers, many of the workers were put behind the bar; BABA was also jailed on false and concocted cases. The government with the help of jail doctor even poisoned HIM. But nothing could harm and check the progress of the noble Mission, which has been created by the Supreme Father BABA HIMSELF.

BABA had to remain in jail custody for nearly seven years. During that period HE did not take any food (except a glass of curd water) for a period of five years four months and two days. HE fasted in protest against HIS demands to set up a high power enquiry to enquire in the matters of HIS poisoning case.

All oppositions started because of BABA'S PROUT ideas.

PROUT is an acronym for the Progressive Utilisation Theory – PRO U T. This PROUT is a socio-economic conception totally different from Capitalism, Communism or Socialism. PROUT stands for the decentralisation of the economy to all people and to all places like rural side development. Now there is a centralisation of capital in few hands and few places (cities). In PROUT, the people will own all wealth, all property; people will become very rich. The entire society has to be reorganised in the form of co-operatives with mutual love and respect for

one and all. In this way both managers and labourers will both become shareholders. The ownership of wealth lies only with the Supreme Father. We are all HIS children. We have every right to utilize the wealth of this universe but no right to accumulate or own it. Thus, there will be a collective body that fixes both the minimum and maximum limits for physical wealth. This collective body is a group of Universal minded socio-spiritual dynamic cadre dedicated for the betterment of all – humans, plants and animals included.

All people of this earth will be guaranteed the minimum necessities of life like food, clothes, housing, medical care, education and transport facilities by being provided the proper purchasing capacity. All will be given scope to develop physically, mentally, spiritually, culturally and all will be given scope to develop in arts, music etc. by reducing the working hours of employment. The entire globe will be reorganised to form a Global Parliament. All languages of the world will be given scope to develop. There will be a Lingua Franca, one common language to connect all. There will be One Human Society. This, in short, is PROUT. PROUT will inaugurate a new golden era on this earth. All moralists of the world should unite to establish this PROUT.

Because of HIS all-pervading philosophy on Socio-Economic aspects of life the local political circle and some of the governments like Russia and others started feeling that this progressive new theory would be widely accepted. So they became active and opposed in whatever way they could.

> 'Today across the entire world, a grim fight has started between the evil forces and the benevolent forces. Only those who possess the moral courage to fight against the *evil* forces can give a soothing touch to the struggle-torn earth with the balm of peace. Remember you are spiritual aspirants, hence *you* alone have to undertake the mighty task of saving the earth.'
>
> \- BABA

## 24. BABA GUIDES ME ON EVERY STEP

In 1972, just after the government jailed BABA, some of workers who had left the organisation contacted my worldly relatives and disclosed my whereabouts, that is, in a jail in Jammu. I was in Srinagar but I was shifted from there because they weren't happy with the one hundred margiis I had created. In Jammu, I was kept with very few prisoners in a palace like place where only top politicians were usually detained.

They could easily trace my whereabouts so my laokik relatives, ex-workers even the Jammu Kashmir government officials all came to take me out of jail. When they arrived I was told that they wanted me to come outside and meet them. I was sure that once I went outside they would forcibly remove me from jail and take me back to Hyderabad just as they had tried before in Varanasi. It's really unbelievable the things I had to go through to break ties with these people. I was facing obstacles at every step.

It was the jail superintendent who came to tell me that everybody was waiting for me outside. I told the superintendent, “You see, you are a family man. You will lose your job if I make a case against you on the charge that you forced me to see them against my wish." I had no intention of seeing these people – especially the ex-workers who had betrayed my Guru. The superintendent, fearing that he might lose his bread-winning job, got worried and went outside for me and told them all that I didn't want to see them. They refused to listen and ordered him to open up the jail gate and let them in. They told him that they had come with all the proper authority to the jail just to release me. Having already warned the superintendent, he did not open the gates and instead conveyed their message to me.

'Do not open the gate," I told him. “If you do, I'll file a case against you. It will be difficult for you to support your family without a job." On this sentiment I could use the superintendent to not to open the gates, in spite of insistence from them.

The whole day that poor superintendent was carrying messages between them and me. All the officials and others when they finally got frustrated then they sent the message: "You will remain your whole life in jail if you do not see us. We have come to take you out of here—that is something that Ananda Marga will never be able to do."

"I am prepared to remain my whole life in jail," I conveyed back to them, "rather than to meet you all, because you came with those evil people who put my BABA in jail. I will come out of jail only when Ananda Margiis take me out. I will never take the help of those traitors." Hearing this, they finally left, frustrated once again. Only BABA gave me the strength and ideas to fight with all these black forces. I felt that BABA was guiding me all the time. Whenever there is need of BABA's help, HE is there, ready to help. Great is BABA!

There was one very devoted margii who lived just nearby in Jammu. His name was Major Keshavananda. He was a very big officer in the Military, working in Akola in Maharashtra in the Military center there. He met BABA several times and often visited our Ananda Marga centers there in Akola to attend group meditation. He helped the Dada there to construct a school and children's home and also helped the Didi there in her social service projects.

The government of India in Delhi came to know that a military officer was a staunch follower of Ananda Marga, and they were not happy. To verify this fact, the Central Intelligence of Indian secret police sent their Inspector to investigate and to get all the facts concerning how far Major Keshavananda was involved with Ananda Marga. The police intelligence inspector came to the military headquarters and sent the message that he had come from Delhi to meet and enquire about his activities. Major Keshavananda invited him to see him in his office.

Once there, Major Keshavananda asked the Inspector who sent him, what his name was, who his boss was, and who was the boss of his boss. In this way he noted down all the names of four high officers in order of their ranks. Then he told the Inspector that he was under arrest as per Military Laws for violating basic Military and Police codes by coming to inspect a high-ranking military officer. "You will be court-martialed in a military court and be sent to jail," Keshavananda told him. "Furthermore, all four of your superiors will be arrested as well, for sending an inspector to enquire a high-ranking military officer."

The poor police inspector started to lament. If he was arrested and put in a military jail he would lose his job. "Please let me go!" he begged. "I have a family! I have children to take care of!"

Major Keshavananda felt pity on him. "If I release you, what will you tell the central police in Delhi?" he asked.

"That you're not an Ananda Margii!" the inspector desperately replied. "That you have no connections to Ananda Marga whatsoever!"

Major Keshavananda was not happy with this reply. He did not want to lie about being a margii. "Report to them that I am a margii," he said.

"But if I tell them you're a margii, they'll send me back to look for you again and again," the inspector pointed out. "It's better if I just close the file and tell them that you have nothing to do with Ananda Marga."

"Say what you want," Keshavananda replied.

It was this great devotee of BABA's who came to Jammu to help secure my release from jail. It was only because I had refused the help of those defectors and governments that BABA sent HIS devotee to release me. Major Keshavananda met with all the Jammu and Kashmir government officers and told them that they had arrested an innocent Sanyasi (Monk) who just did meditation in jail. He told them to release me immediately. He had such a good influence in the government that finally I got bail, a conditional release in which Major Keshavananda agreed to take responsibility for me. But I did not care. I only cared about seeing BABA; right after my release I went straight to meet BABA in the Patna jail in the beginning of 1973.

> "The night of Cimmerian darkness is bound to disappear with the advent of the dawn. That which is ever true, which is eternal, is destined to be illuminated with the rise of the Cimmerian dawn. Be ready for the grand ovation to that new crimson dawn in every house. Be prepared physically and mentally, armed with the force of righteousness for the auspicious moment of the enthronement of the ever new. Be ready to respond to that supra-cosmic trumpet." - BABA

### 25. MEETING BABA IN JAIL

I took a train from Jammu to Patna. I telephoned the Ananda Marga Central Office in Patna and informed them about my release. I also told them I was coming to Patna. I arrived there in the morning and to my surprise there was a big reception given to me at the station, with Central Dadas and other Dadas and so many garlands. BABA's new Personal Assistant (PA), Dada Ramananda, a very devoted, sincere, fully service-minded Dada was there as well. I asked him why a junior worker such as myself was getting such a reception. Dada Ramananda explained to me how he had been meeting BABA in jail every day. "When I told BABA you were coming from Jammu," he said, "HE instructed me to receive you that way, with all other workers." I felt happy that BABA had been thinking of me, I was happy from this reception, and I was happy to meet all the Dadas again after such a long time. I had been in jail for two years. I embraced each Dada one by one. Then we all went to the jagriti.

I wanted to see BABA straight away, so PA Dada Ramananda told me that I could accompany him the next morning to go see HIM in jail. So the next morning I happily went with him to the jail to have BABA'S darshan. We were only permitted twenty minutes to speak with BABA. HE was put in a very small cell. It was extremely hot summer and the cell had no fan.

We entered BABA'S cell and found HIM lying on HIS cot. We did Sastaunga Pranam to BABA. BABA first spoke to HIS PA then BABA called me near HIM. BABA told me to procure a passport and then leave India immediately. My new posting was as the Sectorial Secretary of Sydney sector, which included Australia, New Zealand, Fiji, etc. After this I did Sastaunga Pranam and left HIS room. HIS room was full of beautiful aroma of sandalwood. Even if it was a jail cell, it was still BABA'S place, and because of that it was an abode of peace filled with spiritual vibrations.

Our Central Office had been shifted to Calcutta, so I went there to apply for an Indian Passport. After a few weeks the passport official gave me the reply that they were refusing to give me a passport. They gave me two reasons. Hearing this I went to see BABA again in jail. There, BABA asked about my passport. I told HIM that the government had refused to give it to me because:

1. If I were to leave the country, I would pose a threat to the national security of India.
2. I would jeopardize the integrity of India by breaking India into pieces.

BABA just laughed. BABA told me, "Just see how afraid the government is of a sanyasi (monk). File a case against the government in the High Court of Calcutta for denying you your passport." Again I left for Calcutta and filed a case against the government of India. Why couldn't they issue me a passport? I was a citizen of India, just like everybody else. The case was thus admitted to the Calcutta High Court.

The decision to get a passport can take long time in the Indian courts – sometimes it could take several years. This posed a big problem to me because BABA was sending me messages from jail, telling me that I should leave India immediately. Seeing few options, I tried to get a private passport. There was one man in Basel, Switzerland who was issuing passports – private passports. They were called "World Service Authority" passports. They only charged twenty dollars. His principle was that all human beings were world citizens; hence this passport was only for those who agreed with his ideas. One had to sign this agreement he had written up and send twenty US Dollars. Both the Central Office Secretary Dada and I applied for this WSA passport and within a span of only two months WSA passports were received. Again I went to see BABA in jail after getting this WSA passport. By then BABA had already begun his historic fast.

On 12th of February 1973, BABA was administered poison by the jail doctor under the pretext that it was medicine. Any ordinary person would have died, but BABA lived. We call this day "Nilakantha Diwas". 7000 years ago Lord Shiva took poison and held the poison in His throat, hence Lord Shiva was called Nilakantha (Blue throated). Here the poison symbolically means all the sufferings and sins of humanity. Lord Shiva took all the sins and purified humanity. Similarly, BABA went to jail and now took poison, also all the sins and sufferings of humanity. It is interesting to note here that once BABA said, "Had I not gone takes care of HIS children. BABA took millions of people's samskaras by undergoing this suffering on HIMSELF. HE is so great. There are no words to explain HIS greatness. HIS love for humanity is immense and inexplicable. Every day he was taking away our samskaras (reactions in potential form, usually incorrectly referred to as karma) to purify us. Thus, on 1st of April 1973, BABA started fasting to protest against treatment given to HIS workers and to demand a judicial inquiry into HIS poisoning. HE took only a cup of curd water per day during HIS fasting.

Whenever anybody gave any new ideas, they had to face opposition from the then existing society. Jesus Christ was crucified, Galileo was put to death, and Socrates was given poison. Similarly BABA was given poison in jail because of HIS Progressive Universal ideas. HE wanted to unite all humans and wanted make one universal family, one Cosmic Society. He wanted that all humans should be united and all should enjoy the universal property collectively. Why should some be very wealthy while others starve to death, bit by bit? All are the children of the Supreme Father. All should work together and collectively solve all the social, economic, cultural and religious problems of the world. These ideas have hit directly against the capitalists' and communists' vested interests. All over India in 1970 slogans were written:

CAPITALISM MAKES MAN A BEGGAR;

COMMUNISM MAKES MAN A BEAST;

PROUT MAKES MAN HUMAN;
PROUT IS THE ONLY WAY OUT;
READ PROUT, KNOW PROUT etc.

Such educative slogans were written on the walls in all the cities and even in the villages of India.

For the first time Communism had been dissected and exposed by BABA as the materialistic and illogical theory. BABA showed to the world how really it was just an instrument for dictatorial authorities to befool the people with, a deceptive ideology that turned the common man into a slave of the state – nothing more than a beast that works, eats and sleeps.

The Soviet Union had very good trade relations with India at the time. No one in the 1970's even dared to attack Communism. But in India, under our guidance and inspiration, slogans were written all over India against Communism. Many seminars were being held all over India explaining how dangerous Communism was. In the process of this widespread, growing popularity of PROUT and BABA'S ideas, many Communist cadres became members of Ananda Marga. The Indian and Soviet intelligence agencies read all of BABA'S books.

Karl Marx wrote that society groups people into two classes: rich and poor, bourgeois and proletariat, haves and have-nots. Communism is meant to be a classless society. All become "equal." But BABA explained in HIS PROUT theory that there are actually four classes working in society. Thesis, Anti-Thesis and Synthesis (the method Karl Marx used to prove that there were two classes) was going on in four classes, not two. Communism came as a synthesis by giving an anti-thesis to capitalism. But communism too would face anti-thesis and this would lead to the fall of communism too. Explaining all this in the Karl Marx language of triode theory of Thesis, Anti Thesis and Synthesis, BABA proved how Communism would collapse and how Communism was an Ethiopian theory. It is no surprise that the Soviets turned against Ananda Marga. It was they, along with the Indian Intelligence, who administered BABA poison.

***

> “*Human* beings will have to draw up all sorts of plans and programmes for the collective welfare of universal humanity. This will accelerate the speed of their movement and enable them to easily remove the thorns from the path. Obstacles are inevitable on the path of movement. In fact, in all actions, auspicious and *inauspicious*, obstacles arise, but the innate force lying in each thought and every action, from the cosmic perspective, provides the necessary stamina for forward movement. Let us not forget this fact.” - BABA

## 26. BABA POISONED - GLOBAL PROTESTS

On the evening of 12th February 1973, BABA complained of serious diarrhea and dysentery. Along with BABA there were four other members of Ananda Marga also detained, but each of them were shifted to different jails away from BABA. The doctor gave BABA pills. They were poison. Even a huge elephant could have died in a few seconds, once such tablets were given. The jail doctor gave these tablets to BABA'S caretaker to be given to HIM for HIS troubles. The caretaker did not know that this "medicine" was actually poison, so he brought them to BABA and told HIM they were from the doctor for HIS troubles.

BABA knew that they were poison. Still BABA took those pills to expose the cruel forces and their criminality. After BABA took the tablets, HE went into a coma, lying unconscious in HIS cell for seventy-two hours. Soldiers were posted around BABA's cell. No one was allowed to see HIM. The evil forces might have planned to dispose BABA's body once HE collapsed from the poisoning, but of course the people who gave HIM this poison did not understand who BABA really was. After three long days BABA woke up and was able to narrate the poisoning to HIS PA Dada Ramananda.

When Dada Ramananda told the devotees of the government's attempt to kill BABA, a big stir was created all across the world. Protests were held in all the cities of India as well as in many major cities all across the globe. BABA wrote to the government demanding a judicial inquiry. Thus, on 1ˢᵗ of April 1973, BABA started HIS historic fast.

## 27. BABA'S FASTING FOR FIVE YEARS

On 5th April, 1973, a senior worker, Dada Divyananda committed self-immolation (setting oneself on fire in front of the Bihar state Assembly at 4 AM in protest to BABA'S poisoning. The same month, on 24th April, 1973, another worker, Dada Dineshvarananda committed self-immolation in front of the Old Fort in Delhi, the capital of India. On 4th August, 1973, four prominent members of Parliament came to request BABA to give up HIS fasting. BABA'S had fasted for more than four months. BABA told them that HIS Ideology and Dharma were more important than HIS life. HIS fasting continued even after their requests. Those four Members of Parliament were very prominent leaders of India. One was Mr. Atal Bihari Vajpayee, the opposition leader in Parliament at the time who went on to eventually become Prime Minister, another was Mr. Samar Guha, another prominent opposition leader. All four MP's told BABA that the Communists guided the government. These communist-minded governments wouldn't pay attention to HIS protests anyway. "MY Ideology and MY mission are more important than MY life," HE said. These four MP's were very impressed after seeing BABA. They tried their best in the Indian Parliament to raise awareness for BABA and approached Government officials as well, but they could not achieve much because of the Soviet Government influence behind the curtain. The Indian Government was silent due to Russia. All these malicious persecutions were being planned in Moscow and only being executed through Delhi in Patna.

### STATEMENT OF PARLIAMENT MEMBERS OF INDIA

Shri Atal Bihari Vajpayee, Shri Samar Guha, Sri Prasannbhai Mehta and Shri Shyam Lal Yadav, each of who were important members of Parliament, saw BABA in the Bankipore Central Jail in Patna on 16ᵗʰ of March 1974. Later they jointly issued the following statement at a Press Conference. Samar Guha on behalf of all other M.P.'s briefed the Press.

The statement said:

> "We found Shrii Anandamurtiji extremely weak and anemic, always lying and unable to sit, stand or change side on the bed without assistance. His voice has become very feeble. He could not talk distinctly; he could complete his sentences haltingly and in a very feeble voice.
>
> We requested him to give up his fast, which he declined by saying that he considers his ideals of Dharma, Morality and Humanity more important than his life. These ideals he considers to be the basic principles of democracy."

> "So your duty is threefold. Your first duty is to observe morality and do intuitional practices. Without this you cannot have mental determination. Your next duty is to unite the moralists of the world; otherwise righteousness (*Dharma*) will not endure. This will be your real duty. You *will* become great by this, for the ideation of the Great makes one Great. At the third stage, you will have to mercilessly fight against sin wherever it has habituated this world. You will have to propagate this mission from door to door." - *BABA*
