## 28. BABA's LETTER EXPLAINING INHUMAN TORTURE

To
The Governor of Bihar,
INDIA.
Through
The District Magistrate, Patna.

Sir,

With due respect and humble submission I would like to bring to your kind notice the following facts along with tales of harassment, torture, maltreatment, poisoning and other inhuman activities done by different agencies of the Government.

1. That I was arrested by the C.B.I. (Central Bureau of Investigation) on 29th December, 1971 at 6 o'clock in morning on some charges concocted by the C.B.I. with certain malafide and political motives. The 28th December 1971, being Ekadashi (Eleventh moon), was my fasting day. On the 29th morning after I was arrested by the C.B.I. they did not allow me to do my morning duties throughout the day. They did not allow me to do my spiritual practice. They did not allow me even to take a single drop of water [in order to break my fast]. They produced me before the S.D.O. (sub-division officer) of Patna in the afternoon. My remand order for jail was kept ready in the S.D.O.'s office before I was produced in the S.D.O.'s chamber (not court). This shows that the S.D.O. decided to send me to jail even before she (the S.D.O. was a lady) saw the arrested person. She did not ask me a single question and so I did not get the scope to narrate the tales of torture. I was brought to Buxar Jail (it is in another district) at about 11 o'clock in night i.e. I had to remain without food and water for about sixty hours at a stretch.

2. Due to unhygienic conditions prevailing in jail I became sick and on 25th February, 1972 I was sent to P.M.C.H. (Patna Medical College Hospital) for treatment. There the C.B.I., the state C.I.D. and the State Police kept me under inhuman tortures 24 hours a day leading up to 3rd April 1972. There I was kept without food continuously for 3 (three) days from 25 February 1972. The motive was clear and simple. They wanted to kill me as per the secret instruction of the C.B.I. In P.M.C.H. room no. 17 I was forced to remain within the four walls of the room for the entire period up to 3rd April. I was not allowed to peep through the window or to raise the curtain of the door to see the sky. They did not allow me to read the newspaper coming from my house. They did not allow me take food coming from my house and forced to take unhygienic food prepared by the men of their choice. They did not allow any of my friends or relatives to see me even from a long distance. They created disturbances even in my spiritual practice.

3. I was sent to Bankipore Central Jail in the first week of April 1972. Just a few days after coming here the authorities stopped my interviews with my relations and friends. Even now nobody can see me without a written order from the Court of Law.

4. In the first week of June 1972 my mother died. I am her first son. As per usage of India the first son is to do the spiritual observance of the parent. But the cruel and inhuman authorities of Government did not allow me to see her in her deathbed nor did they allow me to do her last rites.

5. In Bankipur Central Jail authorities stopped my food continuously for fifty hours with the only intention of killing me as per verbal instruction of the C.B.I.

6. I am aged man of above fifty but because of my regulated and routine life I used to maintain a good health, but due to continuous tortures by Government authorities in general and the Jail Department in particular, my health has been ruined. Four persons who used to look after me became the eyesore of the jail authorities. Two of them have been transferred to Dalton Ganj Jail and two to Buxar Jail, although none of them have got any court case in Dalton Ganj and Buxar; the only intention to transfer is to keep me alone and kill me conveniently.

7. In the first portion of 1973 they posted several Sipahis (soldiers) just at the gate of my cell just to prevent others from seeing me. It is clear that they wanted to kill me secretly and shift the dead body silently with the help of those Sipahis.

8. On 12 February, 1973 I felt very much unwell in the afternoon. The Jail Doctor came and prescribed certain medicine for Diarrhea and Dysentery. On the same day at 9 o'clock in the night I again felt unwell. The doctor was informed and he came at about 10:30. He said that he would be informing the Civil Surgeon, Patna. I requested him to please inform my men also and gave him my residential address and phone number. The Doctor came again at about 11 o'clock and told me that he had informed the Jailor to send the news to the address of my men. Afterwards I came to know that the jailor did not convey the news to my men and suppressed the fact for reasons best known to him.

February 1973. At that time I was undergoing the following five reactions of that poison. They were:

i) Extreme weakness throughout the body.
ii) Nervous reaction throughout the body.
iii) Profuse watering from both the eyes.
iv) Extreme pain in the brain.
v) Inertness of the brain.

On 14th morning I felt the sixth reaction, i.e. I was unable to see anything clearly even from a short distance. I became almost blind. A few days after that, a specialist came and said that I was suffering from conjunctivitis. The night of the 15th two more specialists came. At that time my blood pressure was 180/100.

The morning of the 16th my urine was examined, the sugar was + + + (3 plus). I have got no doubt I was poisoned on the night of the 12th and all these were reactions of that poison. On 19th February, 1973 a first class Magistrate was deputed by District Magistrate to record my statement. I have stated that in the case of medical treatment the faith of the patient plays a very important role, and as I have lost faith in these Government Doctors, and as a strong suspicion has been created in my mind that they have poisoned me as per the directions of the C.B.I., I don't want to be treated by any Doctor selected by the Government. I want to be treated by my private Doctor.

On the night of 21st February, 1973 I requested the jail authority verbally and on 27th February, 1973 in writing to request the District Magistrate, Patna to depute a 1st class Magistrate to record my statement demanding a judicial probe. I further told them that I want there to be in the Judicial Commission any Judge of the Supreme Court or of any other High Court excepting that of the Patna High Court (because Patna is the place of occurrence). But as ill luck would have it, I have not yet been favoured with the presence of 1st Class Magistrate to record my statement.

In view of the foregoing I would request you to please favour me by appointing a Judicial Commission to go through all the above-mentioned complaints and other relevant matters at an early date. I have got a strong suspicion that certain black hands are working against me from behind the curtain and some of them are important persons whose names I want to disclose before the Judicial Commission.

Yours faithfully,
Sd./Prabhat Ranjan Sarkar
5/3/73.

1. Copy to President of India for information and necessary action.
2. Copy to Secretary General, U.N.O. for information.

Sd. /Prabhat Ranjan Sarkar,
5/3/73

# PART - V

# BABA SENDS ME ABROAD

### 29. LEAVING INDIA - BABA'S MIRACLE

In spite of prolonged fasting of BABA, the Government of India was not paying any attention. So I was planning to leave India to expose the evil deeds of the government. In this context I met Mr. Mallikarjun who was my medical college friend. He was senior to me. He took active part in what was known as the Telangana Movement. Telangana is a part of Andhra that is economically backward. People were very much exploited there in the past and still are. As a result of his campaign for the people there, Mallikarjii became a member of the Indian Parliament. I met him and asked him if he could arrange a passport for me. He tried his level best but with no result; later on he told me that orders were passed from a very high source to persecute Ananda Marga and BABA Anandamurti. He said he could not do anything, for my passport or for BABA's release.

I also contacted many top ranking people who were also known to me in Hyderabad. I asked them to help Ananda Marga in order to bring justice to stop the persecution of BABA. They told me his hands were tied.

I tried my best but I could not get an Indian passport. I had this World Service Authority passport, but it was just a private passport issued by some person from Switzerland. By then BABA had already fasted for several months. I went to see BABA in jail. BABA asked about my Indian passport case. I told HIM that it would take a long time to get my passport. My earlier

posting was as the Sectorial Secretary of Sydney Sector, but later on BABA had changed it to the Sectorial Secretary of both Cairo and Nairobi sectors. These two sectors included all of Sub-Saharan Africa and all of the Middle East. Now I was also to choose from the list of countries.

"BABA! In which countries should I work?" BABA said HE couldn't hear anything. I repeated the same question again, but still BABA said HE couldn't hear me. Actually BABA had planned on sending me to many different countries, so HE was avoiding a reply. But at the time I had no clue, so I asked BABA again in a different way: "BABA! I would like to know about Cairo Sector."

Then BABA replied, "Oh! You want to know about Cairo Sector." BABA continued, "Lebanon, Turkey, Iran, Iraq, Kuwait and Israel are all potential countries for doing pracar." BABA further explained about each country, "Lebanon and Turkey being in close border with the west became more liberal. Iran, Iraq and Kuwait due to oil production came in contact with the west and became more liberal."

Then, lying there on HIS cot BABA called me near and putting HIS hands on my head, gave me special blessings and asked me to leave India immediately. I did Sastaunga pranam to BABA and left HIS cell.

First I went to the Turkish embassy in New Delhi, but they refused to accept my WSA passport, so next I tried with Iran, then Iraq, then Lebanon, then Kuwait and finally with Israel. No country was ready to accept my WSA passport. I tried to get a visa from some other African countries as well, but every country's embassy was refusing to recognize this World Service Authority passport. At the same time, BABA was sending messages, asking if I had left India or not. So I was getting pressure from both sides. Not knowing what to do, I thought, "BABA is asking every day if I've left India. So let me just go to Thailand and try to get visas from there."

In Calcutta, I went to the Thai consulate to procure a visa. The person sitting there was a new person, working just for that one day only. He did not know the real procedure. I sat there on the sofa in my orange dress, waiting for the queue to dwindle away until finally everybody else had left. Then he came up to me himself. "What can I do for you?" He asked me.

"I would like to visit Thailand and see the Buddhist temples there," I told him. "If you could give me a visa, I would be very grateful." He asked for my passport and the twenty-five rupee (half a dollar) fee. I showed my WSA passport and gave him the money.

"Why don't you have an Indian passport?" He asked me as he suspiciously looked over my WSA passport.

"I believe in a Universal philosophy and a Universal ideology. For me the whole world is one. Hence I keep this World Service Authority passport. I am a World Citizen."

My statement impressed him. He went inside and stamped the visa and returned my WSA passport with a Thai visa that was good for thirty days. The next day the Central Office Secretary Dada, hearing how I was successful in my bid for a Thai visa, also went to the same embassy to try and get a visa there too. Unfortunately for him, that day the regular staff was there on duty – they refused to give him a visa.

Knowing how often BABA was asking if I had left India or not, that same night I got my visa I crossed into Nepal and took a flight from Kathmandu to Bangkok. There I went all around the city making rounds in the different embassies - Middle Eastern and African - but none were willing to accept my WSA passport. I was stuck in Thailand.

> "People have great expectations from their fellow human beings. In order to fulfill those expectations, go on working without the least respite. Remember that a healthy human society, free from exploitation, hatred and malice, must be built,
>
> In this great task the blessings of *Parama Purus'a* (the Supreme Consciousness) are sure to be with you." - BABA

### 30. ARRESTED IN LAOS - BABA SAVES

The longest I could possibly stay in Bangkok (Thailand), as I had visa for ninety days (three months), and after that I would have to leave the country and re-enter with a new visa (how I would get that, I didn't know). Since no country was accepting my WSA passport, I planned to return to India. So I went to the Indian embassy to either obtain an Indian passport or get some kind of permission to return. I was refused on both counts. They told me I would have to return to India in the same way I had left. But it was difficult for me to even get back to Nepal with this passport. So now I found myself with no place to go in the world. Desperate, I went to the United Nations office to ask for a refugee passport, but even that was refused. They told me that they could not help me because I was an Indian citizen and was not a refugee by birth. My ninety-day visa limit was getting closer to its expiration, so I went to the Laotian embassy with the intention of getting another visa for Thailand once in Laos. They gave me a fifteen-day visa without even bothering to see my passport. Laos was a poor, war-torn country, so I was sure the only reason they let me in was for the money I was giving them for the visa (a mere ten dollars).

Meanwhile, while there in Bangkok I was able to initiate many brothers and sisters into meditation – especially in the university. Regular group meditation started in Bangkok. I also got some help from the local Dada there who was in charge of Bangkok Unit.

Laos is a neighboring country of Thailand, so I just took a bus from Bangkok to Vientiane, the Capital of Laos. I stayed in a Buddhist temple. The first day I got there I went straight to the Thai embassy to try and get a new tourist visa. This time I was refused. I went around to every single embassy present in Laos, but not a single country accepted my WSA passport. I went to the Indian embassy again to try and get permission to return from Laos, but I was refused on the grounds of being an Ananda Margii. Hearing this, I thought, "Well, then let me do some good work for Ananda Marga now since I was here."

I contacted a University there and delivered a lecture on Yoga and meditation. The Vice Chancellor, many professors and students all learnt meditation. Also, in one Indian restaurant I met a French brother, initiated him as well and then introduced him to all the new margiis I had created at the university. All this happened in just three days.

The third night I was there it was Amavasya day (new moon), so I had to go out to do my Night Sadhana by the riverside. It was dark so I could easily go to the riverbank. I did my sadhana peacefully, but while I came back up the riverbank military personnel's surrounded me with guns pointed at me. They were all speaking Laotian and I was speaking English. Finally they just arrested me and took me to some military prison. Once there, fellow prisoners gave me some bad news: those who did not have national passports were taken in the middle of the night were shot and thrown in the river. All those without passports were killed - only those who had a guarantee from an embassy were kept in the jail.

Having no national passport, I was sure that my last day had come. The whole night I could not sleep. I was prepared to die, if that was the wishes of the Almighty. Whatever was good, HE would do for me. I left everything in the hands of BABA and did sadhana the whole night through. The next morning the military officers went to the Buddhist temple I had been staying at to verify who I was, but the temple refused to take responsibility for me. Next the military went to the Indian embassy and asked about me. They too refused to accept responsibility for me. "If he's really an Indian citizen," they said, "He'd have an Indian passport." The military was framing false allegations against me in both the temple and the embassy that I was caught in the middle of the night sending signals outside Laos. The military considered me to be an international spy, only pretending to be a sanyasi. Laos was engaged in war with their neighboring countries Cambodia and Vietnam, so they suspected everybody. As they came back to the jail, the military officer told me that neither the temple nor the embassy would take responsibility for me. This meant one option only – to get ready for getting shot dead.

The French brother whom I had initiated apparently went looking for me at the Buddhist temple and there they told him that I was not a monk but an international spy. He didn't believe any of that though and was able to get my whereabout in jail from them. In this way he was able to come and see me in jail. I told him to contact the Vice Chancellor who had learnt meditation from me. He went and contacted as was asked, but the Vice Chancellor told him that he was busy and would come around five o'clock after classes. I thought he wasn't going to come, that he was just afraid of the military, but to my surprise he came to see me at prison at sharp five o'clock.

When he came to the gate and stopped his car, military officers were running up and down and around to receive him, all giving him salutations. He gave some order and one person (Military) came running to fetch me. The person came to me and asked me to accompany him to the gate. I followed him to meet the Vice Chancellor. All the Military officials were standing around and he was sitting in the middle of them in a chair. The Vice Chancellor greeted me and told them that I was his Yogi friend from India. Then they released me and let me go with him.

"Why did they let me go with you?" I asked as I got into his car.

"They had to," he explained. "My father is the President of Laos." This I did not know. I thought he was just a Vice Chancellor, but apparently he was much more than that. The things BABA had to orchestrate to save me from the jaws of death! Great is HIS LIILA (cosmic game)! Whenever one takes risks for Dharma, Dharma protects. Whenever we jump to do HIS work, BABA also helps reciprocally. This was what I felt in Laos when I was saved by BABA from yet another near-death situation.

As we drove along the Vice Chancellor told me, "The country is at war. It'd be better if you not go out at night." I asked him if he could help me get a visa so I could stay longer here in Laos. "You can stay as long as you want," he said. He even wrote me up a letter. The next day I took this letter with me to Laos Immigration - they gave me a permanent resident visa. I breathed a sigh of relief. At least now I had one place on earth in which I could stay with this World Service Authority passport.

This French brother who helped me in pracar and contacted the Vice Chancellor for me while I was in jail, later took on Local Full Timer training from me and helped me start a jagriti (yoga center). I initiated many people. Soon there we had regular Dharma Cakras (weekly group meditations) with over one hundred participants. This French brother called his parents from France to visit him. They were so happy to see that their son had now lost all his bad habits of smoking and taking drugs; happy to see that now he respected his parents. His parents were so happy, in fact, that they gave me one thousand dollars ($1000) for my expenditure. In this way BABA's mission started in full swing there in Laos. Even so, I was still trying my best to leave the country.

Eventually I got my hands on a Cambodian visa, which was also at war like Laos - I'm sure that's the only reason I was able to get that visa. So I flew to Phenom Penh, the capital of Cambodia in another attempt to get a visa from one of the Middle Eastern or African countries.

The day I landed in Cambodia, the airport was bombed, so now I couldn't leave the country even if I wanted to. Communists had occupied the entire country except this capital city of Phenom Penh, and now they were only twenty kilometers from the city. Every day Communists were firing rockets into the city. So now I was stuck in this mess. I was worried that once the Communists took over Phenom Penh they would massacre all the survivors, myself included. All the Cambodians there were living in panic, feeling that their lives were now coming to an end with the enemy only twenty kilometers away. Everybody asked me why I did come to that city? "Every body was fleeing for their lives and I came there."

I went to a University to talk to the students and professors. They were all in a terribly mentally stressed state. I taught them

BABA NAM KEVALAM kiirtan by the thousands. Every day there were literally thousands of us singing BABA's mantra together in the football ground, sitting and meditating as well. Whatever I said they were just following, as they had no other hope. Even the senior most monks at the Buddhist temple I was staying at, even they were learning Ananda Marga meditation. They were depending on BABA only because BABA was their only hope.

I went to the United Nations office to seek refugee status. They refused, as I was born in India. I contacted some embassies, which were closing their offices due to war. They refused to grant me a visa with this WSA passport I had. Even the Thai embassy was refusing to give me a visa. Fortunately, the United Nations had given me one assurance: that they would take me out of Cambodia as soon as the airport runway was repaired.

Every day I was visiting the United Nations office to see if there was any possibility of flying out. One day I forgot my bag and accidentally left it there at the UN office. After walking for five minutes, I realized that I had left my bag and walked back to fetch it. I had been walking through the center of the city. One rocket full of bombs landed and exploded in that center of the city and killed many, many people on the spot had bullet injuries. Had I not forgotten my bag at the United Nations office, I would have been one of the victims of that rocket explosion. It's truly amazing the ways BABA was saving my life.

After two months of waiting for a flight, finally a United Nations aircraft came from Bangkok and landed in the city I was staying at. The airport was partially repaired by this point so the emergency aircraft was able to land. I went to the UN office as I did every other morning to ask about flights, and to my surprise they told me to come in an hour with all my things to leave Phenom Penh and land in Bangkok. I packed my things as fast as I could and came right back. In the noontime we flew off from Phenom Penh and landed in Bangkok, Thailand. Thanks to my permanent resident visa of Laos, I was able to get a seven-day transit visa there in Thailand. These seven days were the only days left for me to make effort to get out of South East Asia by getting a visa from some other country.

Meanwhile, while I had been in Cambodia the Dada who was in charge of Bangkok unit sent a message to the Central Office that I was trapped in Cambodia. The matter was reported to BABA. Then BABA sent a message for me, "LET HIM GO WHEREVER HE WANTS, LET HIM DO WHATEVER HE WANTS." Earlier I was trying only to proceed to Middle Eastern or African countries, but now with this message I had gotten permission to proceed to any country, even to stay somewhere and get political asylum if necessary. This was just what I needed.

Again I went to the United Nations office to seek refugee status. I was able to contact the highest office of the UNO there in Bangkok - they were surprised to see me traveling with a World Service Authority passport like a regular National passport holder. He said to me, "People who have this passport are put in jail, how could you be traveling with this, like a regular passport holder?" I told him that the CORD had sent me on a special mission so I could travel even without any passport - it was all the grace of the Lord (BABA Anandamurti only.)

I contacted almost every embassy there was to get a visa, any visa to any country of the world. When no country was ready to recognize this World Service Authority passport then I decided to settle in Laos, as I had a permanent visa there. I also kept my eyes open to seek political asylum in Sweden.

One sad thing happened; the day I left Cambodia, later that night the Communists attacked and took over all of Phenom Penh. They butchered many innocent people. Had I stayed even one day more, I would have been caught in their trap as well. How minutely BABA was controlling every big and small event of my life!

> "The greater the task, the mightier the obstacles. That is why the person who wants to perform noble deeds must be ready to face opposition from the very outset." - BABA

## 31. BABA SAVES ME FROM RUSSIAN-KGB

After one week of struggling and failing to get any visa from any country, finally I landed up in Laos once again. It was the only country that didn't close its doors to me. Once there, I decided to try to get to Sweden and seek political asylum there. Since BABA gave me the message that I could go or do whatever I wanted, then I felt BABA would guide me through HIS ideas and would show me the path. I started to make all the necessary arrangements to go to Stockholm, Sweden.

There was an Aeroflot flight from Vientiane, Laos to Moscow then from Moscow to Stockholm. This flight had its service only once in a week, every Saturday, reaching Moscow on Sunday in the early morning then Stockholm by noon. So I went to the Aeroflot office and asked them to book a ticket for me. I was ready to buy the ticket right away. The Aeroflot officer asked me if I had a visa to Sweden and also asked for my passport. I just showed him a receipt issued to me by the Laotian Government. "My passport's with them," I said. This was an old receipt, but on the basis of this receipt they issued my ticket for Stockholm. The staff simply told me that they would check my passport at the time of departure. So now I was booked for the forthcoming Saturday. Regarding visa matters I told them that as an Indian I did not require a Swedish visa. They called the Indian Embassy and confirmed that Indian and Swedish persons did not require visas to enter each other's countries accepted policy those days.

The Saturday I was scheduled to take off for Sweden, all the margiis whom I had initiated came to see me at the jagriti for lesson revisions. I spent time with them, and as a result I reached the airport only a half hour before take off. The Aeroflot check-in counter was closed. I could not catch the flight. The staff only returned once the plane had already taken off. Now I was worried, because I had closed my visa in Laos, as I thought I would be leaving. I told them I no longer had a visa to stay in Laos.

"Let me see your passport and visa," the staff manager said. I just opened up the World Service Authority passport to the exact page where my expired visa was stamped, so he did not see the cover of my passport. He looked the visa over and saw that I was telling the truth.

"I can book you for next Saturday," he told me, "And I can give you a letter that will allow you to stay one week longer here in Laos." Then he started turning through the passport pages, one by one, looking over the different visas - the Thai visa, the Cambodian visa, the exit permit from Nepal, the permanent residence visa from Laos, then finally he came to the main page displaying my photo, name and address. Then he closed my passport from the back - thus not seeing the Globe WSA photograph on the front. In this way BABA saved me once again. Now I was booked for the following week.

The next Saturday, I called all the margiis to come to the airport with me to see me off. More than one hundred margiis came to the airport. We were all singing BABA NAM KEVALAM. I remained unworried about the task ahead - I was able to avoid getting my passport checked by telling the Aeroflot staff that I had no luggage to check-in. So I just spent time with all the margiis, dancing kiirtan and doing meditation right in front of the Aeroflot counter. Since I had no luggage to check-in, the staff planned to wait for me until the last minute; they only told me to get on the plane just a few minutes before the take off. Thanks to this, they let me pass right through customs and immigration and let me board the plane, my passport unchecked once again. They said that they had already checked my passport the week before so they didn't need to see it again. I mentally gave pranam to BABA, gave my namaskar (greetings) to the margiis, and left Laos for Sweden.

The Aeroflot plane first came to Burma, landed at Rangoon then flew off to Bombay. At that time in India, during the Emergency Period, Ananda Marga was banned (1975). I was wearing my Ananda Marga monk uniform. All the passengers were asked to leave the plane and were requested to proceed

All the Cambodians there were living in panic, feeling that their lives were now coming to an end with the enemy only twenty kilometers away. Everybody asked me why I did come to that city? "Every body was fleeing for their lives and I came there."

I went to a University to talk to the students and professors. They were all in a terribly mentally stressed state. I taught them

BABA NAM KEVALAM kiirtan by the thousands. Every day there were literally thousands of us singing BABA's mantra together in the football ground, sitting and meditating as well. Whatever I said they were just following, as they had no other hope. Even the senior most monks at the Buddhist temple I was staying at, even they were learning Ananda Marga meditation. They were depending on BABA only because BABA was their only hope.

I went to the United Nations office to seek refugee status. They refused, as I was born in India. I contacted some embassies, which were closing their offices due to war. They refused to grant me a visa with this WSA passport I had. Even the Thai embassy was refusing to give me a visa. Fortunately, the United Nations had given me one assurance: that they would take me out of Cambodia as soon as the airport runway was repaired.

Every day I was visiting the United Nations office to see if there was any possibility of flying out. One day I forgot my bag and accidentally left it there at the UN office. After walking for five minutes, I realized that I had left my bag and walked back to fetch it. I had been walking through the center of the city. One rocket full of bombs landed and exploded in that center of the city and killed many, many people on the spot had bullet injuries. Had I not forgotten my bag at the United Nations office, I would have been one of the victims of that rocket explosion. It's truly amazing the ways BABA was saving my life.

After two months of waiting for a flight, finally a United Nations aircraft came from Bangkok and landed in the city I was staying at. The airport was partially repaired by this point so the emergency aircraft was able to land. I went to the UN office as I did every other morning to ask about flights, and to my surprise they told me to come in an hour with all my things to leave Phenom Penh and land in Bangkok. I packed my things as fast as I could and came right back. In the noontime we flew off from Phenom Penh and landed in Bangkok, Thailand. Thanks to my permanent resident visa of Laos, I was able to get a seven-day transit visa there in Thailand. These seven days were the only days left for me to make effort to get out of South East Asia by getting a visa from some other country.

Meanwhile, while I had been in Cambodia the Dada who was in charge of Bangkok unit sent a message to the Central Office that I was trapped in Cambodia. The matter was reported to BABA. Then BABA sent a message for me, "LET HIM GO WHEREVER HE WANTS, LET HIM DO WHATEVER HE WANTS." Earlier I was trying only to proceed to Middle Eastern or African countries, but now with this message I had gotten permission to proceed to any country, even to stay somewhere and get political asylum if necessary. This was just what I needed.

Again I went to the United Nations office to seek refugee status. I was able to contact the highest office of the UNO there in Bangkok—they were surprised to see me traveling with a World Service Authority passport like a regular National passport holder. He said to me, "People who have this passport are put in jail, how could you be traveling with this, like a regular passport holder?" I told him that the LORD had sent me on a special mission so I could travel even without any passport—it was all the grace of the Lord (BABA Anandamurti only.)

I contacted almost every embassy there was to get a visa, any visa to any country of the world. When no country was ready to recognize this World Service Authority passport then I decided to settle in Laos, as I had a permanent visa there. I also kept my eyes open to seek political asylum in Sweden.

One sad thing happened; the day I left Cambodia, later that night the Communists attacked and took over all of Phenom Penh. They butchered many innocent people. Had I stayed even one day more, I would have been caught in their trap as well. How minutely BABA was controlling every big and small event of my life!

> "The greater the task, the mightier the obstacles. That is why the person who wants to perform noble deeds must be ready to face opposition from the very outset." — BABA

# 31. BABA SAVES ME FROM RUSSIAN-KGB

After one week of struggling and failing to get any visa from any country, finally I landed up in Laos once again. It was the only country that didn't close its doors to me. Once there, I decided to try to get to Sweden and seek political asylum there. Since BABA gave me the message that I could go or do whatever what I wanted, then I felt BABA would guide me through HIS ideas and would show me the path. I started to make all the necessary arrangements to go to Stockholm, Sweden.

There was an Aeroflot flight from Vientiane, Laos to Moscow then from Moscow to Stockholm. This flight had its service only once in a week, every Saturday, reaching Moscow on Sunday in the early morning then Stockholm by noon. So I went to the Aeroflot office and asked them to book a ticket for me. I was ready to buy the ticket right away. The Aeroflot officer asked me if I had a visa to Sweden and also asked for my passport. I just showed him a receipt issued to me by the Laotian Government. "My passport's with them," I said. This was an old receipt, but on the basis of this receipt they issued my ticket for Stockholm. The staff simply told me that they would check my passport at the time of departure. So now I was booked for the forthcoming Saturday. Regarding visa matters I told them that as an Indian I did not require a Swedish visa. They called the Indian Embassy and confirmed that Indian and Swedish persons did not require visas to enter each other's countries accepted policy those days.

The Saturday I was scheduled to take off for Sweden, all the margiis whom I had initiated came to see me at the jagriti for lesson revisions. I spent time with them, and as a result I reached the airport only a half hour before take off. The Aeroflot check-in counter was closed. I could not catch the flight. The staff only returned once the plane had already taken off. Now I was worried, because I had closed my visa in Laos, as I thought I would be leaving. I told them I no longer had a visa to stay in Laos.

"Let me see your passport and visa," the staff manager said. I just opened up the World Service Authority passport to the exact page where my expired visa was stamped, so he did not see the cover of my passport. He looked the visa over and saw that I was telling the truth.

"I can book you for next Saturday," he told me, "And I can give you a letter that will allow you to stay one week longer here in Laos." Then he started turning through the passport pages, one by one, looking over the different visas—the Thai visa, the Cambodian visa, the exit permit from Nepal, the permanent residence visa from Laos, then finally he came to the main page displaying my photo, name and address. Then he closed my passport—from the back—thus not seeing the Globe WSA photograph on the front. In this way BABA saved me once again. Now I was booked for the following week.

The next Saturday, I called all the margiis to come to the airport with me to see me off. More than one hundred margiis came to the airport. We were all singing BABA NAM KEVALAM. I remained unworried about the task ahead—I was able to avoid getting my passport checked by telling the Aeroflot staff that I had no luggage to check-in. So I just spent time time with all the margiis, dancing kiirtan and doing meditation right in front of the Aeroflot counter. Since I had no luggage to check-in, the staff planned to wait for me until the last minute; they only told me to get on the plane just a few minutes before the take off. Thanks to this, they let me pass right through customs and immigration and let me board the plane, my passport unchecked once again. They said that they had already checked my passport the week before so they didn't need to see it again. I mentally gave pranam to BABA, gave my namaskar (greetings) to the margiis, and left Laos for Sweden.

The Aeroflot plane first came to Burma, landed at Rangoon then flew off to Bombay. At that time in India, during the Emergency Period, Ananda Marga was banned (1975). I was wearing my Ananda Marga monk uniform. All the passengers were asked to leave the plane and were requested to proceed

to the airport transit hall. I could not leave this plane. If I did, the CBI would surely know that I was an Ananda Marga monk escaping from India and I would end up in jail again. Thinking this, I covered all of my orange dress in a brown woolen shawl and leaned over the window, pretending to be sick. A team of Intelligence police came on to plane to check and inspect. I was in deep sleep. Of course, I was pretending. Then I heard them speaking amongst themselves.

"It seems he is sick," I heard them say. "Let him sleep. Let us not disturb him." Then they left the plane. Half an hour later all the passengers got on the plane again and we took off from Bombay towards Moscow. This was how by the grace of BABA, I could avoid another arrest in India.

But I could not avoid arrest in Moscow when our plane landed the next morning. It was 6 AM when the plane landed - my flight for Stockholm was not until 11 AM. With five hours of layover time, all the transit passengers were asked to leave the plane and go through immigration. I had no way out, and I knew it; I was immediately arrested for not having an Indian passport and was put in the airport jail.

A team of Russian intelligence officials from the KGB came to enquire about me. They wrote down all my details.

"Why didn't you travel with an Indian passport?" They asked me. Very fortunately for me, it was now Sunday morning. Being Sunday, the KGB could not contact the Indian Embassy for my details, as they were closed. The officials were surprised about how I could get an Aeroflot ticket without a valid passport.

"Last week I also had a reservation from Vientiane to Stockholm via Moscow," I said. "I have nothing to do with Moscow, so why are you holding me here?" The KGB enquiry group wrote down all the details of my story and asked me many questions. After some time they left me but very soon a second enquiry group came to take down my story as well. I felt that they would not let me go to Stockholm. The flight was at 11 AM. Already it was 9 AM.

A third KGB enquiry group came. I started to rebuke the KGB enquiry team by saying, "Listen, I have nothing to do in Moscow, Russia, and I have to get on the 11 AM flight to Stockholm. Last week your Aeroflot staff made a mistake so I had to miss the plane. This week you are holding me here unnecessarily in Moscow, thus violating the Warsaw International Travel Pact. You are not following your own ticketing system. In fact, now you have to pay me 70,000 US dollars as a fine as per the International agreement of the IATA. You violated it twice. Sum total will be 140,000 US Dollar. I will file a case against Russian official airlines, Aeroflot in International Court at The Hague, Holland, then you will have to pay several millions of dollars as damage for holding me here in Moscow. You are spoiling my important meetings in Stockholm. I will go around the whole world holding press conferences in all the cities of the globe, telling them that no one should travel by Aeroflot as they violate all International Agreements. Aeroflot & Russia as a result will be incurring millions of dollars of loss. How could you book me and how could you hold me in Russia and on what basis?" The KGB group just wrote down all of my fiery speech and left without any questions.

A fourth team of KGB came. Now I was really annoyed, until I heard them speak. "We're letting you board the plane to Stockholm," they told me. They also told me one interesting thing, that if I had come last week (last Sunday) as per my first booking, then they never would have allowed me to travel to Stockholm because many important Russian officials were traveling on that flight: But this Sunday nobody important was on my plane, so they were allowing me to travel to Stockholm. I think the threatening I gave them had worked. They were not my ideas. I felt that BABA was speaking through me. Otherwise how could I ever think of such things. Had the KGB decided to hold me for one more day, the next day being Monday, the Indian Embassy would have opened and I surely would have been deported back to the Indian jails or just killed in some Soviet jail because of the ban on the Ananda Marga. Really, BABA saved me yet once again.

So I was able to take the 11 AM flight, and I landed safely in Stockholm. BABA's blessing. Great is BABA!

> "Marching ahead is life, crushing the pebbles of hindrances and obstacles with the stroke of your feet, disdaining the frowns of tornadoes, meteors and roaring thunder, and rendering all superstitions to ashes without any second thought. March on and on, *Parama Purus'a* is with you. Victory is yours." - BABA

# 32. ACCEPTED IN SWEDEN - BABA'S GRACE

When I got to the immigration counter in Stockholm, the official just eyed me suspiciously. "I'd like to see your real passport, please," he said. My WSA passport was not real enough for them, so I was kept aside waiting for a half hour before telling me that I would not be allowed to enter Sweden with a WSA passport. I would be sent back to Moscow again. Again I was in trouble. After all I had gone through to get out of the KGB's clutches I'd just be sent right back into them.

Actually what happened was that when I was in Laos, when I had missed the first flight, (that first Saturday) I spent the extra week doing a lot of deep meditation, thinking of BABA all the time. While doing this meditation BABA gave me the idea and guidelines to write about the tortures I had gone through in jail in India, the tortures of Ananda Marga in India and all this and more in a letter seeking political asylum in Sweden. In that one-week time I wrote two pages all concerning the history of my tortures, the persecution of BABA and Ananda Marga, the declaration of emergency in India, etc. The letter was addressed to the King of Sweden, seeking political asylum. When Sweden refused to give me entry into Sweden then I told them that I was jailed in India and how I had escaped from India with the help of this WSA passport because India had refused to give me a real one. Then I narrated to them the trouble I had just had with the KGB in Moscow and finally handed them the letter I had written. The officials took this and went away for another hour before coming to their decision: I would be allowed to enter Sweden.

They explained to me, "There's a law here in Sweden that if any person asks for political asylum at the border then we have to allow entry." And so I was allowed in. "It will take a few more months for the government to decide whether this is a genuine case for political asylum or not." Then they gave me a thousand dollars, a city bus and train ticket monthly card and even fixed

an appointment for me to buy another thousand dollars worth of clothes and shoes, etc. This was how I could gain entry into Sweden with two thousand dollars just because of a two-page letter that BABA mentally dictated to me. This was all just BABA's grace. Really, without BABA this WSA passport was worthless; it was only because of BABA that I was ever able to get anywhere with it, and no other reason.

And so, in 1975 I entered Sweden as a political refugee. It took six months for the Swedish government to complete their inquiry. They were able to verify Ananda Marga's persecution in India as well as my own imprisonment, so they allowed me to stay. In the meantime I toured all over Sweden, initiating many onto the spiritual path. I traveled even to the extreme North of Sweden and taught meditation there to several hundreds.

One day I was driving a car near the North Pole to reach an interior place to address a group on Yoga. I had no experience driving on snow. One brother and myself were in the car. I was driving at ninety kilometers per hour speed at the top of a snow-buried hill. I was in hurry, but the brother sitting beside me advised me to slow down. "We may slip and lose control of the car," he said. Actually if I slowed down then we might be late for the Yoga programme we were scheduled for, so I was reluctant to slow down. Still, I listened to him and pushed my foot on the break. All of a sudden I lost control of the car; the car turned ninety degrees and flew in the air over the top of the hill, somersaulted and finally landed near the top of the hill with the whole vehicle turned upside down. It was a small Volkswagen car made by Hitler during World War II. Even after landing in the snow, the car's smooth top continued to slide down the hill before skiing to the bottom - there the car flipped over once more before landing right side up at the bottom of the hill. Miraculously, I was unharmed, but I thought that maybe the brother had died. But he was unharmed as well, thinking that maybe I was dead too. By the grace of BABA we both were alive, unhurt and unscathed. We did not have even a single bruise. The car meanwhile, got completely crushed, totaled. Somehow, we both got out from the car and walked up the hill on the snowy path the car had just made for us when it skied down to the bottom. It is a good thing it made this path, otherwise we would not have been able to walk up the hill. We might have frozen to death, but all was well controlled by BABA. We walked slowly, helping each other until we reached the road. Immediately came one car that stopped for us. They drove us right to the town I had to give my lecture in.

My lecture was widely published in the local newspaper so around one hundred people were waiting to hear my talk. I did not mention the car accident I had just survived, but just went right on and gave my lecture on "Yoga and Meditation". When I finished, forty people approached me to learn meditation. It was only until around ten o'clock, long after the programme finished that I mentioned the car. They were surprised, and immediately ordered a crane to lift the car for me. The car was totaled, but thankfully, the persons who arranged this Yoga programme for me arranged another car for me as well. This was how BABA again saved me from a serious accident. HIS work also continued as usual in spite of so many obstacles. When we do HIS work, many problems come. Still, we should carry on until all these problems finally disappear. It is all his LIILA (cosmic sport).

> "The Supreme cognition completely absorbs the individual 'I', and this state of total surrender of the individual brings complete fulfillment." - BABA

# PART - VI

# PERSECUTION OF BABA

### 33. SWEDEN QUESTIONS INDIA IN UNO

The Swedish and Indian governments had good relations. It was because of this that at first I was worried about getting political asylum there. After all, they could just hand me right over to India. Then BABA gave me a plan in my mind to contact top Swedish officers and explain to them the tortures inflicted upon Ananda Margiis in India. I started contacting many officials in Sweden and in the process gained a lot of sympathy from many Swedish citizens. This also helped me to initiate and create many margiis.

This all came to my head when myself and two Swedish margiis joined me in a protest fast just in front of the Swedish Parliament. There we made a setup highlighting BABA's fast in jail (HE had been fasting for two years by them). The poison he had been administered, as well as the torturing of many other margiis in India. We also were sure to point out that it was the Russian and Indian governments working hand in hand to kill BABA and Ananda Marga, especially through the ban imposed on the organization during the "Emergency Period". In this way we got about fifteen thousand signatures from different Swedish people in support of our protest fast for BABA's release.

Our mission was to raise awareness in the world about the situation Ananda Marga was facing in India. BABA was imprisoned, in 1973 he was poisoned, and on the $4^{\text{th}}$ of July 1975, Ananda Marga was officially banned. Thousands and thousands of margiis were all thrown in jail. Two Dadas self-immolated just after BABA started HIS fast and already another acarya, Dada Atulananda, who was in jail with BABA, had also committed self-immolation on $11^{\text{th}}$ December 1974. He could not tolerate the harassment given to BABA there in jail. Still India just persecuted BABA and HIS devotees more and more. India, the CBI, and the KGB—they were all working together, just to persecute BABA.

In December 1974 a team of Police were sent to search BABA's room. By this time BABA's fast had been going on for one year and nine months. BABA just lay on the bed as the police searched HIS cell fruitlessly. They found nothing, but still they took BABA's three-stoned ring and sent for investigation—they thought that the ring might be working as a microphone for sending secret messages outside jail. Every day BABA's messages were going out from HIS cell all over India and around the world, even in spite of HIS imprisonment. This was why they searched HIS cell. Another reason was that a team of Police was trained by the Russian Special Forces to attack BABA in jail and kill HIM. But the police did not dare to enter the room of BABA. They would come near HIS room and then just withdraw. So the Russian intelligence gave instructions to get the police accustomed to coming close to BABA in the form of searches, of both BABA's body and HIS things. Dada Atulananda could not tolerate these inhuman insults given to BABA. Dada was personally taking care of BABA there in jail as HIS Personal Assistant (PA). Dada requested the Indian Government to return

the ring within a week's time otherwise he would self-immolate there in jail. He sent protest letters to many different Indian officials. The Indian government, who was working under the Soviets naturally didn't pay any attention. Then on 11th December 1974 Dada Atulananda committed self-immolation, just as he said he would. When someone dies in jail, the jail Superintendent becomes the criminal. The case went against the jail Superintendent, and it was he who had to appear in court because of Dada Atulananda's actions. After this the jail Superintendent did not cooperate with the Indian and Russian police efforts to attack and kill BABA in jail. And so, Dada sacrificed his life for the mission and was able to prevent more attacks on HIM. Dada's sacrifice was really Great!

I came to know all this in Sweden as I was having regular contact with India, and it inspired me to start the protest fast. After we had collected all these fifteen thousand signatures, we submitted them to the Swedish government. There was a law in Sweden that if ten thousand (10,000) Swedish people signed any petition and submitted it to the Government of Sweden then the Government was obliged to take up the demand and act on it. And so, in spite of good relations between Sweden and India, Sweden charged the Indian government in the United Nations on the Human Rights Floor on the points that the persecution of BABA, of BABA's devotees and BABA's fasting were all gross violations of human rights. The Indian government was unpleasantly surprised to find that Sweden come out against them on such a public forum. They came to know that I was the key person behind all these happenings in Sweden.

> "The accumulated distortions of many lifetimes cannot be removed in the twinkling of an eye. The removal of these requires prolonged cultivation of knowledge, selfless devotion and untiring action.
>
> The world expects tremendous work from you; so you must not sit idle like a frog in a well under the spell of inertia. Therefore awake, arise." - BABA

## 34. SWEDEN RESPECTS BABA AND HIS IDEALS

One time, I was able to meet the Swedish Prime Minister, Olof Palme, and was able to explain to him about the BABA's fasting and tortures on margiis in India. I requested him to grant BABA political asylum. The Prime Minister gave me a letter to be given to the Chairman of the Swedish Immigration Department. I went with the Prime Minister's letter to meet the top Immigration official in the country. He spent five and half-hours with me, going over with me all of BABA's tortures in jail, my arrest in India, the Indian government working hand in hand with the Soviets, Ananda Marga ideology, Prout etc. After our talk he gave the decision to grant political asylum to me. Seeing that I was already in Sweden, I was granted asylum right away, and as for BABA, he told me that the moment BABA arrived in Sweden, and HE would be given all respects. They liked BABA's ideas and HIS Mission. It was my duty and responsibility to get BABA from India to Sweden. He asked me to come back to his office in the next week so that I would be given an official governmental order.

Next week when I returned, I was officially granted political asylum. He gave me a passport - a real passport - that was good to travel to the different Scandinavian countries like Norway, Finland, Denmark and Iceland. He told me one very interesting thing: "It still remains a mystery to us how you could travel all the way to Sweden without a passport but instead with a private and invalid document. Still, we are giving you political asylum here in Sweden. BABA'S word is law in this universe. When BABA says something nature, creation and all super powers obey HIM. HIS WILL IS EVERYTHING. Hence it is wise to surrender unto HIM.

> "Thorns on the path cannot deter those who have assembled with the determination to march in unison. They are blowing the trumpets of victory in an effort to conquer the universe of a timid mind. O human beings, march ahead. Let the song of march be your only slogan." - BABA

## 35. HEINOUS PLAN TO TERMINATE BABA

Now that I had good contacts with the Swedish government, I wanted to know what their opinion was about the Indian version. They showed me an official letter sent by the government of India mentioning that Ananda Marga should not be supported in the country, as it was a "criminal organisation." On this pretext the Indian government had already banned Ananda Marga; now they were requesting all countries to ban Ananda Marga in their countries as well. I asked the opinion of the Swedish government on the matter. They told me very clearly that India and Russia were friends. Nonetheless, when India was sending such a letter attacking Ananda Marga then one thing was made clear for them - that Ananda Marga was not communist and not in the least dangerous because it was only Communists blaming Ananda Marga of these things. So actually they gathered from this letter that Ananda Marga must be a good organisation. They openly allowed Ananda Marga to work fully in the country. This was the opinion of all other countries in Europe as well. Just see how BABA was turning all the nasty, negative slander attempts to tarnish the image of the Ananda Marga were eventually turned in our favour. The evil forces could not harm us in any way. This is BABA's Liila.

The Indian Government under the advice of Russian Intelligence declared Emergency in India and banned Ananda Marga on 4th July, 1975. They adopted a divide and rule policy to finish the organisation. Hence, it was only quite natural for the opposite group to be getting so much support from the government. The Russian's crooked planning to attack BABA physically failed because of a self-immolation of many Dadas and Didis and the poisoning of BABA also failed when BABA survived even after administrating such a deadly poison. Now the last resort of these cruel forces was to ban all the branches of Ananda Marga and arrest all the Ananda Margiis all over India.

Just before all these plans started materialising, BABA suddenly gave an instruction to all margiis; "SAVE ENOUGH FOOD GRAINS FOR TWO YEARS." All the margiis all over India started saving food grains like rice, wheat, pulses and oil. The margiis thought that maybe some natural calamity might occur soon that would prevent crops from production of sufficient grains. When the government declared emergency, many thousands of margiis were arrested and thrown in jail, often the sole bread earner of the family. Many margiis had to go underground to escape arrest. Thanks to BABA's instructions, these families could survive on the stock of food materials they had stored even in the absence of males in the house. Just see how BABA takes care of HIS children and devotees.

When all the steps of the governments of India and Russia failed, finally they planned to just finish BABA off by giving HIM death sentence by court. After two and a half years in jail and no trial during the Emergency, BABA was convicted on the concocted charges of abetment to murder. It was the Emergency Period and the government was taking all steps to make sure that BABA'S "trial" was anything but fair. Still, all the devotees knew that BABA would eventually win. All of us had full faith in BABA even in spite of HIS persecution. They were planning to manipulate Him to be sentenced to death.

Just before BABA was convicted, BABA's junior lawyer – a margii – came to see HIM in jail. He said to BABA, "BABA tomorrow is the last date for our argument. The senior lawyer says we may lose this case..."

"What will happen if we lose the case?" BABA asked.

"They may award you the highest punishment as this is a fabricated murder case plotted by the Governments."

"They will give me the highest punishment then," BABA said, pausing and advised the lawyer to ask judge in advance the highest punishment even before the judge awards.

The next day the senior lawyer began his argument in front of the Judge in the Patna Session Court. He said, "Oh, your

Honor! Today I have come to mention that Socrates was poisoned, that Galileo was put to death, that Jesus Christ was crucified, let Shrii P.R. Sarkar (BABA) remain as immortal as these others. Let HIM be awarded highest punishment. This is my conclusion."

The next day the judge had to deliver his final judgment. This judgment had been already planned in Moscow, sent to Delhi and now was with the Patna judge who would just read the final judgment for BABA to be executed next day. This was the planning of the Communist Russian Government and India as well. Mrs. Indira Gandhi, the Prime Minister of India, was supporting it. So now the judge had to pass the death sentence against BABA. The government of India was going to give him a huge reward of several hundreds of thousands of rupees for passing such judgment. But still the judge stayed up the whole night, thinking over what he should do. Then the next day he came to the court and gave his judgment.

He said in his judgment, "Since P. R. Sarkar's (BABA's) lawyer asked for the death sentence for his client, so I am sentencing Mr. Sarkar to life imprisonment." The approver, that is, the defector of Ananda Marga, who confirmed that he committed the alleged murder, was given a pardon; the blame was put on BABA as a "conspirator" and was given life long imprisonment. I'm sure that this approver was an agent sent by CBI to speak against BABA, this defector was not only pardoned by the government, but given a hefty sum of money for his job. So he got mercy as per a pre-planned agreement and BABA was convicted on false charges. Is this justice? No, but still, it was a great victory within a loss.

The whole night the judge could not sleep even for a minute, thinking over what sentence he should award to BABA the next day, the judge kept thinking that if he were to give BABA the death sentence then the judge would be remembered in the history as a demon (as per Hindu mythology) and BABA as GOD incarnate.

When BABA had to give HIS testimony in court, BABA was carried out in front of this judge on a stretcher. BABA's worldly name is Prabhat Rainjan Sarkar. However, this case was being conducted in the state of Bihar, where they speak Hindi. So the judge asked BABA, "Which language would you like me to record your testimony in?" Judge meant BABA might record HIS speech either in Hindi or in Bengali language.

BABA replied, "As I know over two-hundred languages, you may record my testimony in any language you like." The judge was surprised by this reply. BABA continued, "These Indian laws all come from Britain, from British law, and were written down in Cambridge English. So I'll give my testimony in the Queen's English." BABA then proceeded to give HIS testimony in perfect Cambridge English, taking the time to explain certain things in simpler English for the judge when he couldn't understand the proper terminology. It was one occasion that HE revealed HIMSELF in the public.

So that night as the judge thought, "How can I give the death penalty to this great man?" He was afraid that maybe BABA was God. "Why should I become a demon for just a few hundred thousand rupees?"

So he decided to give BABA life imprisonment instead of the death penalty. This I came to know as the judge told BABA's lawyers after he delivered the sentence, and they told me. BABA had to fight all alone against these so-called "powerful" governments, these Communists. Actually they had no power at all, because all of the power they had, even to oppose HIM, was directly derived from The Supreme the Almighty Lord and none else.

BABA stands for Universal Ideology and love, for service and welfare to all Humans, plants, animals and the entire creation. Demonic forces are anything that stands in the way of Universalism - narrow sentiments, dogmatic religions, self-centered capitalists, and material-centered communists – all of this falls within that category, so it is only natural that it is they

who would unite to oppose DHARMA. This is polarisation. BABA was fasting for the past three years on account of these black, demonic forces. They were all manipulating to see BABA'S execution – they would have been the first ones at the gallows to see HIM go. What these demonic forces did not and could not understand was that BABA represents Cosmic Consciousness, omniscience and omnipotence. No power in heaven or on earth could disrupt HIM or HIS Mission.

The universe is full of energy, but energy is a blind force. BABA is Pure Consciousness, thus HE is the controller of that energy. So how could these measly governments, although appearing powerful, ever hope to defeat BABA? Rather, BABA demonstrated HIS Superiority to the world by proving that HE is ALL in ALL. HE is the LORD OF LORDS. BABA is the nucleus. HE is attracting everyone through centripetal (vidya) forces like love, compassion, service and devotion. HE is also throwing people away from HIMSELF through centrifugal forces like narrow sentiments, self-centered, material-centered and dogma-centered ideologies. Since BABA is the nucleus of all, all are moving around HIM either at shorter or longer radii. Hence the people moving at a shorter radius are HIS devotees and the people moving at a longer radius are opposing HIM, trying to persecute HIM and trying to stop HIS Mission. Could these Demonic forces be successful? No. How can something so distant from the Cosmic Nucleus ever hope to destroy the Cosmic Nucleus itself? All are moving around BABA only, for HE is the Nucleus of all nuclei, the LORD of all LORDS.

"HE is even where the human intellect cannot reach, and where the imagination bounds back, thwarted and baffled." - BABA

## 36. AMNESTY INTERNATIONAL, JURISTS FOR BABA

When I was in Sweden, I went to England and met Amnesty International in London. I narrated them all persecution stories. They had also all the information about BABA'S persecution. Here is their observation enclosed (only a part).

Amnesty International
International Secretariat
53 Theobald's Road
London WC1X 8SP

3rd July 1974

AMNESTY INTERNATIONAL'S POSITION REGARDING THE CASE OF MR. P.R. SARKAR

"Mr. Prabhat Ranjan Sarkar (Shrii Shrii Anandamurtii), who is the leader of the Ananda Marga Society, had been imprisoned since 29th December, 1971,..............

...Mr. Sarkar's health at the moment has seriously deteriorated, following a hunger strike, which he staged from 1st April 1973 onwards. On humanitarian grounds, Amnesty has now asked the Indian government to reconsider its position on the four demands made by Mr. P.R. Sarkar, and has specifically recommended that Mr. Sarkar's first demand relating to prison conditions be met as this is largely in accordance with Rule 87 and 92 of the United Nations Standards Minimum Rules for the treatment of Prisoners for prisoners awaiting trial. Mr. Sarkar had been specifically demanded that he be allowed to receive food from his residence, that he is granted interviews in jail with friends and relatives, and that four co-accused Ananda Marga Workers be brought back to the same jail.

Secondly, Amnesty asked the Indian Government to set up a judicial enquiry into the general conditions under which Mr.

Sarkar is detained and, more specifically into the allegations made by him that there was an attempt to poison him in prison on 12th February 1973.
