## 37. BABA'S BLESSINGS - SWEDEN CENTER

When Indira Gandhi declared State of Emergency to get "Dictatorial Powers," she banned Ananda Marga and all its branches all over. When this happened, the Government of India confiscated all of our buildings, meditation centers, schools, children homes, lands, farms and printing presses etc. Everything, even the training centers were closed. There was a fear of arrest in Nepal as well because Nepal and India were close friends. Well, Ananda Marga might have been banned in India but outside we were spreading like a wild fire. Many wanted to take whole timer training to become monks and nuns. The real question was how to do that, now that our training centers were closed in India.

I was working in Sweden though my posting was as Sectorial Secretary for Cairo and Nairobi Sectors, (Middle East, Africa, and Eastern European countries). I was a guiding worker of those sectors from Stockholm, Sweden only. I could not leave any of the Scandinavian countries due to my passport problem. I thought that since I was stationed, it would be better for me to impart whole timer training by starting a training center in Sweden or Norway. So I sent the proposal to BABA as Ananda Marga was banned in India. BABA gave HIS permission and sent HIS special blessings to start the whole timer-training centre in Sweden.

On receiving this instruction, I asked the Scandinavian margiis to look for two buildings, which I could use as a training center. One day in December 1975, I was delivering a lecture in Uppsala University, in a town in Sweden where a group of margiis was created. I was staying at the house of one margii there. I asked him to look for houses, which could be bought. I saw in one magazine an advertisement about an old abandoned house for sale. Previously it had been used as the folk dancing hall - it cost only ten thousand dollars ($10,000). I was interested

in it because all the other houses cost several hundreds of thousands of dollars. So we drove straight to the south of Sweden, in Smaland to see the house.

The house was abandoned fifteen years ago. None lived in it or used it for last fifteen years. There was a big hall; a drama stage with an attached green room and in the cellar was a kitchen, with cold, natural storing space. There was no electricity, no water, and no toilets. It was pretty much only a big hall that was once used for dance programs. I felt that it was best to just buy it, as we too would be using it for spiritual dancing – kiirtan (singing BABA NAM KEVALAM). So I bought the house in the name of a margii from Uppsala who later on became a Dada. This was how the whole timer training center project was started for special blessings of BABA.

Information was sent all over the world that a whole timer-training center had started in Sweden. Many prospective trainees from all over the world expressed their interest to come and join, so I fixed a date to start training of the first batch right away. Many started to arrive in Stockholm, Sweden from all over the world, especially from America and Australia. The French brother who helped me in Laos whom I gave local full timer training also came to Sweden to become a whole timer. Later on he became a monk well known by the name Ac Rudreshvarananda Avt who was the pioneer of Burkina Faso relief project in Africa.

I bought a second-hand car and also bought utensils, food etc. And so, in Ydrefors, Gullringen, in the South of Sweden, in the beginning of 1976 on an Amavasya (new moon), fasting day, we officially began training classes. The village hall I used to train the brothers with, but a group of sisters also arrived for training, so I rented a flat in the village for them. They were the lucky ones, with all the amenities of hot water and electricity. But for us brothers, living in the old, abandoned dancing hall, things were a bit tougher. It couldn't even justifiably be called a house – it was a house structure that we were living in. Either way, we started training with three hours of kiirtan, singing BABA NAM KEVALAM, the brothers in the old abandoned hall and the sisters in their rented flat. This was how the whole timer-training center started there in Sweden.

The next morning, we woke up at four o'clock to find the whole landscape to have blanketed itself in the night with a fresh layer of white snow. We were warm enough in the night thanks to our sleeping bags, which were good enough for extreme cold temperatures (around minus 20°C). There was no water for shower, so we all took the name of BABA and started jogging towards a lake, which was two kilometers from the training center. Not surprisingly, the lake was totally frozen over. Nonetheless, at the edge of the lake we broke a sheet of ice where it was thin and jumped in for our morning baths. It was -20° outside. We broke the ice near the shore and swam into the water under the frozen ice. This was our shower. Our shower's ceiling was the frozen ice above us and actually compared to what it was like outside, the water was warm down there. After bathing we came back, put on our clothes and ran the two kilometers distance back to make us warm again. Reaching the training center, we sat for meditation from 5:00 AM to 8:00 AM, covering ourselves with thick blankets. After asanas (yoga exercises), we were finally ready to break our fast. There was no tap and thus no drinking water in the house; thankfully, there was plenty of drinking water outside in the form of frozen ice. We collected ice from outside, put it in a pot, heated the ice with the forest wood available and solved the problem. This was how training was started by the grace of BABA.

I got the water supply and electricity connections in two months. Meanwhile, we all worked on the building and made the interiors beautiful. After three months, we invited all the local villagers for a tea party. They were all happy to look at the improvements we had made on what had been lying for so long just was an old and abandoned building. They had happy memories of the good times they had spent there dancing and celebrating. Selected for BABA's Mission, this once rejected, abandoned place was now a vibrating place of great importance.

Our whole-timer training center was in the midst of an alpine jungle, surrounded on all sides by pine forest, ponds, lakes and flowing rivulets. It was a perfect place for sadhana. It was free from industrial pollution or any other crude influences on the mind. We spent twelve hours in spiritual pursuits like kiirtan, sadhana and asanas; six to seven hours for studies, bath and food etc. We would sleep for only five hours at night.

We ate mostly raw salads, fresh fruits and berries from the forest; this was our main diet. I approached the supermarkets in the nearby town of Vimmerby for food collection. From the supermarkets we would collect as donation lot of fruits and vegetables. I approached them and asked if the food could be given to us so that we could feed the forest birds and animals. They agreed. There was snow everywhere, so it was difficult for the animals to get food. Once a week, we went around all the supermarkets and collected the fruits and vegetables. We even fed animals and birds. There were enough food, fruits, vegetables and milk products for all the trainees. We enjoyed taking it as prasad (spiritually vibrated food) given to us by BABA.

The brothers and sisters who came for the whole timer training, the trainees, within the short span of six months of training, became Dadas or Didis, put on orange uniform and started working in society as respectable and dignified members of the community. These whole timers generated such a great spiritual flow and energy that those who came in their contact got vibrated and charged with tremendous spiritual energy. This was all the miracle of BABA. It was only by the grace of BABA that could and did convert ordinary persons into extraordinary persons possessing all divine qualities.

> "When a spiritual aspirant gets adequately acquainted with HIS Blissful Entity, we say that he is established in the Supreme Consciousness." - BABA

---

### 38. NON-STOP 365 DAYS KIIRTAN IN SWEDEN

In modern physics when anything is repeated again and again it creates a wave; a continuous wave can influence a lot. Similarly, in Spiritual Science - Intuitional Science - when a Siddha Mantra like BABA NAM KEVALAM is repeated continuously then this repetition generates a very powerful vibration and whosoever gets involved in that wave starts feeling highly elevated and peaceful. At the whole timer-training center in Sweden, everyday three hours of kiirtan was sung in the evening from 6:00 PM to 9:00 PM followed by meditation and BABA stories. For several years this practice continued. Later, the intense interest in kiirtan made the trainees do kiirtan by turns all the twenty-four hours of the day. This non-stop kiirtan continued for an entire one-year period during the Emergency.

Whosoever visited our training center felt immense peace and bliss. Some margiis from Norway and Sweden went to see BABA in jail in India. They met BABA in jail and felt so peaceful and powerful. When these margiis came to visit our training center in Sweden, the non-stop BABA NAM KEVALAM kiirtan had by this point been going on for several months. They felt the same as if BABA was physically present there with us. This was because of our non-stop kiirtan. Now here they were in Sweden, feeling the same feeling as they had when they were with BABA in jail in Patna, Bihar, India.

This means that if we do continuous kiirtan running for several months and years, we will also have the presence of BABA. Hence, the future of humanity is Great and bright. More and more kiirtans have to be organized everywhere to diffuse the crude waves of this world and to establish sublime, spirituality on this planet earth. Three hours minimum kiirtan is called Akhanda Kiirtan. We must organize more and more of Akhanda kiirtans everywhere on this globe for establishment of peace, prosperity and harmony in the society.

Lord Krishna used to vibrate His devotees through His flute. Now just by singing BABA NAM KEVALAM, one can generate the same spiritual and blissful vibrations that Lord Krishna emanated through HIS flute.

> “*My Lord* Krishna plays HIS flute even today. Only the lucky ones get to listen to HIS divine music.” - BABA

### 39. BABA'S DARSHAN IN JAIL, AGAIN

Whole timer training sessions were of six months duration. New batches of trainees were arriving regularly. Still my desire of visiting India to have BABA'S darshan in jail grew stronger. That would be a bit difficult though, as I didn't have a passport to take me there, not to mention the fact that I was blacklisted for getting the Swedish government to attack India in the UN on the grounds of human rights violations. The passport the Swedish Government gave me was only good for a few European countries, and traveling to India with the WSA passport was out of the question. I decided I had to go to India to see BABA by any means. By this time BABA'S fasting had been for more than four years. For more than four years BABA took nothing but curd water, provided to HIM by HIS PA Dada Ramananda. This amount of food he took to avoid force-feeding by the authorities.

I made a plan to travel to India with the passport I had. I bought my ticket and flew directly from Stockholm to Karachi, Pakistan. Had I landed in India I might have been arrested at the airport. From Karachi I traveled by train to Lahore, which was just near the Indian-Pakistani border. I had no problem getting into India at the border because I had a European passport. The immigration officials just let me into the country as if I was just an ordinary tourist. I took a train from Amritsar to Patna (India). In Patna, I met BABA'S Personal Assistant, Acarya Ramananda Avadhuta. I accompanied him to jail to see BABA. Just as last time, we were only given twenty minutes to have BABA's darshan. With us came other workers from other parts of India also to have HIS darshan.

We all entered BABA'S very small cell. BABA was lying on HIS cot. A CBI Intelligence officer meanwhile sat like a rat in the corner to listen to everything we said. It was this man's job to regularly inform his authorities in India exactly what was happening there in BABA'S little room. Still, BABA acted as if we were alone with HIM and asked us all to come nearer.

We all did Sastaunga Pranam to BABA. First BABA's Personal Assistant spoke with BABA and gave HIM HIS curd water in a flask brought from BABA's house. BABA then continued to talk to PA Dada for a little while in a low voice. Then BABA looked over all the rest of us and said, "I would like to speak with the one who came from the extreme North." The other workers understood that BABA meant me, and as I stepped forward, the CBI man thought BABA meant that I came from the extreme North of India, but of course BABA really meant the extreme North of Europe.

BABA started to speak with me slowly in a low tone as the CBI (Indian Intelligence) was listening carefully to know what BABA was saying to me. BABA asked, "How many boys and girls are there in your home?" In India we had many schools and homes; we were responsible for schools and homes and also responsible for reporting to BABA the progress. BABA meant to know how many trainees were there in Sweden training center. How many brothers were ready to become Dadas and how many sisters were ready to become Didis?

"I have twelve boys and ten girls in my children's home," I replied.

BABA said, "Your love and care should be greater than enforcement of rules, regulations and discipline." I was harsh in maintaining the strict routine and discipline. BABA wanted that I should be more loving to the trainees so that the trainees would feel more comfortable to follow the discipline and they would follow easily. This was what BABA meant. I asked BABA if I should make any more changes in the training. BABA said, "No, everything is very fine".

BABA gave a message to trainees, "Tell them that they are the pioneers and harbingers of a New Civilization. The Lord's grace is with them. Victory will be theirs." This was the message BABA was giving to the trainees. The CBI (Indian Intelligence) man was thinking that BABA was giving this message to the boys and girls of some imaginary children's home somewhere.

Later on BABA spoke quickly one by one to all. As our time was running out, we all did collective Guru Puja at HIS feet, and then BABA gave me some prasad (spiritually vibrated food) for the trainees in the form of some sugar candy bottles (given to HIM by devotees to keep under HIS bed and distribute as prasad). This was how I enjoyed BABA's darshan in jail. Finally we did Sastaunga Pranam to BABA and left.

> “All human beings are to be served with the same steadiness in all the three strata - physical, mental and spiritual - and you have to render service by ascribing Narayanahood to the served. Remember that your service does not oblige the Supreme, rather you are obliged because you have such a chance to serve the Supreme in the form of living beings." - BABA

# 40. BABA SAVES MY LIFE IN PAKISTAN

After enjoying BABA's blissful darshan, I planned to return to Sweden to continue my work i.e. imparting training to make Dadas and Didis. I traveled by train from Patna to Amritsar, the bordering town of India. I crossed the Indian border at Wagah and entered Lahore, Pakistan. I took the train from Lahore to Karachi and from there I planned to fly back to Sweden.

As I was traveling from Lahore to Karachi, I met many Pakistani passengers on the train who became good friends. I bought a second-class ticket to meet ordinary Pakistanis. There were four people in particular from a Military Dairy Farm near Karachi. Over the course of our friendly conversations, I told them that I came from Sweden and was now returning to Sweden. I had a flight to Stockholm the next night. Our train was arriving just in the morning, so as I wasn't taking off until nighttime, I had free time during the day. They invited me to visit their Dairy Farm. They had plenty of milk and milk products and said I should stop by and try some. They requested me to visit them before I left for Europe. I thought the whole day was free, so why not? So I agreed to visit them in the afternoon. They gave me their address and also explained the directions to reach their place.

After I confirmed my travel reservation to Stockholm from Karachi I had the whole day to my disposal, so I took a local train to the Military Dairy Farm, on the outskirts of Karachi. I met the four persons who invited me at the nearby military barracks. They appeared surprised to see me. They showed me a room where I could keep my things and rest. Somehow their attitude and behavior had changed since I saw them on the train. I started to doubt them. I had to leave them in a few hours anyhow, so I didn't think much of it.

I planned to leave them after bathing and doing my evening sadhana. Four other persons volunteered to show me the place where I could bathe. I took my towel, clothes and soap and went off with the four people to see where I could bathe. They were taking me off through some wheat fields. We crossed the barracks where there were big tanks and water and now they were taking me through the green fields where there wasn't anything at all. I was getting annoyed, as it was getting late for my sadhana.

"Why are you taking me away from the barracks?" I protested. "There is a lot of water back there, I saw it; I don't see any buildings now, I see fields. Let's go back – I can take a bath at the barracks."

"The water in the tanks is only good for the cows and buffaloes, not for a proper bath," they said.

"I don't have time for this," I said angrily. "It's getting late and I'm in a hurry, so I'll take a shower at the barracks." Finally they led me back and showed me where the water taps were. They left me alone as I pretended I was about to take a bath.

Hurriedly, I put on clothes and walked slowly towards the barracks where these four people would meet the four who had met me on the train and invited me. I wanted to know the reason behind their strange behavior. I hid myself by the window and listened in on their conversation.

"What happened?" The four who invited me asked. "Why are you back so quickly?"

"He wouldn't walk any further into the fields," the four who escorted me said. "There's too much light out so we couldn't kill him unless he came out with us farther."

Shocked, I rushed back to the tank, took my shower and went back to my room, the new realization that they were planning to rob and kill me still in my mind. Now as I sat for my sadhana, I saw that the small room was like a prison, with iron rod doors. I wanted to do my sadhana, but I thought that maybe they might attack me while I sat. "Let me do Guru Puja (offering oneself to the Guru) first," I thought. "Then my sadhana won't get disturbed." So I did Guru Puja first then started to proceed

with my other lessons so that I could get up immediately and defend myself from their attack. I was doing deep sadhana without any fear, as I felt BABA would take care of me, when suddenly I heard some noises in my room. I got up from my sadhana to find four people entering my room with chains to tie me, knives to kill me and clothes to cover my mouth and gag me. Immediately I stood up from my sadhana and started shouting at them, which they did not expect.

"I know that you all have called me here just to kill me!" I proclaimed defiantly. "I came intentionally just to see how you were going to kill me. Before I die at least two of you will also die, be prepared for that! So! Those who want to die – come forward!" They were shaken. I said, "You invited me as a friend – does Prophet Mohammed say in the Koran that you should invite a person to your house as a friend and then kill him? I came here knowing all this to see how you were going to kill me and to see that you are going to get killed!" They panicked.

Only BABA gave me the courage to shout at them. I was not afraid of death but they were afraid of death. I knew that I would die only when BABA wanted me to die, so I was fearless by HIS grace. But they had fear of life, fear of death, fear of everything. Human beings control big animals like elephants, tigers, and lions. It's because animals have more fear than human beings. They had all the weapons to attack me with but they had fear. Now I had attacked them only with words and that was all it took to shake them. They were blocking my escape out of the room. I walked between them distracting them with words and ran to the other barracks and started to shout inviting all the other Military personnel to explain that these four persons invited me and were planning to kill me.

The military personnel came out of their barracks. As they were local and Muslims, they appeared silently support these four killers. I took the Chief of the military Dairy Farm aside and told him the whole story. I asked him, "Does prophet Mohammed condone in Koran a Muslim inviting a person to the house and then kill him?" "No," he replied. Then he told me that these four persons were a murderous gang who killed many foreigners and stole their passports and belongings. They declared to Pakistan Government that they killed a spy and received money from the Government also. He told me that he was not in position to arrest or punish them, but he would take me out of the danger.

He shouted at everyone standing around. Apparently they didn't like a foreigner like me in their barracks. This gave the military chief an excuse to get me out of there. "A foreigner doesn't belong here," he said. "I'll get his things and take him out of here right now." Then he went with me to the place where my luggage was kept. I packed all my things and then he escorted me to the railway station and put me on the train with security guards to accompany me until I boarded my flight to Sweden. BABA saved my life; otherwise I would have been killed in Pakistan. It was only by HIS grace that I was able to safely return to the training center.

> "HE is witnessing not only your physical or abstract expressions, above that; your controlling hub is also being witnessed by HIM. Not only is HE *all-pervasive* in the realm of physicality and mentality, but your Unit Cognition is also being controlled and witnessed by *HIM.*" – BABA

### 41. BABA FACES ANIMOSITY OF INDIRA GANDHI

The Central Bureau of Investigation (CBI) is the vigilant intelligence Police of India, responsible for the maintenances of security in the country. They need huge funds to watch over other organizations considered potential dangers. They projected Ananda Marga to be a big terrorist organisation in the eyes of the Indian Government so that they could get funds to supervise Ananda Marga all over the world. They were able to get enormous funds to travel abroad just to keep watch on Ananda Marga. They were stealing the public's money for themselves while they made others the scapegoats. They were living at the cost of the government. We wrote slogans all over the streets in India. Indira Gandhi, the Prime Minister of India was providing the CBI with whatever amounts of money they wanted because they were supporting her dictatorial behavior. They were also feeding her "information" that Ananda Marga was a threat to her life. In this way the CBI was able to extract more and more funds from Indira Gandhi.

Here is the part of a letter written by Indira Gandhi in reply to a Swedish sister.

Mr. Jaya Prakash Narayan was a famous leader of India and Mr. Abdul Bhukhari was a famous Muslim leader of India. Both of them openly came in full support of BABA. They condemned Indira Gandhi for BABA's arrest, BABA's poisoning and BABA's now four-year long fast. They helped to mobilize the masses against the declaration of Emergency in India.

Once BABA said, "Whosoever opposes Ananda Marga, prakrti (nature) will punish them in one of three ways: (1) one will lose their post, lose their respect, honor and prestige, or (2) one will become sick or (3) one will die."

A special murder squad was trained by the CBI as per instructions of the KGB (Russian Intelligence) to attack BABA in jail. The murder squad came to BABA's room in jail to attack HIM in the middle of the night as HE slept. The squad entered BABA's room but they didn't see BABA in HIS room at all – the cell was empty! They searched BABA's room and returned frustrated.

Another day a new squad was sent to attack BABA again. This time instead of BABA simply not being there, the squad saw a powerful, overwhelming light coming from BABA's room. It was unbearably bright; they returned back, frightened this time.

A third attempt was made. This time the squad entered BABA's room and to their surprise found that the ceiling of the cell had stretched up very high, much higher than it should have been. They looked up to the ceiling and saw BABA walking about upside down as he would if the ceiling were the floor. The scene was so dreadful that the murder squad just ran away from HIS cell for their lives.

The physical attempts on BABA's life were finally given up when the jail Superintendent himself was charged for murder when Dada Atulananda self-immolated in jail due to the tortures given to BABA in jail. All this, we came to know from the CBI source who was a devotee of BABA, who supported Ananda Marga secretly. Indira Gandhi was the worst sinner as she committed all of the below mentioned crimes against BABA.

> "Anyone who by the use of brute force wants to take possession of your property, abducts one's wife, comes with a weapon to murder you, wants to snatch away wealth, sets fire to your house or wants to take your life by administrating poison, is called Atatayii - the worst sinner." - BABA

## 42. UK PARLIAMENT, USA SENATORS FOR BABA

Many UK Members of Parliament and USA Senators and many other dignitaries wrote to Indira Gandhi, the Prime Minister of India, to stop the systematic persecution against BABA. Here are some letters written by prominent members of Parliaments of England, American Senators and others addressed to Indira Gandhi to consider for BABA'S release.

Niall MacDermont
Commission International of Jurists
July 24, 1974
Ref. 2/04/06

Dear Ambassador,

We have been in correspondence about the case of Mr. Prabhat R. SARKAR of Ananda Marga. You kindly wrote to me this subject on January 7 last.

We have recently received a copy of the Memorandum on this case prepared by the well-known British lawyer and Queen's Counsel. Mr. William Wells. It appears to us to be a very well written and balanced report, which brings out the difficulties in this case.

As appears from the report, it seems that an unfortunate deadlock situation has now been reached. In the hope that it may lead to a way out of the impasse, may I respectfully suggest that your Government should give careful and if possible, favorable consideration to the suggestion put forward by Mr. Wells at the end of his report.

Yours Sincerely,

Niall MacDermont
Secretary-General

From: Mrs. Jill Knight, M.B.E., M.P.

House of Commons
London, S.W.1.

29th July 1974

Mrs. Indira Gandhi,
Prime Minister of India
NEW DELHI,
India.

Dear Mrs. Gandhi,

As a woman Member of the British Parliament, conscious that women, who attain high office in any State do, by their action, enhance or despoil the reputation of the whole of the female sex everywhere, I make a direct appeal to you.

I have read the report made by Mr. William Wells, Q.C. on the case of Mr. P.R. Sarkar who has now been in Prison in India awaiting trial for 2 1/2 years. I believe from what Mr. Wells says that Mr. Sarkar is now gravely ill from the effects of his prolonged fast, protesting at his treatment in prison.

How you administer the internal affairs of India is of course not my business, nor would I seek to interfere in any with it.

Nevertheless, justice and mercy must be served by any country with a claim to greatness, and I submit that India's standing in the eyes of the world is harmed by this case and your own reputation cannot but be blackened if a man is kept imprisoned without trial for 2 1/2 years under your rule.

I would ask you to give this case your personal consideration.

Yours sincerely,

Jill Knight, M.B.E. M.P.

---

UNITED STATES SENATE
COMMITTEE ON FOREIGN RELATIONS
WASHINGTON, D.C. 20510

July 15, 1974

Honorable Triloki Nath Kaul, Ambassador
Embassy of India
2107 Massachusetts Avenue, N.W.
Washington, DC 20008

Dear Mr. Ambassador:

The situation regarding the imprisonment in New Delhi of Mr. Prabhat Ranjan Sarkar has come to my attention. Known as Shrii Shrii Anandamurtii by his followers of the Ananda Marga movement, Mr. Sarkar has begun a fast in protest of the treatment he has received during his two and one-half years of imprisonment.

While I do not wish to interfere in the internal matters of India, since many of Mr. Sarkar's followers are Americans, I would like to express my concern in his welfare. It is my hope that Mr. Sarkar is afforded the most fair and humane treatment possible and that his situation is given immediate and thoughtful attention.

With every good wish
I am Sincerely
George McGovern
Ambassador

From: The Rt. Hon. The Lord Gardiner, P.C.

July 16, 1974

Dear Mrs. Gandhi,

I was Lord Chancellor in the last Labour Government and I am a member of the International Commission of Jurists and therefore naturally interested in the rule of law throughout the world.

I have been considering the report made by Mr. William T. Wells Q.C. on the case of Mr. P.R. Sarkar (Shrii Shrii Anandamurti) who has now been in prison in India awaiting trial for 2 1/2 years and is, I understand, seriously ill from prolonged fast protesting at his treatment in prison.

I do not of course want to seem to interfere in any way in the internal affairs of your country but I venture to express the hope that on humanitarian grounds you may think fit to give this matter your personal attention.

Yours sincerely

Mrs. Indira Gandhi
Prime Minister of India
New Delhi
India

---

UNITED STATES SENATE
COMMITTEE ON APPROPRIATIONS
WASHINGTON, D.C. 20510

July 10, 1974

International Committee to Obtain Justice
For Shrii Shrii Anandamurti
1354 Montague St. N.W.
Washington, D.C. 20011

Dear Sirs:

I have written on behalf of Mr. P.R. Sarkar to the Secretary of State and urged that he do everything possible to contribute to a humanitarian resolution of the conflicts between the Indian State and the Ananda Marga movement.

I am hopeful that a just solution to this problem will be worked out between Mr. Sarkar and the Indian State.

My warm regards.

Sincerely,

William Proxmire,
U.S.S.

---

"The path of divine life is unknown to humans. Humans do not fear to tread a known path, but they always hesitate and fear to travel along an unknown path." - BABA

## 43. MORE SELF IMMOLATIONS PROTESTS

In spite of protest letters written by many important persons from all over the world, the Prime Minister of India, Mrs. Indira Gandhi paid no attention and refused to consider releasing BABA. Already three Indian Dadas had committed self-immolation. There was no response from the Indian Government. Seeing this, the trainees from Sweden decided to take the lead to protest for BABA'S release.

Six months after we opened up the whole-timer training center, the first batch of qualified Dadas and Didis (nuns) were given their postings. Every six months a batch of Dadas and Didis were posted all over the world. Within four years, more than one hundred countries were covered. In this way, BABA'S Mission started to spread all over the world, in a very short span of time. The Indian and Soviet governments were doing all they could to harm BABA'S Mission, banning the organization, attempting to kill BABA, but all their efforts boomeranged. Ananda Marga was in five countries and when HE was finally released, we were in one hundred and twenty.

As I mentioned earlier, in protest for BABA'S release, three Indian Dadas committed self-immolation. There were two German whole-timers who were in the first group of trainees in Sweden in 1975. They were Dada Lokesh and Didi Uma. They got posted to different countries and started to establish BABA'S Mission. Then after doing some work they met in India and saw BABA in such a condition in jail. BABA'S fasting had already entered its fourth year. They were pained to see BABA'S fasting, and were inspired to do something for BABA'S release. They decided to commit self-immolation outside India in protest of BABA'S persecution. They chose Berlin, Germany, both committing self-immolation in the main center in front of a church in Berlin on 4th February 1977. They were the fourth and fifth to commit self-immolation. They were great saints, sacrificing their lives for the ideology. They could have led a happy life, a comfortable life, but they wanted to arouse the public's awareness to the fact that BABA'S innocent life was in danger because of HIS prolonged fasting; let humanity be awakened to save BABA'S life, even at the cost of their own lives. Their sacrifice is historical. I knew them personally as I gave them training for six months. They were so great and pure, like innocent divine children: They were highly spiritual, fully established in the conception and ideation of the Supreme Consciousness. For such great saints, sacrificing their lives for BABA'S sake was great dedication.

We never encouraged people to end their lives. Still, an American worker, Dada Gagan, committed the sixth instance of self-immolation in Dallas, Texas. Didi Asitima's was the seventh self-immolation on the 6th of June 1978. Didi Shanti, an Australian, in front of the United Nations in Geneva, Switzerland, performed the eighth self-immolation.

All of these self-immolations, three by Indian Dadas and five by Western Dadas, Didis aroused a very high consciousness amongst the public, the intellectuals and the media all over the world. Everyone started to understand BABA'S persecution by the Communist-backed Indian and Russian governments.

> "No power in heaven or on earth can separate ME from MY children. Even if this universe comes to an end, I will be with them in the expressionless cosmos." - BABA

## 44. BABA'S DARSHAN IN FLIGHT

By the middle of 1977, I had already been in Sweden for three years finally they granted me Swedish citizenship. They even gave me a passport – my first regular national passport. Now that I had this; I thought I would be able to fly directly to India and have BABA's darshan. It was unbearable for me to remain away from BABA. So, right after I got my citizenship and passport I flew straight to Bombay, India from Stockholm via Brussels.

For the first time I was landing directly on Indian soil with Swedish citizenship and a Swedish passport. I thought I would be allowed to enter India as a Swedish citizen, but I was immediately arrested in Bombay because my name was blacklisted. I told the Indian police that I would not land in India and wanted to continue my flight with Sabena Airlines (the airline which brought me to India). The Indian police contacted Sabena Airlines (Belgium) and asked if they could take me back on their flight. They agreed to take me because they understood that the Indian Police was working hand in hand with the Russian Government. So they asked me to leave India and board their plane, which was going to Bangkok (Thailand). I had no plane ticket for Bangkok, but since the Sabena plane was going to Bangkok, they took me along on their flight anyway.

There were very few passengers in the plane. I decided during the flight that I would get off at Bangkok and then fly from there to Kathmandu and enter India by land. When the plane landed at Bangkok, Thailand, I went to Immigration to get a transit visa to enter Thailand, but the Indian Government already informed them that I was blacklisted in India and no country should permit me to enter. On these grounds, Thailand refused me entry. Again I contacted Sabena to take me back to Brussels and Stockholm but their flight was going to Manila (Philippines). So Sabena Airlines (Belgium) took me by their flight to Manila.

When I was refused entry to Thailand, I felt very sad that I could not travel to Nepal and India to see BABA. I was weeping on that flight to Manila. There were very few passengers on the plane. I was deeply thinking of BABA, thinking about how I couldn't have HIS darshan. There were tears in my eyes. In my section of the plane there was nobody.

Suddenly BABA appeared before me in the plane and consoled me, saying, "There is nothing to worry about, do not weep, I am always with you." Seeing BABA, I felt so happy. I had come to have BABA'S darshan and now I had gotten it. After a few minutes BABA disappeared. I was in such a blissful state; it was beyond words to explain. Our plane landed in Manila, Philippines.

I thought of entering Manila to see the margiis there. BABA twice visited Manila, so there were a good number of devotees there. I went to the immigration office to enter the Philippines, but the Immigration officials refused to give me entry under the Indian Government's instructions. Sabena again wanted me to travel with them to Tokyo (Japan). Tokyo was the final destination of the Sabena flight, and then it would finally return all the way whence it came, back to Manila, to Bangkok to Brussels and at last to Stockholm. By this time I had been on that plane for three days, and if I went with them to Tokyo then I would end up on there for more than one week continuously. So I told Sabena airlines to continue to Tokyo without me and I would board on the plane the next day on the return flight.

Next day I took the Sabena flight back to Stockholm, Sweden traveling another three days. Almost one week I was on that plane, bouncing out and about all across the globe. The only satisfaction I had was that I had received BABA's darshan on the plane. BABA's darshan was all I wanted, and it was all I needed – it was also all I got.

> "Everything in this universe is moving. The hours, human beings, stars, *planets* and nebula - all are on the move. Movement is a must for all; there is no scope for its cessation. The path of movement is not always smooth or strewn with *flowers nor* is it always beset with thorns or encumbered with violent clashes. According to the nature of the path human beings will have to prepare themselves and move courageously. In that movement itself lies the very essence of life." - BABA

## 45. BABA PROVED INNOCENT - COURT RELEASES

Mrs. Indira Gandhi was attacking BABA and Ananda Marga, but one who attacks Ananda Marga, BABA said once that the nature punishes one if one opposes Ananda Marga like losing post, honor, prestige or becoming sick etc. BABA doesn't punish anybody. It is nature, which takes its course as per the law of action and reaction. If one can't follow Ananda Marga, then at least one should not oppose Ananda Marga because Ananda Marga is Dharma – it has come only to serve humanity.

After Indira Gandhi kept BABA in jail for so many years she lost the elections; an election in which she rigged the polling, at that. Forced to resign as Prime Minister, she declared Emergency in 1975 and arrested all opposition leaders including the Ananda Margiis. In 1977 she ordered a re-election. The masses of India revolted against Indira Gandhi for imposing the Emergency and she was defeated in the election by a landslide vote. Even her son was defeated in the election. Her party was badly defeated. A new coalition Ministry came to power in India. They pretended to support us but in reality they didn't want to release BABA. They were playing a double game.

All over the world there were wide protests for BABA's release. Already nine persons sacrificed (eight self immolations and one died in jail) their lives in protest for BABA's release from jail.

The Australian margiis took an active role in exposing the Indian Government's conspiracy in attacking BABA. The staff working in Indian Embassies all over the world took a long leave or vacation to go back to India because they did not want to face the margiis' opposition. All this and more created a tremendous amount of pressure on the Indian Government.

Now that Indira Gandhi had lost power, BABA's cases were brought back in court. All the false, fabricated cases couldn't

stand. The Patna High Court finally quashed all the concocted cases framed against BABA in July 1978. All the cases against Ananda Margiis were dismissed and all the workers and margiis were released. BABA was released on 3rd August 1978. Finally, after five years, four months and two days, BABA broke HIS fast by taking coconut water offered to HIM by HIS elder sister. When BABA was fasting in jail, she requested BABA to give up fasting. BABA reminded her that she herself taught HIM not to break a promise that one makes (when BABA was a small boy). She had no reply. BABA appeased her by promising that when HE would break HIS fast then HE would break it with something offered to HIM by her first. Now BABA was keeping another promise. Ananda Marga, which was only in five countries at the time of HIS arrest had now spread to one hundred and twenty. By this HE had fulfilled yet another promise.

When BABA was released on 3rd August 1978, thousands of margiis and non-margiis gathered in front of the Patna jail gate to have HIS darshan and to celebrate BABA'S release. A big procession of several thousands of people accompanied BABA from jail to HIS Quarters. BABA then said, "You have heard of the victory of Dharma in the past, you are seeing the victory of Dharma in the present, and you will see the victory of Dharma in the future also."

> "Remember that victory does not come by itself. Victory has to be invited and welcomed with sweat, the warmth of blood and the flames of hard labour." - BABA

### 46. DHARMA WINS

Wherever there is Dharma, there is BABA, and where there is BABA, certainly there is victory. BABA remained in the government's custody in jail for seven long years. BABA alone fought with these insidious forces and defeated them. These evil forces tried to split the organization by banning HIS organization and by confiscating the properties of HIS organization. BABA continued to struggle with them all alone and proved to the world that Dharma always wins finally.

Once BABA said in jail, "I am alone in jail and now my property is only the dhoti (cloth wrapped around waist and legs) and kurta (shirt) I am wearing. I am only five-foot two inches, why are all these powerful administrative forces of the world afraid of ME? It is because of MY ideas."

BABA stands for Universal ideas. When Universal ideas are introduced, then naturally these ideas polarize the world between universal, service-minded, idea-led forces and dogmatic, narrow, selfish and exploitation motivated forces. Human beings are guided by sentiment, but if it goes against the collective interest and welfare, as it does in geo (pride of an area of land) or socio-sentiment (pride of a social group), then it hurts the universal fraternity and spirit of service and welfare of all beings.

Once BABA told a story about who really rules the world. A King once asked the Prime Minister who ruled the kingdom, expecting that he would reply that, 'The King is the ruler.' But the Minister said, "Women are ruling the kingdom." Kings love flattery, so he was disappointed with the reply of the Prime Minister.

The King again questioned the minister, "Are you sure?"

The Minister replied, "Yes, everyone follows women in the world."

Then the King said, "Let us put this question to the assembly and let us see what they say."

Then they both went to the Representative Assembly of their kingdom. Everybody got up as the King arrived. The King sat on his throne; everybody else occupied their seats. Then the King addressed the assembly: "The Prime Minister says that women are ruling the kingdom and everyone follows women in the world. I want your opinion. Those who follow women should occupy the seats on the left side of the assembly and those who do not follow women, sit on the right side of the assembly."

All the members occupied the seats on the left side of the assembly except one member who occupied the seat on the right side of the assembly. It was conceded that all follow women but one person indicated disagreement. The King thought, "Well, at least one person has saved my prestige." The King asked the person, "Now, you tell me what made you to come all alone to the right side." The King expected that he would reply that he followed only the King not the women.

The person replied, "My wife has advised me to avoid joining groups because mobs always start fights. She doesn't want me to get hurt, so she said I should stand apart in public." It proved he also followed women by sitting on the right.

BABA told this story and we all laughed.

To materialize the Lord's mission, both positive and negative forces need tremendous spiritual power, which one can get only from the Lord. The Lord has to prepare and strengthen both the forces. This is HIS LIILA!

There is a story in the Puranas (Mythology) about how demons were created. Lord Vishnu (purusa) was lying on his Sarpasheshanaga (snake) in an ocean of milk. Laxmi (Prakriti) was massaging HIS feet. They were in Vaikunta (spiritual abode) in a very high Spiritual world. One day, four great saints morphed into children and went to Vaikunta to have Vishnu's darshan. The two gatekeepers of Lord Vishnu stopped the entry of the children. The children requested them to allow them to enter but the gatekeepers refused, thinking that they were only children. Then these four children cursed them to be born as humans on Earth.

The gatekeepers were losing their lives. The four children, who went inside, had Vishnu's darshan and now were returning. They saw at the gate that both the gatekeepers were about to die. The gatekeepers requested them (Four saints) to withdraw their curse. They said to the gatekeepers, "We cannot withdraw the curse, but we can give you a choice - do you want to live seven human lives or three human lives? If you choose seven lives as a human then you will remain as devotee of Lord Vishnu but if you choose three lives as humans then you will be born as demons, as haters of the Lord to mobilize immoralists, to oppose Dharma - morality - the Lord Himself will kill you. Which one will you choose?"

Vishnu's gatekeepers chose three lives to be born as demons to oppose Dharma and to be killed by Lord Vishnu Himself. They chose three lives as demons instead of seven as devotees in order to return to the Lord as early as possible.

As per Hindu Mythology the two gatekeepers were born as demons. They mobilized all the dark, evil forces and were killed three times by Lord Vishnu. This is an illustrative story. BABA had to create HIS own opposition as well. Hence in the 1970's BABA's wife, the organizational Heads trained by BABA came together to oppose HIM. When Lord Krishna came to India 3500 years ago, HE had to fight against HIS own army HE trained HIMSELF, the Yadavas, during the Mahabharata war. Now in the twentieth century, as a result of these defections, evil forces like the Indian Government and the Russian Government were given the chance to persecute BABA and HIS Mission. All these evil forces are nothing but a representation of narrow sentiments, which naturally oppose Universal ideas when they arise.

This opposition is helpful in establishing our ideology. Once BABA said, "Opposition will establish the Mission." Then HE gave an example. BABA said, "The dogs have their local area. If some new dog enters the area, all the dogs combine to attack and drive the dog away. There was one dog traveling from Calcutta to Delhi, a long journey that would take a hundred and fifty days - five months to travel with a normal speed of ten

kilometers everyday. The dog traveling from Kolkata to Delhi had to run faster to save itself from the opposition of local area dogs all along the route, and because of that it took one tenth of the time - fifteen days - to reach its destination."

When Ananda Marga was constantly under attack it had to move fast and work harder for its establishment. Opposition will establish Ananda Marga, just as BABA said, but it should be remembered that it is BABA alone who is also controlling our very opposition. All is the play of BABA. BABA has proved to the world that eventually, Morality, Universalism and Dharma shall win. BABA is the Supreme Master, the controller of all forces. Let us depend on BABA and Materialize HIS Mission unitedly.

"Where there is Dharma there is Is't'a and where there is Is't'a there is victory." - BABA

# PART - VII

# BABA's WORLD TOUR

## 47. MEETING BABA IN MUMBAI

After BABA's release on 3^rd August 1978, BABA slowly started to recover from the long years of fasting. A few months later, BABA resumed touring. BABA had to lead a very hectic program in order to spread HIS Mission.

On 6th September 1978, BABA taught the Kaoshiki dance to one Dada who in turn demonstrated Kaoshiki for the first time. BABA was also present during the Kaoshiki presentation.

Later on BABA said, "Kaoshiki dance is good for all but especially good for women to prevent and cure many chronic diseases." Lord Shiva invented Tandava for men. Tandava is not for women as they may also develop masculine qualities such as facial hair, etc. So BABA invented Kaoshiki dance especially for sisters, so that they may get the benefit of Tandava.

BABA gave Dharma Maha Cakra in various parts of India. I particularly wanted to attend the DMC HE was going to conduct

in Bombay by the end of 1978. I landed in Bombay, India without any hassle. In India we must wear orange but I was wearing civil dress so I could see BABA. I would be in trouble again if the CBI came to know of my identity.

On the second day I met BABA personally in HIS room. BABA told me about my past lives. HE told me that I had been with HIM in many past lives and was very close to HIM. This revelation explained to me why I was constantly being pulled to HIM even in spite of adverse circumstances. I left my Swedish training center several times only to see BABA. I wanted to stay with HIM rather than return to Sweden. I began to seriously consider the possibility of staying in India.

The next day, I sat with BABA HIS car to go for field walk. Suddenly, BABA asked the driver to stop the car. BABA was angry with me. "Why are you here in India?!" HE scolded me. Then HE had me get out from the car. Later BABA called me, the General Secretary, and my Departmental Head and demanded an explanation - "Why have you left training center, why did you come here?" and allotted punishment to me.

For two days, BABA was very nice to me and as a result I thought of giving up my Sweden training center posting to remain with BABA. But BABA ordered me that I take the first available flight back to Sweden. General Secretary Dada and my departmental Head Dada were instructed to arrange for my journey back to Sweden to continue to impart training.

Before I left Bombay, I arranged one-fourth of the amount needed to buy the land we needed in Tiljala, Calcutta, in West Bengal. BABA gave instructions to buy our own land in Calcutta to start a Global Central Office Finance Secretary Dada Shradhananda Avadhuta (who is now Purodha Pramukha, the Head of Ananda Marga) told me that we needed a big amount to buy the land. Half of the amount was arranged by the Bombay margiis, one fourth of it the Central Office was having and the rest of the one-fourth amount was to be mobilized by me. So I cancelled my airplane ticket and got the cash and arranged the one-fourth amount needed to buy the Tiljala land in Calcutta for our Central Office.

I was happy that BABA gave me the opportunity to contribute to the Tiljala Central Office during my trip to India. The next day the entire amount needed to buy the land in Tiljala was arranged. I bought a plane ticket for General Secretary Dada to fly from Bombay to Calcutta to finalise the purchase of land. This was how the new Central Office started in Calcutta.

In the beginning of 1979, I came to know that there was to be a Dharma Maha Cakra in the Western part of India. Again I wanted to see BABA. It was very difficult for me to be away from HIM. The trainees advised me not to visit India, as I might be arrested in India because of my visa problem. I did not heed their advice, and flew to India to attend DMC, as I had not gone for a pretty long time.

Sure enough, I was arrested in India. Dada Dhruvananda was posted to the Sweden training center in my place. I was released only by the intervention of Swedish authorities and was eventually sent back to Europe. Fortunately enough, right when I came back to Europe, BABA's European tour was just getting underway. This way I was able to attend DMC in Fiesch, Switzerland, etc.

> "From place to place, from age to age, the elixir of immortality has been flowing forever. Through the medium of human structure this message of immortality is transmitted onwards. So no human beings should ever belittle or slight themselves, rather they should make rigorous efforts to become worthy mediums (media). The wise and the intelligent ideate less on their defects and demerits and more on the Supreme desideratum." - BABA

## 48. BABA VISITS THE ALP IN SWITZERLAND

One Swiss margii (a devotee) arranged a Swiss visa for BABA. The margii and the President of Switzerland hailed from the same village and happened to be good friends, and thanks to that relationship, the President of Switzerland himself instructed the Swiss Embassy to grant BABA along with HIS entourage of Dadas and Didis all visas. Though it was the weekend and not their policy, the embassy issued the visas.

The flight bookings were done all at once directly to Geneva, Switzerland from Delhi. Earlier, the program was that BABA would come to the United Kingdom first and only later on proceed to Switzerland. BABA had different plans – HE didn't want to even put HIS foot on British soil, even on transit. The United Kingdom exploited the whole world for hundreds of years. The United Kingdom in alliance with the United States of America is still dominating many countries, exploiting in the name of democracy millions of people all over the world instead of guiding for their prosperity which needs real sacrifice from advanced nations to work for the development of the poor nations.

Switzerland and its President extended all their help and cooperation to make BABA's tour successful. Amongst all the Governments of world, there was polarization, some supporting BABA and some trying to oppose BABA's programs.

BABA's Swiss program was arranged in the Alps Mountains in a tourist village, Fiesch, a two hours drive from Geneva. The entire program was arranged by this margii who was the friend of the President of Switzerland. Information was sent to all the margiis in Europe. At that time I was posted as Chief Secretary for Hong Kong Sector. I was busy making all the necessary arrangements to travel to the Far East when I heard that BABA had obtained a Swiss Visa and would be landing in Geneva Airport; by the time I found out, I had hardly eight hours to reach there.

It was on the 27th of April, 1979, that I had to arrange a transport to Switzerland. I approached a car company. By BABA's grace, the company needed one of their new cars to be driven to Switzerland. They bore the cost and gave extra money for the journey and return; it goes without saying that I enthusiastically agreed with the terms. Still, things were going to be tight – we were four persons going to Switzerland, about a thousand kilometers away, and I was the only driver. I only stopped once. By the grace of BABA, I could drive at a good speed and was able to reach Geneva Airport just as BABA's plane landed. I parked the car and sped to the visitor's gallery to have BABA's darshan.

BABA came out from the airplane and was taken by the airport bus to the terminal. In the airport terminal we could see BABA entering immigration check. BABA was not stopped and was taken respectfully to the luggage collection hall. We could see BABA speaking to Central Dadas, Central Didis and margiis from India who had accompanied HIM.

BABA with HIS team of workers arrived at a special VIP lounge where arrangements were made for BABA to sit for a while. BABA came and sat on the sofa specially decorated for HIM. Many devotees garlanded BABA, while others displayed Kaoshiki and Tandava dances. Special dress was prescribed for performing these dances, but the dancers were not wearing this special dress, as there was some difficulty in arranging them at such a short notice. BABA said, "Speed without system is dangerous and system without speed is meaningless." BABA wanted that we should follow the proper system laid down by HIM and then move at a rapid speed towards perfection. After the grand reception at the airport, we traveled by our cars and followed BABA to the tourist village of Fiesch in the heights of the Alps.

A ten-day program was held in Fiesch, and it could not have been a more beautiful spot to have it. The ice-capped mountain peaks and ancient pine forests only heightened the special glow of BABA's presence. It was truly a glorious time.

BABA enjoyed too, going up into the mountains by cable car during HIS field walks. On these walks BABA told the devotees some very interesting things that are now popular amongst the margiis. BABA said that LORD SADASHIVA visited Fiesch, Switzerland about 7000 years ago on HIS yak. BABA also said that once upon a time these mountains were the highest in the world. The Alps Mountains are 300 million years old, whereas the Himalayas are only 30 million years old. Himalayas came into existence due to the collision of two tectonic plates relatively recently; hence the Himalayas are still growing every year. BABA further said, "Life cells migrated to this earth from planet Mars. Now Mars is a dead planet. Life cells traveled from planet Mars and settled on the peaks of these Alps Mountains. Planet earth gradually cooled down and became congenial for life cells to grow. If one studies the archeology here one can trace the evolution of living cells and the development of different species of plants and animals and humans as well on these Mountains."

Every morning and evening BABA gave general Darshan in a very big hall. Margiis from all over Europe, the USA and other parts of world assembled to enjoy BABA's pravacans (discourses) and blissful Darshans. BABA gave emphasis on Kiirtan, singing and Kaoshiki and Tandava dances. BABA explained how these dances could help the development of body and mind.

On one particular occasion, BABA said, "LORD SHIVA invented the Tandava dance 7000 years ago. At that time Tandava was performed holding snakes in the left hand, which symbolised death, even a skull or a fire (Masal). The right hand holds a trishul (a trident) or dagger or sword that symbolises life.

When BABA mentioned about the snakes, some of the margiis were inspired to perform Tandava in front of BABA holding snakes as was done during the time of Lord Shiva. To this end, some margiis went out in groups to look for snakes in the forest. It took some time, but at last one margii caught a snake and kept it in his bag. When BABA came for general darshan then the margii brother did Tandava holding the snake in his hand. The margiis were surprised to see such a thing but BABA just smiled gracefully.

All throughout the ten-day program, BABA NAM KEVALAM Kiirtan was continuously sung. On the final day, BABA's birthday (Ananda Purnima), Dharma Maha Cakra was held. On the night of DMC, around one hundred margiis were doing the Tandava dance, holding burning torches in their left hands. The hall was fire proof - even the slightest smoke could irritate the sensors and then automatically water would start pouring down and fill the whole hall. Still the margiis went on with their torches.

The organisers of the Fiesch village came running to us when they saw flames in the hall. They thought that the hall would be filled with water to extinguish the fire by the time they got there, but to their surprise, no water had come from the sensors. They checked the fire fighting system, but everything was in order. What the organisers did not know was that the controller of the whole universe was sitting right in the hall, watching everything. Nothing could happen without HIS wish. Everyone enjoyed the Dharma Maha Cakra in Fiesch, Switzerland. BABA was extremely happy for all these ten days in the Alps mountains.

> "Devotees do not consider pa'pa (sin) and pun'ya (virtue). They only know the Lord.
>
> And every one should remember this fact." - BABA

## 49. FIELD WALK WITH BABA IN MAINZ, GERMANY

Switzerland opened the gateway for BABA to enter Europe. After such a blissful Dharma Maha Cakra held in the Alps, in Switzerland, BABA toured the countries HE got permission or visa to enter. Germany was one of them. BABA liked Germany very much. HE said that Germans are the most intellectually developed race of this planet earth. He said that the word Germany came from the Sanskrit word "shraman," which means an intellectually and spiritually developed person. BABA visited many cities in Germany.

While BABA was touring Germany, I accompanied HIM. I was also blessed with the opportunity of joining HIM on HIS daily field walks. Every morning and evening wherever BABA was, HE had the routine to walk regularly; these were HIS most interesting field walks. Dadas, margiis, even Didis and sisters were allowed to walk with BABA, although only in the West. BABA always pays attention to the local customs and traditions. Even regarding food, BABA preferred to eat only the local dishes that devotees served HIM with great devotion. In every country, each city is famous for certain special types of food; devotees would serve BABA the special dishes of that area. BABA appreciated this and preferred doing things in that fashion.

While walking with BABA on a field walk along the River Rhine near Mainz, BABA mentioned to me that HE was interested in teaching a special science; just by looking at the face, one could understand everything about a person — what one thinks, what one does etc. Then BABA mentioned that HE was not finding a suitable candidate to whom HE could teach such a science. When BABA said this to me, I understood that it is difficult for human beings to tolerate the bad or the negative sides of a person. All humans are moving from imperfection to perfection, but it would be difficult for an ordinary person to know all the bad aspects of a person and still be able to view them properly for all their virtues as well. In spite of knowing all the bad about a person, one should try to guide that person to move along the spiritual path with the hope that one day he or she would certainly become a good person. BABA knew about everybody's merits and demerits. BABA loved all and gave an opportunity to one and all to become a good person. Balance of mind is needed to guide and love in such an unconditional way. BABA is the only real example that one should follow in this Human Society.

In Germany BABA visited Timmern, our Global local full timer-training center, about thirty km from Braunschweig. BABA enjoyed four days of stay there and even initiated some Dadas into Kapalika Sadhana. BABA once said, "Wherever TARAKA BRAHMA stays even for a day, that place becomes a Tantra Piitha." Tantra Piitha means a place that has absorbed the high spiritual vibrations from Taraka Brahma or from a Great soul who attained liberation in that place after intense meditation. Whosoever even sits in such a place and meditates there can attain a very high spiritual state, as the place also helps the spiritual aspirant. Timmern is such a Tantra Piitha and is the only place in the world outside India where BABA stayed in our own building. Hence this place is a most vibrated place. Whenever one gets the time, one should try to go to Timmern and meditate there to feel the vibrations of a genuine Tantra Piitha. While HE was there, BABA said that HE had sent very special blessings, which went high into the space, and then would pour down on Germany to vibrate all people, places, and plants.

BABA also visited Berlin. During one of HIS field walks, HE was taken to the Berlin Wall, which was separating East Berlin (controlled by Russia, the Communists) and West Berlin (controlled by America, the Capitalists). When BABA saw the Wall HE said, "Why is there a wall standing between two groups of people of the same background?" BABA raised HIS stick and pointed it at the wall. "One day," BABA said prophetically, "the people themselves will break down this wall, and the two German

groups that this wall divides will become one." A short ten years later, BABA's words came true. At the time, in 1979, such a thing was unforeseeable, but nonetheless, the wall came crashing down, just as BABA said. Whatever BABA says or plans in HIS Mind, Prakriti or Nature, being HIS servant, must materialize it. In the near future HIS ideology is sure to get established. All humans for certain will become one Human Society.

> "The fact that the fortune of every individual, not only of this earth but the entire cosmos, has been wreathed together will have to be admitted, one day by people. The spiritual aspirant has to hasten that auspicious movement by pauseless effort, service and propagation of the Great Ideology. This alone is the Supreme task for the present humanity." - BABA

### 50. BABA'S ENTRY INTO ITALY REFUSED

BABA toured Holland and Sweden as well. In Sweden BABA gave personal contact to all the whole-timer trainees. Many European Governments welcomed BABA's visit in their countries by granting HIM a visa. The Indian Government under the influence of Communists advised countries across the world not to entertain Ananda Marga and BABA, but still, no country objected to BABA's visit except England, America and Italy.

England and America refused giving BABA a visa because they were afraid that BABA might capture the intellect of America and England; that the Americans and the British might revolt against Capitalism and establish PROUT. Once, while BABA was in jail, HE said that if HE were to deliver three lectures in America, then all the intellectuals of America would surrender to HIM.

At first, Italy granted BABA a visa. It was only afterwards that the Italian government refused HIS entry due to intense pressure from an established Institution, who was also afraid of BABA. They thought that BABA might influence the Italians with HIS tour there.

There were several hundreds of margiis waiting at the Milano Airport, Italy to receive BABA. Grand arrangements and programs were made for BABA's visit there. Some of the top Christian Missionaries became Ananda Margiis and started to do intense propagation about BABA's visit to Italy and were sincerely working for the spread of Ananda Marga there in the region. The spread of Ananda Marga in Italy disturbed the Italian Religious Institutions. They felt that they might lose ground if Ananda Marga continued to spread as it was doing. Hence they opposed BABA's entry.

BABA, since HE at first had an Italian visa, was able to clear Immigration along with HIS entourage. Then, just in the nick of the time a special officer came and collected all of their passports,

including BABA's, and then cancelled their Italian visa. All the hundreds of margiis assembled in Milano all were disappointed and deprived of BABA's darshan. All understood that it was their institutions who put pressure on Italian Immigration to refuse BABA's visa and to stop the entry of BABA and others with HIM.

BABA wanted that everyone should think of GOD as the Father and themselves as the children; all are the Divine sons and daughters of the Supreme Father. BABA discouraged the idea the church and priests were imposing, that all humans are sinners and sinners only. BABA said in HIS discourses that if one thinks of oneself as a sinner then one really would become a sinner. Rather, if one thinks that one is a Divine child of that Supreme Father then one will really become pure. Even if one is a sinner, there is still no need to remind GOD that one is a sinner. HE already knows everything. Ananda Marga is better in philosophy than the Hindus, better in social service than the Christians, better than the Muslims in social equality, better than the Jains in asceticism and better than the Communists in mobility. Only those who thrive on the exploitation of others were afraid of BABA and HIS ideology, and that's why they opposed HIM.

When Italy refused BABA's entry, BABA and others, they came to Lyon, France instead. I was in Paris, France trying to make arrangements to proceed to Tokyo, Japan for my new posting as Chief Secretary in Hong Kong Sector (Far East Asian countries) when I received the news that BABA was not allowed to enter Italy and that now HE was on HIS way to Lyon, France. I immediately rushed to Lyon to arrange BABA's reception at the airport. The French Government extended all their cooperation to BABA. All were happy to receive BABA. I came to know that Sweden, France and Germany were always supporting Ananda Marga whenever a debate on Ananda Marga was discussed amongst themselves in their All European security meetings and conferences.

"Much work has been done by not entering Italy," is what BABA said when HIS entry was refused. Standing there disappointed in the airport, all the Italian margiis, understanding very well that it was the dogmatic institution behind these doings, became even more strongly determined to establish BABA's Universal Ideology there in Italy as well as in Europe. BABA always knows what is best finally.

> "The entire universe has to be brought within the realm of your mind and made whole-heartedly your own. This indeed is the way is the way to benediction, the way to all round prosperity and success. This is the only path of yours." - BABA

## 51. PERSONAL TALK WITH BABA IN LYON, FRANCE

After arriving in Lyon, France, BABA was taken to a hotel for HIS stay, but BABA refused to enter. HE preferred to stay in a jagriti with devotees, so HE expressed HIS wish.

"But BABA," they protested, "there's no attached toilet there."

"I would rather be in the company of spiritualists than in a hotel with luxuries and comforts," HE said.

At the jagriti there in Lyon there was one room with an attached bathroom, but it had no toilet; a chair toilet had to be installed. Finally we invited BABA to stay in our jagriti. In spite of the poor living conditions compared to a hotel, BABA enjoyed staying at the jagriti and spent the last three days of HIS memorable European tour intimately with the devotees before leaving for India.

I had an intense desire to see BABA alone. BABA knows everything - HE told HIS PA that HE wanted to see me, so I was called to HIS room. I was alone with BABA for a long time; it goes without saying that I was happy to be with HIM once again. BABA told me many things. BABA said, "In Europe people have comforts, facilities and they lead luxurious lives, yet most of them feel lonely and suffer from mental problems. The only way to overcome all these troubles is by surrendering to the Supreme, by taking shelter in HIM; by cultivating HIS Company, mental problems are solved." BABA advised me to teach Kaoshiki and Tandava dances and BABA NAM KEVALAM music to the Europeans, as they appreciate singing and dancing as part of their culture. I told BABA that I would be leaving for Tokyo, Japan; Taiwan, Formosa China. BABA was happy and said that HE would soon visit them. This was good news. I did Sastang Pranam to BABA and left the room. BABA had set all HIS programs where and when HE should be. Once HE said that HE was moving as per HIS plan and still HE is a few months ahead of HIS schedule.

> "Marching ahead from Cimmerian darkness to the stratum divine is the true spirit of life. Staticism, superstition and narrowness stand for Cimmerian darkness. Ensconcing oneself in the cosmic soul is the life divine. Striving ahead, together with the entire universe, along the path of spirituality is verily the greatest task for humanity" - BABA

## 52. BABA A STATE GUEST IN TAIWAN

I flew to Japan from France and there I started doing pracar (propagation of Ananda Marga) when I heard the good news that BABA was coming to Taipei, Taiwan on a fifteen-day program. I immediately flew to Taipei to arrange BABA's reception. It was in August of 1979 that BABA came to Taipei, Taiwan. Rather than refusing HIS entry like Italy, BABA was given a State Guest reception and honour, with a red carpet rolled out and a 21-gun salute in Taipei, capital city of Taiwan. The whole city was decorated for BABA's visit with Taiwanese and Indian flags hung all about everywhere.

BABA was fighting with the Russian Communists; BABA was harassed and poisoned in jail. Having defeated the Communists in court, BABA was a hero now for all those who also were fighting against the Communists—like Taiwan, fighting against Mainland China. The whole reception was raised to the level of a reception given to a President or a Prime Minister of a country. A special VIP room was arranged for BABA so HE could meet all the margiis. The President of a Yoga association read a long poem praising BABA as the LORD who could help Taiwan get back Mainland China (Peking China). After reading the Chinese poem, he, along with several government officials presented it to BABA. It was written in long calligraphy, which if hanged on the wall rolled down from the ceiling to the ground. It was so long.

After the reception at the airport and VIP room, the Taiwanese officials wanted to take BABA to a specially arranged house for HIS stay. But BABA declined the offer, thanking them for all their kind hospitality and receptions. BABA told them that HIS friends were eagerly waiting for HIM to give a talk. The friend about whom BABA was speaking of was an Italian margii, a manager of intercontinental hotels. He had a huge house that was able to accommodate BABA and HIS entourage of Dadas and Didis. BABA always preferred to stay with HIS devotees rather than in some luxurious place, no matter who was offering. In this way we were able to intimately enjoy BABA's darshans for all the fifteen days.

> "After millions of animal lives, a created being attains human form. That is why all the sacred books speak of the rarity of human life. The wise properly utilize all objects and this utilization alone makes the existence of the object worthy.
>
> You have achieved human frame, you must make it meaningful by your *sa'dhana'*, service and sacrifice. Engage in such useful pursuits that even your worst enemies have hardly any chance to despise you. Utilize yourself in such a manner so as to satisfy your self mentally, that you never wasted your time uselessly on this earth." - BABA

## 53. ANANDA MARGA IN GLOBAL PARLIAMENT

Every morning and evening, BABA was giving general darshan to the Taipei margiis in a University hall. BABA spoke mostly about Lord Buddha and explained Lord Buddha's eight fold moral principles. Margiis enjoyed listening to BABA's discourses. BABA said, "India and China had very ancient civilizations on this planet earth." These two countries had similar life styles and understanding in the family.

Lord Shiva lived in the Himalayas. From there HE gave spiritual teachings that became Tantra in India when it spread south of the Himalayas. That same Tantra, when spread to the northern side of the Himalayas, became Taoism. In Taoism it was explained that Tao was everything, while in Tantra, Brahma-infinity - was everything. Tantra and Taoism are of one and the same conception but with different ideas, both having originated from Lord Shiva 7000 years ago.

Lord Shiva taught process of meditation, which was called Dhyan in India. That same word Dhyan in India became Chan in China, Chen in Korea, and Zen in Japan. Dhyan, Chan, Chen and Zen were the same word, only pronounced differently in different countries due to varying climatic conditions; so the tunes became different. It was a great revelation to the Chinese margiis to know that their ancient culture and civilization was actually coming from Lord Shiva. About Lord Buddha's teachings also, BABA explained in the true spirit of the terms what Lord Buddha really meant in his discourses. BABA's pravacans opened the eyes of the margiis in Taiwan. They all felt that India and China are one and the same in many aspects. Having heard HIS pravacans (discourses) their faith and reverence for BABA grew.

At the time I was posted as the Chief Secretary - a global socio-economico-political organization - of Hong Kong Sector (Far Eastern countries). Due to this, I was given a special duty from BABA. HE asked me to contact the concerned officials of Taiwan to get a special status for Ananda Marga to represent India in the Anti-Communist Global Parliament regularly held in Taipei, Taiwan. BABA asked me to report my progress to HIM every hour, commencing from 10.00 AM to 10.00 PM.

I attended morning and evening General Darshans but spent the rest of the days rushing to contact different Taiwanese officials to get recognition for Ananda Marga to represent India in this Anti-Communist Global Parliament. I contacted many officials, ministers and even important public figures to get Ananda Marga the spot as the representative of India in the International Forum. After one week of lobbying, the Government of Taiwan gave me an official letter asking Ananda Marga to represent India in their Anti-Communist Global Parliament. I showed the letter to BABA. HE was happy. I knew that it was only the special grace of BABA that I could do this. Only HE arranges everything, but still BABA always gave the credit to HIS children. Because of this special grace, every year Ananda Marga was representing India in the Anti-Communist Global Parliament there in Taipei, Taiwan. BABA does HIS work through different media.

> "Just as the advent of the purple dawn is inevitable at the end of the Cimmerian darkness of the inter-lunar night, exactly in the same way I know that a gloriously brilliant chapter will also come after the endless reproach and humiliation of the neglected humanity of today.
>
> Those who love humanity and those who desire the welfare of living beings should be vigorously active from this very moment, after shaking off all lethargy and sloth, so that the most auspicious hour arrives at the earliest." - BABA

### 54. TAIWAN - RICH IN OIL RESOURCES - BABA

One day the lift that was supposed to carry BABA to the top of the darshan hall failed. We had no time to check how to repair the lift, so we asked BABA to sit in a chair; we workers carried BABA sitting in the chair to the top floor where BABA gave HIS discourse. BABA just sat and smiled quietly as we carried HIM about like a king. I still have a photograph of the Dadas and myself carrying BABA on HIS chair - it is one of my very special treasures.

Taiwan is beautiful country decorated by the nature with lots of fruit trees and gardens. BABA liked the climate and the vegetation there very much. One day I was with BABA on a field walk.
