First we went in a car then stopped as per the direction of a margii near a forest. Then we started to walk into the forest. We already had been walking for a long distance when a folding chair was put on the ground for BABA to sit. BABA sat and took some fruit juice offered to HIM. Meanwhile, BABA talked about the different plants in the area and their various medicinal uses.

BABA said that Taiwan has lot of oil resources and that if these oil resources were tapped then Taiwan would become very rich. BABA asked us for a map of Taiwan to show us the location of the oil resources. In the forest it was difficult for us to fetch a map of Taiwan, so we missed the opportunity. Maybe in the future when PROUT is established in Taiwan, maybe then they will be able to find these hidden resources as hinted at by BABA and utilize them to enrich the country and make it a powerful presence of the world. BABA had a special love for Taiwan, and HE gave HIS appropriate blessings. I'm sure it is only a matter of time before the oil is found.

> "Encourage everyone to build their career in a nice way. Let none get the opportunity to think that their life has become useless." - BABA

---

### 55. BABA REVEALS RELATIONSHIP OF MANY LIVES

BABA completed HIS fifteen days Darshan by conducting DMC. That night BABA gave Varabhaya Mudra and also gave another special mudra that I was seeing only for the first time. Now the program was finished and BABA was soon to leave. BABA's departures were always unbearable. Now, just before leaving, with only a few Dadas, Didis and margiis, BABA gave us some very special blessings.

BABA told us that there is a cause and effect process working behind each and every happening of this Universe. Whatever was happening was the effect of a cause unknown to us. "There are millions of people all over the world," BABA said. "Why have only a few come in contact with ME? Why are there only a few with ME here in this room? This too is the effect of a cause, but you do not know the cause. But it might have been that on some other planet we all decided that we would travel to this planet earth after finishing the work on that planet. That was the cause, and this is the effect." BABA's departing words were so touching, so deep. BABA made us realize that our relationship with HIM and HIS Mission was not from this present, one lifetime but was continuing on from so many innumerable previous lives. Only HE knows how many lifetimes I have spent with HIM, working for HIM and HIS Divine Mission. Why I was blessed with this, I suppose, is also the effect of another unknown cause, known only to HIM. The same is the case with everyone. BABA is the only our Father in all our lives.

> "Clouds cannot overcast the sun for a long time. The creatures of darkness never want the expansive exaltation of human society. Even then, human beings shall march ahead. No one can arrest the speed of their progress. You must be the harbingers; you must be the pioneers of this victorious march. See that not a single individual lags behind." - BABA

## 56. ONE PRINTING PRESS = 100 WORKERS - BABA

When BABA was in Taipei, BABA said that I should start a Newspaper for spreading PROUT theory with the help of the Taiwanese margiis. Hearing this, I immediately arranged a PROUT Newspaper to be printed in the local Chinese language. BABA insisted that I should buy one printing press. Of course buying a press was a big task, so I was given two months time to materialize the target. BABA said, "One printing press is equal to one hundred workers."

Having a printing press, we could print the literature of Ananda Marga and its vibrant activities and circulate them to thousands of people. Immoralists and the people who are exploiting the common man are afraid of the high standard of morality existing in Ananda Marga. They are afraid because the margiis might (and often did) expose their immoral and exploitative activities.

In India the Government supplies the newspapers with the necessary printing ink and color. Thus, if any newspaper writes against the Government, then the Government is able to withdraw the printing ink thereby financially crippling the press. This is nothing but a blatant system of control. Whatever the government doesn't like, the government can punish accordingly. This especially applies to the leading, more largely circulated newspapers. However, with a small press, requiring very little printing color, it can be easily managed. The government cannot control small printing presses. Hence the small printing press can write all the facts and details to expose all the corrupt immoralists that are in power and position, exploiting the common man. Hence one printing press does the work of one hundred workers. After BABA leaving Taiwan only HIS thought was in my mind.

Once again, I had a strong desire to see BABA. It goes without saying by now that it was very difficult for me to remain away from BABA. So I sent a message to India that I would be coming to India. The message was given to BABA. BABA sent me the message that I should go to Korea instead and start doing pracar there. But my mind was so much possessed by thoughts of BABA that it was difficult for me to think of anything else other than HIM. Instead of going to Korea, I flew to India and met BABA. HE was very happy to see me again.

> "Literature is that which moves together with the society, which leads society towards the true *fulfillment* and welfare by *providing* the inspiration for *service.*" - BABA

## 57. GETTING FLOWERS FROM BABA

I spent many good days with BABA. There was reporting held regularly in presence of BABA every month. I thought it would be better if I left for Sweden right after reporting. I would return one month later for the next monthly reporting, I would have enough funds and there would be no problem. Still, I thought in my mind to offer a flower to BABA. If BABA returned the flower, then it would be a green signal for me to go to Sweden. If BABA did not return the flower to me then it was not a good idea to go to Sweden. With this decision in my mind I bought a beautiful flower for BABA.

I met BABA'S Personal Assistant and told him that I would like to offer BABA a flower when HE went out for HIS field walk. Dada advised me to stand near the window of the car. Just as BABA was about to enter the car from HIS quarters, PA Dada informed BABA that I wanted to offer flowers to HIM. BABA was happy and accepted the flowers.

Usually when flowers or garlands were given to BABA, after HIS acceptance they were given to PA Dada who kept them for some time in BABA's room. This time BABA unusually asked PA whether HE should return them to me. PA Dada was not aware of the planning I had behind these flowers so he said, "Yes, BABA! You can give him the flowers; he is still standing and waiting near the window." BABA blessed the flowers and gave them back to me. "Keep these carefully," HE said. I took this to be as good as if HE directly gave me the permission to go. It was my green signal. So I made a plan to proceed to Sweden. BABA knows everything what we think in our mind.

> "Those who can dedicate their all to the thought of the Great and the inspiration of the Supreme are verily the most heroic. Indeed, they are the virtuous, and they alone are capable of taking human history from darkness to light." - BABA

## 58. BABA’S BLESSINGS IN SWEDEN

If I had to go to Sweden then I had to go to Nepal and fly from there – and that was quite expensive. I had very little money. I could hardly afford to buy even a one-way ticket. Still, I thought that by the time I had to return I would have already gotten the money from Sweden, so I spent all the money I had to buy a one-way ticket from Nepal to Copenhagen, Denmark. There was no direct flight to Sweden. I planned to go to Sweden from Denmark.

I went to the North of Sweden to meet the brother who had promised to give me some money after selling his house. To my astonishment, he told me that he was not finding any buyer to sell his house. He had been trying for several months, but nobody was showing any interest. The economic condition in the north of Sweden was quite poor compared to south of Sweden, which was wealthier comparatively.

Several days passed, and I just waited there with the hope that he would find a buyer, but no one came and no one showed any interest. Now I had no money to return to India. If I did not present myself before BABA within a month, I would be allotted some punishment from the organisation. Since I had no money, I had no other choice but to just simply wait and hope to somehow get the money. It took me two months and so many different ways of fund raising, but finally I raised the money to return to India. When I got the sufficient funds, I called the Ananda Marga Central Office at Ananda Nagar to contact my Acaraya Asiimananda. The Central Office told me that Dada Asiimananda was out touring Europe. I traced him down and actually found out that he would be coming to Sweden. I made my plan to meet him in Sweden and then I would travel with him back to India.

The first thing I did when I was able to meet with Dada was I explained to him why I had left India (but not the flower incident with BABA).

"What have you been doing all these past two months?" He asked me. I told him that due to shortage of money I was stuck in Sweden, but now I could go with him to India. He was pleased that at least I was going to return to BABA, and we both traveled back to India together by Aeroflot.

During the time I spent in Sweden I enjoyed BABA's blessings. Every morning from 5 AM to 8 AM I did kiirtan and meditated afterwards for two to three hours at a stretch. After breakfast and after reading some of BABA's books I would sit and meditate again. In every meditation and in every kiirtan I was feeling BABA's presence and HIS bliss. Mentally, I was feeling BABA closer and closer than ever before. If one is very much mentally attached to BABA then no one on earth can separate HIM from them. This I could only realize through BABA's great blessings.

> "Human society is one and indivisible, don't try to divide it. Each and every individual should be looked upon as the manifestation of the Cosmic Entity." - BABA

### 59. MEETING BABA IN VARANASI

My Acarya Asiimananda Avadhuta and I were traveling by Aeroflot from Moscow to India. Dada got down at Calcutta, India. I did not get down in India, as it was difficult for me to land. Instead I continued my journey to Dhaka, Bangladesh. I crossed into India by land and came to Calcutta. I found out that BABA was in Varanasi conducting Dharma Maha Cakra, so I took a flight from Calcutta to Varanasi and went to the place where BABA was staying. BABA instructed me to stay in a separate room there. Behind every action of BABA's there is the spirit of welfare and spiritual development.

I reached BABA's place at 5 PM on Sunday. There would be Dharma Maha Cakra at 8 PM. BABA called me to HIS room three times before going to conduct Dharma Maha Cakra.

The first time BABA called me, there were five other organisational Heads standing in HIS room with HIM. "You tell everybody why you left India," BABA said, "and then tell them why you returned back to India!"

I thought in my mind, "BABA knows everything - why I should tell everything in front of these Dadas?" So I kept quiet.

BABA asked the others if they would accept me back in the organization because I missed monthly reporting twice. They said, "No." Hearing this, BABA finished the meeting and dismissed me. After another half an hour, BABA called me for a second meeting and the organisational Heads. BABA repeated what HE said before - to tell my reasons for leaving India and what made me come back.

Again I thought, "BABA knows everything, what is the need to say these things in front of these people." So again I kept quiet. BABA asked other organisational Heads if they would like to accept me back. As I did not offer an explanation, they plainly refused. For the second time BABA closed our meeting.

During the time when I was in Sweden, I contemplated perhaps going to Mainland China and doing pracar there. BABA

once said that one day the top leaders of China would take to Ananda Marga and the entire population would then follow Ananda Marga as well. I thought it would be better for me to work in China rather than in India, illegally and without a visa. But then I thought, what if I were to establish all of Ananda Marga in China – would BABA accept it? In doing so I would not be following HIS organizational rules and guidelines, so what was the use in building such an organization? I decided to follow BABA and HIS Mission only. I surrendered to BABA and decided to work only for HIS Mission, in HIS system only.

BABA called the organizational Heads and myself a third time. BABA asked me the same question. Again I thought the same response. "BABA knows everything," I thought, "Even how I wanted to start Ananda Marga in China… how and why I left and returned to India… Why should I tell this to these people?" BABA might have thought that by revealing my inner plans about China pracar etc. to others then all would understand that one should never try to do anything alone - one would not be successful. This might have been BABA's inner motive behind the drama.

Either way, at the time I did not understand BABA's inner motive - I just kept quiet. Then for the third time I was not accepted back into Ananda Marga by the organisational Heads. This revealed to me one thing - that besides BABA, I had no real friend in the organization. I left it to BABA and prepared for getting expelled, the highest punishment. BABA closed the third meeting without a decision.

The three meetings took place between 5 to 7 PM before the DMC. BABA cancelled further meetings with me due to Dharm Maha Cakra. I went to the pandal and attended the DMC. BABA's Varabhaya Mudra was very blissful and these meetings with HIM were also very inspiring. I realized only BABA can help and save me for HE is the only real friend.

"Encourage everyone to build their career in a nice way. Let none get the opportunity to think that their life has become useless." - BABA

# PART - VIII

## BABA'S KINDNESS

### 60. BABA POSTS ME TO NORTH BRAZIL

After Dharma Maha Cakra was over, BABA again called me for a fourth meeting. Central workers were also present. BABA asked the Central workers to choose a region or a place in the world where no worker had yet put his foot. BABA gave them an hour's time to figure out the details for themselves. An hour later, we met again for the fifth time, this time late at night. Ananda Marga was not present in two regions of the world. One region was in Africa and the other one was in South America, the Manaus Region, which covers the North of Brazil. South America was also called George Town sector.

I was posted as the Regional Secretary for Manaus Region in GT Sector. HE gave me clear instructions that I should not stay more than twenty-four hours in the Sao Paulo Sectoral Office. Rather, I had to head straight to Manaus Region and start pracar there right away. BABA also gave me instructions to leave India within twenty-four hours as well.

All workers were extremely surprised to see that BABA had given me back my worker status, my acarayaship and my avadhutaship. Not only had HE given me back my worker status, but also HE even posted me outside India.

Only BABA knew my inner thoughts. I understood one thing clearly from all this drama, and that was that BABA was the only real friend I had, the only one who helped me even at the cost of controversy. BABA's love for me was unconditional and without any bondages. HIS kindness was beyond any sort of limitations. When the whole organisation turned against me, BABA HIMSELF stood by my side and restored my posting and honour even though these things really only belong to HIM, as I depended on HIM for all the small and big things in my life.

I went to Calcutta by train, crossed into Bangladesh and took a flight from Dhaka to Europe to Sao Paulo, Brazil. The money I had already arranged in Sweden, so I had enough money to buy the ticket. BABA knew that I had enough money to travel to Brazil so BABA gave the order that within twenty-four hours I should leave India and within the next twenty-four hours I should leave the Sao Paulo Sectorial office in Brazil to proceed to my new field, Manus Region, in the North of Brazil.

I spent a few days in Europe for travel arrangements to Brazil, and then I flew from Frankfurt to Sao Paulo, Brazil. I stayed for a day at Sao Paulo and the next night took a bus to Salvador, a city on the coast in Manaus Region, Brazil. I deliberately took an overnight journey so that I could have the rest of the entire day to contact people. I had to start a yoga group in the new city by evening and somehow find shelter by that time as well which is possible only by the grace of BABA.

> "It is action that makes a person great. Be great by your sadhana, by your service, by your sacrifice." - BABA

### 61. BABA SAVES ME FROM ATTACKERS IN BRAZIL

Early in the morning I arrived at Salvador by bus from Sao Paulo. I had very small luggage - only a small bag that contained a few necessities. I had no place to stay, and hotels were out of the question. I didn't like staying at hotels, and besides that, I had almost no money left with me. But I had faith that BABA would arrange everything for me. I started contacting people in gardens, in parks, in offices and at public places, offering to teach them yoga and meditation. The whole day I just went around contacting people in my orange uniform, which served as a big attraction for many people.

Here in the North of Brazil people spoke only Portuguese. Hardly anybody understood English, so I had to search for the people who could talk to me. At last, by the grace of BABA I found a group of people interested in learning meditation from me. They took me to a house and invited many brothers. We even held collective meditation that evening. In this way, on the very first day the first group of margiis in Manaus Region was created, I could find a place to stay in a brother's flat and we started doing collective meditation. They consulted amongst themselves and were even able to arrange money and food for me - I got off to a good start.

The next day I had to do my night sadhana. I explained to the margii brother I was staying with, that I had to find a solitary place and he explained to me how to get there. The big ground was far outside Salvador, about an hour's to walk from the outskirts of the city.

Finally I reached the place after walking alone on the deserted streets for an hour. A fort like wall surrounded the whole area. I was amazed to see the gate; it was so tall, with iron rods coming up to sharp points at the top. I wondered how I would ever be able to even climb the gate—it was sealed with huge locks.

Meanwhile, I noticed a car coming towards me. It stopped only a few meters away from me. Save for this one car, there was no other traffic on the road at that dreadful hour of the night. It was a new moon, pitch-black night. In that dark atmosphere by the lonely place, the car stopped and four persons came out of the car with automatic rifles in their hands. They started approaching me, their rifles pointed right at me. I was alone; there was nobody who could come to my rescue. Still, even it being night, even with their machine guns, I did not expect them to really attack me. So I stopped looking at them and started to look through the gate towards the burial ground and tried calculating how much time it would take for me to climb up the gate.

When I took a quick glance behind, there was utter confusion. The four gangsters were racing back to the car as fast as they could. They leaped into their car, but instead of driving towards me they tore away in reverse for some distance before finally turning around and speeding away.

I was surprised at the turn of events but I had no time to think about them. I scaled up the gate, gained entry into the ground and did my Sadhana for about a half hour and then took the same route back home.

The next morning I narrated to my newly initiated margii brother the whole incident of the last night happenings. Then he told me that in Brazil, especially in the north, when women die they get buried with all their valuables - their gold, jewelry, diamonds etc. These gangsters also go there in the middle of night, break the gate locks, exhume the graves and steal all the jewelry from the dead bodies.

What actually happened was that I firmly believed in BABA, so I had no fear, even if they had automatic rifles. But those four gangsters, though they were carrying weapons, they had fear in their minds, otherwise they would not have had weapons to begin with. I, on the other hand, did not appear frightened of them even after seeing them. I think that BABA caused something to protect me from them. They all ran away as if their lives were in danger. This was a miracle of BABA, saving my life from gangsters. BABA is always with us all the time. There's nothing to worry.

> "Struggle against evil forces is life. We have to remember this fact once again on this threshold of a happy New Year." - BABA

## 62. 'BABA NAM KEVALAM' KIIRTAN ON TV IN BRAZIL

In Manaus Region, North of Brazil, there are twenty-four districts. I started spreading BABA NAM KEVALAM Kiirtan, dance, meditation and BABA's ideology in each and every district of Manaus Region. In every district I would undertake an overnight bus ride to the new place and establish a yoga meditation group by the evening. Within a period of two years, 1980 and 1981, I covered all the twenty-four districts and established yoga meditation groups, all singing the kiirtan of BABA NAM KEVALAM. This work was done by BABA only. But HE needs certain media to work. All credit goes only to HIM.

In one of the biggest cities in the North of Brazil, Recife, I was there teaching several hundreds of persons BABA NAM KEVALAM kiirtan. In Brazil the people like singing and dancing. This is in their blood. They sing and dance samba, an African dance. I taught them spiritual dances. This particular time that I was teaching kiirtan, to so many hundreds of people at once, the National Television station broadcasted a clip of me teaching kiirtan all over Brazil. When I walked in the city or in any other city, people used to identify me by calling out BABA NAM KEVALAM. It was not difficult to recognize me – I was the only person clad in orange.

After covering the coastal cities, like Salvador, Recife, Aracaju, Fortaleza, and Belem etc., I traveled by boat along the Amazon River to enter Manaus. The Amazon Rainforest is as big as the whole of India. The largest river in the world, the Amazon flows right through the middle of the forest. I traveled by boat from Belem to Manaus – a ten-day journey, as the boat went against the flow of the river. When you are on the Amazon, if you can see one of the riverbanks then the other is never visible; it was like traveling about an ocean out there.

After ten days of travel by boat from Belem, finally I reached Manaus, surrounded on all sides by the dense Amazon Rainforest. All around were huge coffee and rubber plantations, all run by capitalistic multinational corporations. Planet Earth has two lungs to purify the world's pollution: the Amazon Rainforest and the South of Russia. And here I saw the Manaus forest, being torn down by greedy capitalists and multinational corporations. Who knows but BABA how great of an ecological imbalance they were causing.

I was able to contact many people in Manaus city and was able to form a new Unit of Ananda Marga spiritualist there.

I took a flight from Manaus to Brasilia, the capital of Brazil. The whole city is designed to look like a plane from above. Close to Brasilia one person whom I initiated in Salvador had four hundred square miles of land. He wanted to give me about one thousand hectares of it just for teaching and spreading yoga. I agreed to accept only one hundred hectares because if it were too big then it would be too difficult to manage. Through BABA's grace, the pracar work in Manaus Region was successful.

> "To come in direct contact with the Supreme Father, you have to *serve* the universe as the ideal daughters and sons of the Supreme Father." - BABA

# 63. BABA RESCUES ME

After two years of intense pracar in Brazil, I made a plan to go to India to see BABA. I was to attend the New Year's Dharma Maha Cakra in Ananda Nagar. On account of land registrations in Brazil and also due to travel arrangements, I showed up at Ananda Nagar a week late. If any worker does not come in time, automatically one is expelled from the organisation. Since I came one week late, I was thus expelled.

Two years back, BABA saved me by re-inducting me into the organization and posting me in North Brazil. The moment I reached Ananda Nagar I was called inside BABA's room. All the Organisational Heads and other workers were present. "Why did you come late?" BABA asked me.

"BABA knows everything," I thought. "HE knows why I'm late. I don't want to talk in front of all these workers." I remained silent.

All the organisational Heads proposed to BABA to expel me for being late for reporting. Even after all that had happened between us, still I was amazed to see these workers so vehemently against me for no reason at all. They were giving BABA false reports just to save their posts and positions.

I got angry standing there, and wanted more than anything to question their sincerity in working for BABA'S Mission right there in front of BABA HIMSELF. I wanted to tell them that if I were to be expelled from the organization, they were the least deserving people to pronounce it.

But then I remembered BABA and thought, "BABA knows about everybody, why should I tell BABA these things? HE knows best. Anyhow BABA is with me, so how can I be expelled?" So I kept quiet and just waited for BABA's decision. BABA told all the workers present, to think things over and then report to HIM in three hours about whether or not they wanted me to remain in the organisation.

I was stunned to see what was going on. I had dedicated my life for BABA. Who were these people to decide my fate in the organization? Even while others were leaving, I stayed in BABA's room. I wanted to know BABA's will – if HE really wanted me to stay in the organization or not. If BABA wanted me to leave, then I would not hesitate to accept HIS decision. Whatever BABA wanted, I was ready to accept.

"BABA," I asked. "Do you want me in the organization?"

BABA smiled. "Let us see what they decide," HE said. Then I was happy to know that BABA was on my side. The whole world could make a union against me and I didn't care, just so long as BABA was with me. I was filled with a newfound hope and inspiration.

As I was coming out of BABA's room, I heard the General Secretary Dada whispering about me.

"BABA doesn't want him to expel" I heard GS Dada say. "Did you see how HE was smiling as HE talked to him?" They were all unhappily surprised to know that BABA was supporting me.

After three hours BABA called back all the same workers. BABA asked for their collective decision. They said that I should be posted in Ananda Nagar only. BABA was angry and shouted, "Who gave you the right to decide his posting? There is another committee to decide about his posting. I wanted to know whether you want him in the organisation or not!"

They all collectively said: "BABA! We want him in the organisation; we don't want him to be expelled." They all agreed without an exception. BABA's word changed their collective decision. It was HE alone who came to my rescue.

When everybody left the room, I stayed alone with HIM. BABA said, "As long as I am present, I will take care, but in the future you have to be careful and try to adjust with the collective flow." This was a great warning for future incidents and developments. Everything eventually came to a front many years later, but all of

it was still brewing even back then. BABA's plan is unknown to us. Hence HIS plan is called Liila where reason is unknown.

> “It is the *duty* of the Supreme to save the *prestige of the devotee*, and the duty of devotee is to leave *everything on HIM*.”
> — BABA

# 64. BABA IN PATNA – A GLORIOUS PERIOD

Though I was working for two years in Brazil, I was constantly thinking about how I could go to India to see BABA. I really wanted to get a posting in India so that I could see BABA regularly.

By the grace of BABA, I was posted in the field in Bihar, the place where BABA started Ananda Marga, the place where BABA spent more than sixty years of HIS life. My duty in Bihar was to organise Akhanda Kiirtan (nonstop kiirtan for at least three hours) wherever I went. In Bihar there is real devotion, though people are economically poor. There are BABA's great devotees. They like to do a lot of kiirtan and listen to BABA's stories. I enjoyed doing pracar in Bihar. Wherever I went, by BABA's grace, twenty-four hour Akhanda Kiirtans were organised. A new flowing wave of kiirtan, full of devotion, started in Bihar. BABA was very happy to hear of all the kiirtan going on there in Bihar. I was very happy too, because very soon HE shifted HIS residence from Bengal and stayed in Bihar for one month. "This little boy has brought a big spiritual wave in Bihar by conducting kiirtans," BABA said to others about me, "The margiis like him so much. I get my reports through my own sources." BABA didn't listen to those workers, but listened to the margiis themselves.

So I was able to stay with BABA for one month in Patna, Bihar. Margiis from all over Bihar came to have HIS darhsan and while they were there I would give them duties to organize more Akhanda Kiirtans when they left. Having BABA there made it much easier to organize things. Many whole-timers and local full-timers were created as well.

Patna city is on the bank of the Ganges. The other side of the river Ganges is called Hajipur where they grow many varieties of fruits and flower plants. Everyday I used to go to the other side of river Ganges, buy plants and submit them to BABA. In

those days, BABA wanted many varieties of fruits, plants and flowers. After accepting them, BABA used to give away these plants to different parts of India to be planted at different Master Units, BABA’s quarters as well as at Ananda Nagar. My Acarya Asiimananda was the in-charge of plants. Dada was an agricultural scientist, so BABA made him responsible for that. Everyday plants were coming from different parts of the world, from every nook and corner of India. When workers used to come from different parts of the world, they had to bring plants to submit to BABA.

After one month of staying with BABA, I was posted as the Kiirtan Organizer for all of North India. One month staying with BABA, every day spending around 8-10 hours reporting with BABA, was glorious period of my life, I ever spent with BABA.

> "You take one step towards HIM and HE will take twenty steps towards you." - BABA

---

## 65. FIELD WALKS WITH BABA IN ALLAHABAD

My new posting in North India comprised the states of Himachal Pradesh, Jammu Kashmir, Haryana, Punjab and Delhi. I intensively toured these States. Soon I received news that BABA was arriving at Allahabad, Uttar Pradesh, so I rushed to receive HIM. Since I was posted to that area, so I got the chance to be with HIM once again.

There were a good number of devotees in Uttar Pradesh. Allahabad is the city where the Ganges and Yamuna rivers meet. BABA liked Allahabad city. HE said that Allahabad was an ideal city to be the capital of India from a security point of view.

Many margiis came from all over north India to see BABA. Every day I used to go with BABA on field walk. Sometimes we used to walk on the banks of the river Ganges or Yamuna and sometimes near the confluence of rivers, known as Sangam. BABA said that earlier civilisations started on the tops of hills and then continued along the river and finally when they reached the river deltas there was blend of all the civilizations that came along the river. Human civilization can be grouped into three categories - Hill civilizations, River valley civilizations and Delta civilizations (near sea or ocean). Plants also grow first in the hills then continue along the banks of the rivers and then become the most complex plants in the Delta regions. Allahabad city was the result of the blending of two rivers - Ganga and Yamuna, hence there was blending of two civilizations. The plants and vegetation are also more advanced; more developed varieties are seen in Allahabad.

Next day, I was walking with BABA. BABA said, "The South American civilization is a transplanted civilization from South India. The Andhra Kings used to rule up to South America, thousand years ago. Since I worked in South America, Brazil, and also toured all the other countries like Paraguay, Argentina and Uruguay etc., I knew the culture there. In South America the

food habits closely resemble Indian's habits, especially South Indian habits. I didn't understand why it was like that. But when BABA said that South Americans look occidental from outside but actually they are oriental on the inside because of the influence of South India, then it made everything crystal clear to me.

Another day also I was with BABA walking in the field. BABA said one very interesting thing about Telangana, a samaj (a socio-economic cultural society) of Andhra, South India. BABA asked me why it was called Telangana. I kept quiet, as I did not know. Then BABA said, "There are three Shiva lingas, located at three corners of Andhra. The land between these three lingas is called Trilinga; later on it became Telangana." BABA further said, "The River Godavari was once upon a time an ice-fed river. It was the biggest river in India. Telangana is very rich in minerals." I was saved by BABA about fifteen years back from the whirlpools of river Godavari. It was the turning point of my life.

I collected and brought different varieties of fruits and flower plants from different nurseries to BABA in Allahabad. BABA felt very happy when different varieties of plants were presented to HIM. BABA explained the different characteristics of the plants and went into extremely minute details that usually only scholars and scientists knew. BABA knew everything—about plants and all other subjects in detail. There is nothing, nothing at all that is unknown to HIM.

One day in Allahabad, there was one margii from Jammu whom BABA was talking to about Indian psychology. BABA said, "It is common in India that if someone is developing or progressing, others instead of being happy or helping him, will try to pull the person down. This is Indian psychology." In such a condition and atmosphere BABA was working all alone to establish HIS Mission—to serve all, to help all, and to develop all physically, mentally and spiritually. HE is Greater than the Greatest!

"Both sadhana and success are within your easy reach. The result is already secured with ME; I shall give it to you at the appropriate time. Do not bother about it." - BABA

## 66. INDIAN GOVERNMENT GIVES BABA VIP STATUS

Every month all the workers of India used to spend a week or ten days, sometimes even fifteen days with BABA during reporting sessions.

The Global Headquarters of Ananda Marga was constructed in 1981 in Calcutta, even in spite of opposition from the West Bengal Government. They refused to sanction us with water, electricity connections, or even a connecting road, but through court we got all the necessary facilities. The West Bengal Government influenced and threatened the labourers and contractors not to work for the construction of the Ananda Marga Global Office. Margiis from Bihar worked as labourers. Margiis from all over India gave a month's salary, and more contributions were pouring in from margiis and workers from all over. Since it was the Global Office, it was the responsibility of all margiis and workers from all over to financially support. Within a record span of five and a half months, our Global Office was constructed in Tiljala, Calcutta. Later Didi's and BABA's Quarters were also constructed.

BABA used to stay sometimes in Lake Gardens, which was in the city and sometimes in Tiljala. Every month there was regular 'reporting' in Calcutta in the presence of BABA. The construction of the huge Tiljala complex right in the middle of their Communist stronghold proved to be a great eyesore for the West Bengal Government. They made a cruel plan to kill workers, and on 30 April 1982, fifteen monks, one nun and one margii were inhumanly massacred. They committed this heinous crime right in broad daylight.

The Communists' barbarous attacks did nothing to hinder the Mission's progress. The intellectuals of Bengal and the news media criticised this cruel attack by the Communists on Ananda Margiis and raised India's consciousness to our plight.

The new government of India had changed its attitude towards BABA. Because of HIS VIP status awarded by the Central government of India, the West Bengal Communist government had to stop its attacks on Ananda Marga. Now we were getting full protection from the Central Indian government, so if we were attacked once again, then the Communists would lose their power due to the imposition of Presidential Rule there in West Bengal, by Central government to maintain law and order.

This was how Calcutta became peaceful and Communist became silent but only after the sacrifice of seventeen members of Ananda Marga. We had been in a constant fight with the Communists of the West Bengal Government. BABA was always inspiring us to fight with the evil forces. Dharma always wins.

> "The only creed of a spiritual aspirant is to love the Universal Consciousness and the Creation. One must not be guided by any national, caste, linguistic or religious sentiment and must not tolerate any fissiparous tendency." - BABA

---

## 67. BABA AVOIDED THIRD WORLD WAR

Every month BABA presided over 'reporting' from workers from all over India. They were all instructed to bring at least five plants from their region. In this way, during each reporting we would get over three hundred plants from all over the world. The plants brought from cold countries would rarely survive the hot Indian climate. Many used to die after only a few days.

BABA also knew that plants were dying. One day BABA was told by my acaraya Dada Asiimananda that the plants were dying because of the hot climate. Still BABA insisted that the plants must be brought. "By getting these plants from all over the world," BABA said. "I am avoiding conflicts amongst different countries; I am avoiding a third world war."

This is my analysis. Plants are the smallest media through which samskaras and vibrations can travel. When these plants were brought to BABA, brought from so many different places by so many different people carrying so many vibrations and samskaras, both individual and collective, then BABA would take these samskaras and finish them right there at HIS quarters. In this way BABA purified the world. Lord Shiva is sometimes called Nilkantha, blue-throated, because it is said that HE took poison to purify all the bad samskaras of the society of that time.

It is a general custom to offer flowers, fruits, or plants to great Spiritual Masters. The science behind all these offerings is that there is a need of certain media to carry all the bad samskaras. Plants or flowers, being less developed, carry the different waves, vibrations of the environment.

Scientists and botanists have traced out that plants hold all the good or bad thoughts and impressions of the persons visiting them. In the USA police have traced the routes of criminals by recording the waves of the trees of the forest. It is same in the case of spiritual or intuitional science.

Lord Ananda Murtijii, the ultimate well wisher of humanity, took the entire burden, all the load onto HIMSELF by assimilating the bad samskaras of the people, thus enabling them to march ahead along the path of self realization, towards the attainment of the Supreme Bliss. HE is the only Savior and Protector of all.

> "The night of Cimmerian darkness is bound to disappear seeing the advent of the dawn. That which is ever true, which is eternal, is destined to be illuminated with the rise of the crimson dawn." — BABA

## 68. BABA'S INTERESTING MONTHLY REPORTINGS

While presiding over our monthly 'reporting,' quite often BABA used to allot some punishment to the workers. By giving punishment BABA was exhausting all our bad samskaras, our suffering samskaras. In HIS presence only, BABA took away all our bad samskaras so that when we went outside to do HIS work, we were full of new energy and inspiration.

BABA used to give us different targets to be fulfilled within a short time. These targets were very difficult to fulfill, often impossible.

One more thing I noticed was that whatever target BABA set, then the situations were created which took time for nature to work on the whole society. BABA targeted that we should bring plants from different parts of the world and then these plants were allotted to different parts of India. After a few months the Government of India took up the plan to plant millions of plants all over the country. The waves, the vibration, to plant the trees originated from BABA and then all others started to work accordingly without their knowledge. This showed that BABA was controlling each and every event on this earth.

Once BABA said, "Whenever I take up a program, project or planning, I take one thousand years ahead of time into account and only then do I give the planning." For example, BABA gave us the target that in every village we should open one school. There are millions of villages all over the world. HE planned that all the villages of the world should be covered by schools of the Ananda Marga system, which is based on Neo-Humanism, universal sentiment, where no caste, no social, no religious divisions are encouraged.

Education is based on universal love for plants, animals, humans, nature and ecology. Then HE used to ask us how soon we would cover all the villages of the world. Mostly Central Dadas

were given this target and then were pressurized by BABA for the time to complete the target. They used to ask for one year, six months, three months, a month, fifteen days or even three days to fulfill the target for covering all the villages, which was also impossible. Still BABA was not satisfied with workers target.

It doesn't take very much common sense to understand that it takes more than three days to even pass on the message across the world that schools have to be started in every village, what to say of actually starting them. So why was BABA giving such targets? BABA was creating the necessary waves so that HIS plans would get materialized in the future; as BABA said, HE kept one thousand years ahead of time in HIS mind before giving any planning or project. HE was creating powerful waves in HIS mental projection and nature, being HIS servant, must materialize those waves, although it could take many years in doing so. HIS whole planning and dealing was beyond human intellect and understanding. Only by devotion can one understand even a little of it. All of BABA's reporting sessions were very educative, inspiring and full of mental and spiritual upliftment.

> "Be ready for the grand ovation to that new crimson dawn in every house. Be prepared physically and mentally, armed with the force of righteousness, for the auspicious moment of the enthronement of the ever new. Be ready to respond to the supra Cosmic trumpet." - BABA

# PART-IX

## BABA'S INDIA TOUR

### 69. BABA IN VARANASI (KASHI)

BABA said, "Varanasi is the oldest city on this planet Earth." BABA also mentioned that Varanasi was the winter capital of LORD SHIVA. He resided in the Himalaya Mountains during the summer and traveled by yak in the snowy mountains. In winter Lord Shiva resided and spent HIS time in Varanasi. Hence Varanasi was considered HIS winter capital. Even today, Hindus consider it to be a holy city because of Lord Shiva.

BABA once said, "Wherever Taraka Brahma (LORD SHIVA or LORD KRISHNA) stayed for even one night, that place got spiritually vibrated and became a Tantra Piitha (a spiritually vibrated place). Later on those places were turned into religious centers where temples were built."

Tantra Piithas are not necessary for one to come in close touch with the bliss of Taraka Brahma. If one even remembers Taraka Brahma or BABA in their mind or repeats HIS name or HIS given mantras, then naturally the mind and the heart will get purified. Such a mind becomes holy. So it is better to sing BABA NAM KEVALAM, the song of love or repeat HIS name or HIS given mantras than to go on a pilgrimage to some far away place or some exploitative religious centre. BABA came on this earth to remove the dirt accumulated from thousands of years in the form of blind faiths and dogmas, BABA came to show us the right direction, the right path so that all humans could march ahead towards all-round development in the physical, mental and spiritual spheres.

BABA went and toured all over north India by car. BABA refused to travel by plane, as the Tandava dance was disallowed

at the Calcutta Airport by the West Bengal Government. The right to do Tandava dance in public places was pending in the Calcutta High Court. It takes a long time to get any decision from those Indian courts, so BABA just preferred to travel by car around India instead of by plane. A series of Dharma Maha Cakras were conducted in different parts of North India.

On this tour of North India, BABA came to Varanasi. BABA's quarters was not yet constructed. There was land arranged for BABA's quarters, but no construction was done, so a separate place was arranged for BABA to stay. All margiis were requesting BABA to stay in Varanasi for Dharma Maha Cakra, but BABA was saying that since there were no quarters, how could HE stay in Varanasi? BABA was telling the driver to drive further to Allahabad where they had quarters for HIM already built. Still the margiis and Dadas begged BABA to give them three months to finish the construction.

Eventually, Dada Nigamananda, the in-charge of the Varanasi Training Center, took the lead to take up the construction of BABA's quarters. BABA stayed at Varanasi and DMC was held.

The margii who BABA agreed to stay with was also the same margii who arranged the DMC on a government agricultural farm where he was the director. This margii got so much opposition from the government, but still, this margii was strong. For him, BABA was LORD OF THE UNIVERSE, why should he care anything. In spite of the opposition, we had Dharma Maha Cakra that weekend on a government farm. During HIS discourse, BABA concluded a book on the life story and teachings of Lord Shiva there in Varanasi, Lord Shiva's own winter capital. The book is *Namah Shivaya Shantaya*, the most authentic book ever written on Lord Shiva.

Once the Varanasi program was over, Dada Shraddhananda and I were sent in advance to arrange Dharma Maha Cakra in Allahabad.

> "The approach of 'I am a sinner, I am a sinner, Oh Father, save me, Oh Father, save me,' is defective! You should say, *'I am your son, I'm your daughter, Oh Father, take me on your lap, I am your son, I am your daughter.'*" - BABA

## 70. TICKETLESS TRAVEL TO RECEIVE BABA

Dada Shraddhananda was the most senior Dada initiated by BABA personally and is now the Purodha Pramukha, the spiritual Head of Ananda Marga. Both of us were sent to Allahabad in the North of India to organize BABA's forthcoming visit. Dada was the General Finance Secretary at the time and I was the North Organizer. We had no money to organize BABA's program, we had only one day at our disposal to raise required funds. The margiis in Allahabad were not so rich, but the margiis in Kanpur, which was nearby, were rich. They had the capacity to raise the funds for our DMC. We had only one day to raise the funds. The next day BABA would arrive any time.

We both left Allahabad and proceeded to Kanpur to raise the funds needed for organising Dharma Maha Cakra and other expenditures involved for the program. It was difficult to meet margiis in the daytime, so the only time we had to arrange all this was during the evening when they came home for dinner. First, we went to margii's houses, contacted them and got little help. Then we went to the house of a capable margii who by himself had the capacity to finance the whole program. We waited till he came home from work, which was a bit late, performed sadhana together, ate dinner and then talked to him about BABA's forthcoming visit to Allahabad. He helped with all the finances needed for the program. Hearing this good news, we then rushed to the railway station to catch the train back to Allahabad. It takes about three hours to travel by train from Kanpur to Allahabad. It was almost midnight, and because of this there was only one train left. When we got to the station, it was already about to leave. If we missed this train, we would miss BABA.

I ran towards the train, but still in the midst of all this, Dadaji insisted that we buy a ticket.

"We don't have time to buy a ticket!" I said. "We're going to miss the train! We'll be stuck here in Kanpur and won't be able to receive BABA in the morning!" Spiritual morality is more important than simple morality. We both ran over the walking bridge and the train started moving. We came down the bridge and dashed to the platform just as the train started moving.

"Get on the guard compartment, quick!" I said. The guard was giving the signal for the train to keep moving. In the middle of all this I went up to the guard and told him that our Guru was coming to Allahabad and we had to get on the train. Well, he didn't stop the train for us, but he did help Dada get onto the last compartment. I also managed to get up onto the moving train.

Thankfully, we made it. The guard offered us places to sit. We relaxed and even managed to have a good conversation with the guard. I told him that we had no time to buy tickets for the train. He understood the urgency and let it pass. We arrived at Allahabad early in the morning, rushed to the jagriti as we rushed for everything else for the past two days, cleaned BABA's room, made the necessary arrangements, took our showers and started to do our meditation.

BABA arrived by car from Varanasi while we were still performing meditation. We just got up and rushed to receive HIM. We didn't even have time to call the local margiis, so no one was there to receive BABA only us.

Two hours later we had reporting in presence of BABA. BABA was not happy with our arrangements. After reporting then Dadaji and I came out of BABA's room and we talked. Somehow we made in time at least to receive BABA in Allahabad. We were happy about it. We were satisfied at least we could receive BABA in Allahabad.

> "In our A'nanda Marga, the seed for a common ideal is sown from the very beginning of a Childs life. At the age of five, when the child has acquired only a preliminary understanding of her surroundings, she is given the ideal of Cosmic Consciousness. The entire society of A'nanda' Ma'rga is then founded on a common ideal - the goal being the Supreme consciousness." - BABA

# 71. PURCHASE OF BABA'S QUARTERS IN DELHI

The Intelligence of India (CBI) made it a point to harass those who were willing to rent or sell a place for us in Delhi. This especially was a problem because we were trying to get a place to build BABA's Quarters there. The CBI was apprehensive that if BABA stayed in Delhi, they might be in trouble. Evil forces are always afraid of good people hence they (evil) do harm to good people. Whenever BABA came to Delhi, BABA had to stay in some margii's house.

After the DMC program at Allahabad, which was well attended, BABA proceeded by car to Delhi. A margii's house was arranged for BABA to stay at during the DMC. A big public hall was booked on rent for conducting a DMC.

BABA told the Delhi margiis that HE did not want to conduct Dharma Maha Cakra in some public hall, but wanted it to be in our own place or jagriti. It had to be arranged within a week's time. A frantic search was made to find a house for purchase. Within the time limit BABA had set, we settled for the purchase of a big farm. The money was raised through the generous donations of the margiis. It was a great miracle that a big farmhouse was bought. That was why BABA was always setting these time limits and demands – to get us to work to materialize HIS plans as soon as possible. They provided HIM with a place to stay, but only one room was reserved for BABA while the rest of building was utilised by the margiis for different spiritual programs.

A few people handled the purchase of the farmhouse. Had they had any prior knowledge of what we were up to, the Indian Intelligence would approach the owner and would warn him not to sell to Ananda Marga. Only when all the papers were signed, registered, money paid then it was announced that BABA's quarters had been purchased in Delhi. BABA happily agreed to conduct Dharma Maha Cakra in the newly bought farmhouse.

Delhi margiis and margiis from other parts of India came to see BABA. There was a small house for BABA, but there was a whole big farm for all the other activities. A huge tent was installed on the ground to conduct the DMC. The whole project of acquiring BABA's quarters was achieved only by HIS special blessings. Thus, the Grand Dharma Maha Cakra was conducted in Delhi. The next day, I was sent to Jaipur, Rajasthan to arrange for BABA's next program.

> "You must keep yourself straight in all strata just as in Sa's't'a'uṋga Pran'a'ma (prostration) and remain vigilant that the fire of your spiritedness is under no circumstances buried." - BABA

## 72. BABA IN JAIPUR, RAJASTHAN

I was sent to Jaipur, Rajasthan that was BABA's next place of visit, on HIS North Indian tour, to arrange Dharma Maha Cakra. Thankfully, this time I was assigned two weeks time instead of one day.

Rajasthan is the place where Lord Krishna spent most of His time. Hence the people of Rajasthan are great devotees, pure and service-minded. The margiis of Jaipur arranged everything for BABA's tour and Dharma Maha Cakra.

Grand reception was given to BABA by the margiis with aratis (lamps) and garlands. BABA was extremely happy to be in Rajasthan. BABA liked the local food. Whatever food was served to BABA with a pure heart, BABA always enjoyed that food. It is the devotion of the devotee that BABA enjoyed. To make BABA happy, only devotion is required. Nothing of the world pleases HIM only the love, faith and feeling of surrender. When BABA is happy then everything goes on smoothly and all is well because Prakriti - nature - being the counterpart of Brahma, has to arrange everything accordingly. The entire creation is HIS mental projection. HE has given different roles to play on this earth to different people, but finally HE controls everything. One realizes this directly by doing kiirtan and meditation.

> "The mission of human life is to move towards perfection, towards God. You have no right to rest until you have fulfilled your mission of being perfect or being divine." - BABA

## 73. RAJASTHAN HAS A GREAT HISTORY

Everyday I accompanied BABA on field walks. Many margiis of Rajasthan would also accompany there. Once BABA said in a fort near Jaipur, "I know all the histories of the world. Of all the histories, Indian history is very interesting and out of all of Indian history, Rajasthan's history is the best and the most glittering." BABA said, "Rajasthan was the bordering state, fighting and protecting India from foreign invaders for centuries."

There were two defects in its history. The two defects were, one was 'the Rajput folly,' that is, the idea that the Rajputs, with a small army, could attack any larger army because of the belief that one Rajput could take on many people. The second defect was that there was a fight amongst the Rajput kings and it ended up helping the Muslims kings to take over India. Had there not been these two defects, India would have never come under foreign rule; Indian history would have been totally different.

BABA said, "The mothers sent their sons and the wives sent their husbands to fight against invaders and the women themselves preferred to die rather than falling in the hands of the cruel invaders." Great were those heroes and heroines who sacrificed their lives for their country.

Pointing to a cannon in the fort BABA said, "It is more than a thousand year old cannon, and it is made of steel. At that time they had knowledge of making steel, brass and other materials."

Speaking about Jaipur, BABA said, "Jaipur, being several hundreds of years old, is the first planned city in the world; the present planned cities are Chandigarh, Paris, and Brasilia etc." Explaining more about Jaipur BABA said, "Jaipur is famous for five 'P's.- Princes, Palaces, Peacocks, Parks (ponds) and Pink color." BABA was explaining many interesting things about Rajasthan.

On the final day there was Dharma Maha Cakra where BABA gave HIS special Varabhaya blessings. After Dharma Maha Cakra, I was given the duty to lead the Avarta kiirtan, which means doing kiirtan in presence of BABA in six alternating directions. I told PA Dada that I would be leading the kiirtan on Prabhat Samgiit tunes (5018 spiritual songs written by BABA). BABA just started composing HIS Prabhat Samgiit, and I wanted to popularize the tunes. I prepared PA's mind so that BABA wouldn't object.

BABA always likes any progressive idea or progressive step, so I had all faith that BABA would support me. I started the first kiirtan on the tune of the first Prabhat Samgiit song BABA wrote- "Bandhu he niye calo - oh Lord take me, lead me on the right path." I sang all six tunes in different Prabhat Samgiit tunes. BABA left after doing namaskar (salutations) to all of us. Then some Dadas came up to me and asked me why I sang BABA NAM KEVALAM to Prabhat Samgiit tunes. They said BABA would be angry with me for doing so.

Afterwards I asked PA Dada what was BABA's reaction when I started singing kiirtan in Prabhat Samgiita tunes. Dada gladly told me that he had already mentioned my name to BABA and conveyed the message to BABA that I was going to sing kiirtan in those tunes, and HE was very happy to hear it. Dada also told me that while I was singing BABA NAM KEVALAM, BABA was also singing the corresponding Prabhat Samgiit to match with my kiirtan. BABA was happy and always encouraged us, just so long as what we were doing was for the welfare of all.

> "Spirituality must inculcate sense in human psychology and develop a natural affinity amongst all the species of the creation."
> – BABA

# PART - X

# BABA'S ORGANISATIONAL SYSTEM

## 74. BABA'S MASTER UNIT PROGRAM

Master Units are a program started by BABA as a kind of mini-PROUT eco-community. On these Master Units (which have at least five acres of land), PROUT principles are put into place as far as possible to establish agricultural self-sufficiency, relief, education and medical programs. The concept of these Master Units is to serve as a model community for every village to make the model of – a PROUT-in-progress. These Master Units would be especially important when Capitalism falls.

After BABA's tour of North India, HE initiated a program to start Master Units in every district and then in every block of India and then move on to establishing them all over the world as well. The model Master Unit project is at Ananda Nagar, our current Global Headquarters situated near Bokaro Steel City at the border of Bengal and Bihar. Now BABA wanted us to establish Master Units like Ananda Nagar all over the world.

Every month we had 'reporting' in presence of BABA, during which HE would give new ideas for us to work on in the field. HE gave us this Master Units program during one such reporting. I took this planning very seriously, and decided right there and then to get the land for as many Master Units as I could by the next 'reporting'. I had hardly three weeks time. I went to Andhra on tour. In Andhra, I knew the local language. It was easy to talk to farmers in their language and get them to donate land for Ananda Marga Master Units.

First I contacted a farmer in Mehboobnagar, in Andhra. Though not very rich, he immediately agreed to transfer and register eight acres of land to Ananda Marga. The process took one week. The next week I contacted one more farmer in the Ranga Reddy district of Andhra. He was also poor, but he was impressed by BABA's Master Unit plannings, and agreed to transfer and register six acres of land. This also took a week's time.

For the next month's 'reporting', I went to see BABA with two Master Unit lands registration papers. BABA was happy to know that two Master Units had been arranged and registered in Andhra. BABA said, "I gave the Master Unit program and Savitananda arranged two Master Units in a short time." BABA announced the new Master Units to all the margiis from all over India that Sunday during his general darshan. I was very happy for BABA's work had been done.

Actually BABA arranged these Master Units, not me. It is not so easy to convince a farmer to donate his land for an idealistic project. Only BABA inspired them psychically. Even in spite of this, BABA would always give credit to others. This is HIS greatness. In my absence BABA said, "If Savitananda wants, he can start a Master Unit in every block of Andhra." This may happen in the future; this also just depends upon HIS wishes.

BABA gave spiritual names to those Master Units I started, and also posted workers to look after them. The Master Units still exist in Andhra and are gradually developing. A lot of work still has to be done to further develop these Master Units, to upgrade their standards. I am sure BABA will give us strength to develop them further.

> "Bad company strengthens the bondage of the soul while good company is conducive to liberation and salvation." - BABA

### 75. BHUKTI PRADHAN 'REPORTING' BEFORE BABA

Bhukti Pradhan is the elected person from amongst the margiis. He is the district level elected member of Ananda Marga, held by a margii working under the supervision of a whole timer posted in that area. Every month BABA called all the Bhukti Pradhans in turns from the North, South, East, and West of India, etc. The same is the case with all the other sectors across the world. If it were the month for North Bhukti Pradhan reporting, then the Bhukti Pradhans of the eight Sectors of the northern hemisphere would also attend Bhukti Pradhan reporting. BABA's system was to unite the whole world and the entire humanity as one cosmic family.

It was the month for North Bhukti Pradhan reporting. I was working in North India, so I was there too as all the Bhukti Pradhans from the North stood in front of BABA, waiting to report.

BABA called up one Bhukti Pradhan for a demonstration. He was a doctor from Haryana. He was having lung trouble, but no medical check up could diagnose his difficulties. To BABA's enquiry he admitted it. BABA said, "Your lung trouble is in the preliminary stage, hence it was not diagnosed. But you still have a lung problem." BABA spoke to the assembly, "You see, there are four things which should be removed from the very beginning. If you neglect them then they can create a big problem. The first one is fire. If fire is in the form of sparks lying in some corner and you neglect it, then those same sparks can be converted into a big fire any moment. In the very beginning the sparks of fire should be controlled." BABA continued, "The second one is enemy. If you neglect an enemy who is weak in the beginning then they can pose a big danger to your very existence later on." BABA continued. "The third one is debt. If you have a little bit of debt to pay then the same debt can become much larger later; it can become a big burden. If you have not paid loans, you will suffer from inferiority complex. So loans and debts must be repaid in the very beginning." BABA continued, "The fourth one is disease. If you neglect diseases when they are in their preliminary stages, then that same disease can become fatal. So disease must be controlled in the very beginning." BABA, looking at the doctor and said, "This boy has tuberculosis, but it is only in its preliminary stages. However, if it is neglected now then it can become serious in future. So it must be cured now."

BABA took HIS stick and touched the middle of the doctor's chest. BABA asked him to breathe normally and asked him how he was feeling.

The doctor said, "BABA! Now I can breathe smoothly and without any problem, my chest feels very light."

"Now you are ninety percent cured," BABA said. "The rest will go away by doing regular sadhana."

BABA then asked the doctor to meditate while in standing position. BABA asked him to take his mind to higher cakras (energy centers) and asked him to narrate to others, what he was feeling? The margii started to meditate and took his mind to higher cakras, higher points of the spinal cord and finally to the top of brain.

He said, "BABA I am now feeling very soothing, very light." BABA asked him to bring his mind to lower cakras, lower points, and asked him to narrate his feeling to others. He brought his mind to lower cakras. He said. "BABA, I am feeling very heavy, my whole mind is filled with troubles." Then BABA asked him to take his mind to higher cakras again. He said, "I am feeling very happy again - I want to stay here all the time."

BABA said to all of us, "When you do sadhana, meditation, at least twice a day then your mind goes into higher and higher cakras; then you feel very happy. So always be regular in your spiritual practices, because they are the effort to take your mind to higher cakras." Afterwards BABA asked the doctor to rejoin the rest of the Bhukti Pradhans.

It's very important that we are very sincere in our spiritual practices (Sadhana) to elevate our minds to higher levels.

> "In this universe there is nothing beyond the Supreme Consciousness. You always remain within HIS circle." - BABA

### 76. BABA's LOVE AND CARE

I was working in the North of India. One day while I was working I fell down and broke my leg. Needless to say, I could not walk at all. Fortunately, it happened just near the house of a margii who was also a Bhukti Pradhana for East Delhi. He was kind enough to arrange my medical treatment. I stayed at his house until I was able to walk properly by myself. Nonetheless, I made full use of this time by turning his house into an improvised training center, with regular sadhana every morning and evening and lots of kiirtan as well. All the children of the family were strengthened by this regular routine.

Since I was stuck in Delhi due to my leg fracture, I obviously couldn't attend monthly 'reporting' before BABA. BABA was not told of my injury during reporting, but HE was told by my acaraya, Dada Asiimananda. So I recovered fast.

After some time I shifted to BABA's quarters in Delhi and stayed there for a few months for complete recovery. I went to see BABA in Calcutta. When BABA came out of HIS room, we all did Sastanga pranam - I was still having leg pain. BABA enquired about my leg and looked at the fracture and advised me to take the help of a stick while walking to avoid putting any undue pressure on it. I followed BABA's instructions and started using the stick while walking.

I went to see my Acarya Asiimananda Avadhuta. He was the in-charge of plants at the time, so I went to him to give him the plants I had brought for BABA. While I was there, Dada told me one very interesting thing:

Everyday Dadaji used to spend several hours with BABA. Every morning, BABA's 'reporting' used to start with Dadaji, as he was the in-charge of plants. BABA knows everything. One day BABA asked about me. Dada Asiimanandaji told BABA that I got a fracture in my leg so I was unable to come to Calcutta to see HIM. Every single morning the first thing BABA used to ask my acarya was about my health and how I was progressing in my recovery. BABA was asking about me for all the six months I did not come to Calcutta to be with HIM. When I heard this, tears rolled down my cheeks. BABA's love and care is so deep, beyond Human understanding. HIS love and care is there for each and every one. One can know HIS love by singing BABA NAM KEVALAM kiirtan, by dancing in HIS rhythm and by meditating on HIM only. HE is the only real friend, the only one who always came to rescue me every time I was in trouble.

Great will enrich our own.

> "Spiritual practice comes within the scope of science; the first scientist of this spiritual science was Lord Shiva, who was born about 7000 years ago." - BABA

## 77. BABA GAVE US MONKEY JOB & BLESSED US

For a while I was working in India, we had a social service camp in Ananda Nagar. We were staying at one place when one Dada misplaced his money. We were searching for this money when PA Dada came to our room and saw us looking everywhere. He just laughed and went. Eventually he ended up telling BABA that the volunteer workers were spending their time looking for lost money. The money was very little, but still the news reached BABA.

BABA called all of us. We were hardly twenty workers. BABA told us, "We were supposed to help others, supposed to give security to others – how can they lose money, even if just a little amount (hardly five dollars)?" Our organization was removed. We became jobless. HE also gave us punishment. Ananda Nagar had many trees. Our punishment was to climb up and down the trees like monkeys. Actually it was not the money (five dollars) but there was some other deeper meaning hidden behind giving us such kind of punishment.

While all this was going on, there was also a Dharma Maha Cakra there in Ananda Nagar, so there were many margiis present. They were curious and surprised to see us climbing up and down the trees all day over and over again. They did not know of our monkey job punishment. For us, it was a fun punishment from our dear BABA and we enjoyed it and cherished the memory.

In the evening BABA selected only nine persons out of the twenty and formed a new organization - Ananda Marga Seva Dal. I was also one of the founding members of Ananda Marga Seva Dal.

BABA addressed the nine members: "Let us start a new organization Ananda Marga Seva Dal. Be very strong morally and spiritually. Go on doing great works. Even if you are alone in the noble task, go ahead, I am with you as long as you help and serve humanity. Victory is yours." BABA blessed all of us. It was a very special day for us. First BABA purified us and then graced us with HIS Blessings. This was how BABA was establishing HIS children and the Mission.

> "In each sphere of life, we should follow a code of discipline. Knowing or adhering to the code of discipline means 60% success." - BABA

### 78. BABA PREDICTS FUTURE - POLE SHIFT

One day 'reporting' was going on. All at once there was heavy storm and rain. It was not the season for it. BABA said, "The poles are shifting, hence the seasons are changing. This is not the time for rains. The rain is due to the shifting of the poles. They are shifting very slowly right now."

Afterwards BABA gave a detailed talk on the pole shift. HIS talk was predicting the future. It was a very interesting discourse and I should mention that it was published. Here I would like to explain some of the salient features of HIS discourse.

All the planets are always on the move. The solar system with its sun as the nucleus is one set, and this set is moving around some other galaxies and nebulae. There was a change in the movement of other galaxies, and this in turn is affecting this solar system. Hence the movement of the earth around itself will also be changing. The poles are shifting along its axis. This will bring enormous changes in the climate, vegetation and even in the electromagnetic fields of the earth. Due to this change in the electromagnetic fields, the thoughts and psychology of humanity will also change. When this happens, ordinary people will become more naturally spiritually oriented.

The future of humanity is great and bright. Mankind will take up spirituality naturally. Humanity is evolving from imperfection to perfection. In due course of time there will be a mass awakening of spirituality.

BABA took advent on this earth to guide humanity to the right direction - if all human beings start singing and dancing, and do BABA NAM KEVALAM kiirtan and meditate, all the problems of the earth will be solved. Humanity has to move from crude physicality to subtle spirituality. BABA's guidance and directions are good for thousands of years to come. The sooner we take up BABA's ideals for humanity, the better it will be. HIS ideals are to unite one and all and to establish one Universal Human Family, One Human Society.

> "The Supreme being is calling you in the roar of the ocean, in the thunder of clouds, in the speed of lightning, in the meteors flaming fires." - *BABA*

## 79. BABA DISCLOSES TEMPLES MANIPULATION

One day BABA was explaining about drugs. When one gets habituated to drugs then one needs to take that drug all the time. Similarly, if birds or animals are given drugs, they will also get addicted, and a pattern in their behaviour can also be observed. This habit was exploited in a temple in south India.

There was a belief at one south Indian temple that LORD VISHNU daily visited the temple flying on Garuda (mythological eagle) from Vaikuntha (Heaven) to earth to have prasad (offering to God) at the temple. The Garuda used to come exactly at twelve o'clock noon. Thousands of devotees assembled at the temple to have Lord Vishnu's darshan. The Priests told the people that Lord Vishnu was not visible to them because their hearts and minds were not pure; people could, however, see Lord Vishnu's vahan, (vehicle) the bird Garuda. Devotees assembled there with a sincere heart to have Lord Vishnu's darshan but were satisfied by seeing the eagle alighting on the temple area at the appointed hour. This was how everyday thousands of devotees were befooled.

Explaining about the psychology behind the birds visiting the temple at the right time, BABA said, "The birds were daily fed with a little drug like opium. Once the bird got addicted to the drug then the bird was left alone to fly. Exactly at that same time the next day, the bird would come in search of the drug, which it got in the form of prasad (sweet offered to Lord)."

This was how the common man's religious faith was exploited in one form or another and how the common man was misguided. It is not possible to get closer to spirituality if one is being exploited with such religious superstitions. BABA has come on this earth to remove all such superstitions, dogmas, blind faiths and to guide the humanity towards light and perfection.

"The approach to spirituality should be psychological. Through rational analysis, humanity should appreciate its relationship with God." - BABA

## 80. BABA MAKES FUN OF DRUGS

Sometimes BABA told amusing stories. Sometimes we would laugh so much that we would have to ask BABA to not make us laugh anymore. HE used to enjoy keeping us happy.

One story was about drugs. BABA said that there was once one priest traveling by train from Jammu to Kanyakumari on a very long journey. He told so many interesting stories about gods, goddesses etc., to the travelers in his compartment. At one point he was searching his pockets for the drug (opium) he took at regular intervals. It was not there.

One of the listeners offered to arrange it for him at the next station. The train stopped at Pathankot station. When the train started again the listener offered him (priest) the drug (opium). The priest took it happily and then continued his speech on gods, goddesses, temples and so on.

When the Priest went to the bathroom others enquired and were told that the supposed drug was a mud and not the real opium. He arranged the mud tablet just to satisfy the Priest as a placebo. They were amused.

The priest continued with his talk on religion. They asked him how he was accustomed to the drug and what benefit he would get from it. Then priest said, "Don't you know if one takes the drug then one becomes immortal? One can never die!" He continued, "There were four persons who became immortal. One was Hanuman, son of the air God, the second one was Ashwathama, son of Dronacharya, and the third one was Yamraj, the God of death. Do you know how Yamraj became immortal?"

The Priest continued on with another story. There was one person who was supposed to die at 13.30 o'clock in the afternoon. Yamraj went to him at 13:30 in the afternoon and prepared for his death. Then the person told Yamraj to wait till he took an opium tablet at 14:00 hours. Yamraj, the God of death, agreed to wait half an hour more, reading the newspaper as he

did so. At 14:00 hours, the person prepared his opium tablet, and as it was custom in India to offer to others before eating, so he offered the opium tablet to Yamraj (death God).

Yamraj took the tablet and became intoxicated, felt happy and told the man that he would come the next day to take him at the same time. The next day also Yamraj (death God) took the opium drug and left the man alive. This continued for many days. Then Yamraj decided that the man should accompany Yamraj (death God) to his place, without dying and give him the dose of opium at the appointed hour regularly. Thus, Yamraj and the clever man, who took opium, both became immortal. In this way four persons became immortal. With such stories, religious priests were exploiting and amusing the common folk.

In India religious people take drugs in the name of Lord Shiva. But Lord Shiva never took drugs; HE was always in trances due to establishment in higher states of Consciousness. One who consumes drugs cannot realize the Supreme Self. One should never consume drugs or intoxicants which is against spirituality. This is the main theme of the story which BABA meant.

> "Inspire those who are liars and criminals to live honest lives by pointing out their defects. This is the only spiritual approach to punish wrongdoers." - BABA

## 81. BABA GAVE DUTY MICROVITA PROPAGATION

In the late 80's, BABA gave a new scientific theory called Microvita. Microvita is the smallest particle, so small that millions of Microvita combine to form an electron. This means that an atom consists of billions of Microvita. Microvita also works at different levels and helps in spiritual growth. Scientists of the future will someday be able to count the exact number of Microvita in each substance and will be able to create the exact object needed through this science of Microvita.

BABA said that Microvita theory is four hundred years ahead of time - BABA gave this Microvita theory just to accelerate the research in this direction for the benefit of humanity.

BABA made a Global Microvita Steering Committee consisting of five Dadas, myself included. Everyday BABA was checking up on us, asking us about progress in propagating the Microvita theory.
