I organized two programs in India, one in Bombay at the Atomic Research Center and the other one in Delhi at the All India Institute of Medical Sciences (AIIMS).

In Bombay (present Mumbai) the top nuclear scientists of India assembled to listen to BABA's Microvita theory. Dadas from the West (America and Europe) gave the lecture, talking about how every electron is further sub-divided into many smaller particles. Modern Science has no means to measure it, but in the future science will discover the truth: that an electron is made of millions of Microvita. Microvitum particle, called in its singular form, is the fundamental unit of Energy.

The universe is full of Energy. Microvita is also working on the psychic level, influencing the mind as well. The source of Energy and the source of Microvita is nothing but Pure Consciousness itself.

"Where does the energy from an atom come from then, during nuclear fission or fusion?": They asked. We answered their question, and they were very impressed. They told us that their whole lives they had been doing research, trying to find the source of energy but now in the light of Microvita theory they realized that matter itself, having come from Consciousness, was not the same matter that they understood it to be before.

Our Microvita conference was well published in the Bombay newspapers. BABA was extremely happy to know that our Committee was propagating HIS Microvita theory.

The next program was at AIIMS, Delhi. From the very outset, negative Microvita in the form of the Police Intelligence of India interfered to stop our program there. They used all their tactics to influence the Directors of the All India Institute of Medical Sciences to not provide us a hall for our lecture, simply because this theory was coming from Ananda Marga. The positive Microvita of ours was stronger than the negative microvita of the police, so eventually we won; the All India Institute of Medical Science supported us, as this was their academic subject and not the police's business. Indian Intelligence had nothing to do with a scientific research subject. We conducted the Microvita program at AIIMS, Delhi, successfully. Jai BABA!

The next day all the leading newspapers of India published BABA's Microvita theory after our symposium. I went to see BABA after conducting these programs. BABA was extremely happy to see me and blessed me after the good news was conveyed to HIM. Actually everything was done by HIS wish, by HIM only, but BABA needs certain media to get HIS work done. So this planet is really just waiting now for HIS Mission, for HIS planning to materialize. The work had been already accomplished in psychic level. Only physical form has to be taken. This is just a matter of time; it is bound to happen, because it is HIS divine wish.

"Science is moving fast - it is moving ahead and it *will* continue to move ahead. Nobody can check the advancement of science by criticizing it." - BABA

# 82. BABA REMOVES A DREADFUL DISEASE

At Ahmedabad, Gujrat, in West India we had a training centre for soon-to-be monks. One day a mysterious, serious disease was reported amongst the trainees. It affected all the trainees, and the symptoms were strange. Medical doctors started to investigate the disease, but they never heard of such symptoms. Specialist doctors were consulted, but still, no one could understand and diagnose the disease because the symptoms were quite new for them. They were all wondering how they could control the spread of this new disease. They gave up when they failed to control it by administering different drugs.

When the matter was reported to BABA then HE revealed the secret behind the incident. BABA said that some of the most powerful negative microvita of the universe had come to this planet earth. It was so destructive that it could have finished the whole planet; it took shelter in the water tank of the training center, on the roof. The trainees were drinking, bathing, and cooking food with that Negative Microvita-infested water, so that is why they were getting effected with the disease. Ordinary persons could not have survived it.

The trainees were surviving, fighting against this most powerful negative microvita. BABA asked for rose flowers equal to the number of trainees at the centre. BABA blessed those rose flowers and gave some instructions on how to counter the Negative Microvita. One Dada was sent immediately by flight from Calcutta to Ahmedabad with those blessed roses.

Dada arrived in Ahmedabad. As per the instructions of BABA, the roses were given to each of the trainees – one each. The trainees then changed their clothes for new ones, took the roses with them and left the training center. Nothing else was taken out from there – everything was burnt, all the pots were buried — the whole training center was left empty. Gradually the trainees, with the help of the roses BABA gave them, got rid of

the influence of that Negative Microvita and slowly but surely all their symptoms vanished.

All the trainees were moved to a new city called Gandhidham, in Gujarat. New land was purchased and a new training center was established there in Gandhidham. Soon enough, all the trainees returned to their normal health again. The medical doctors were completely astonished to see the trainees make their full recoveries.

Spiritual science or intuitional science is beyond the human capacity to understand. Certain things can be described, but subtler things are beyond the capacity of human intellect. This was how BABA saved a major catastrophe of this planet earth by sacrificing one of HIS own sons who came to the training center to serve humanity. Jai BABA!

> "Human beings will definitely be able to increase human longevity by scientific techniques. In certain special cases they will even be able to restore life to dead bodies." - BABA

### 83. BABA PROTECTS DEVOTEES IN GAS TRAGEDY

Connected to Negative Microvita, there is one more incident that happened. There was this American Union Carbide Company at Bhopal, Madhya Pradesh, in Central India. One night there was a gas leakage in which several thousands persons died and many more became permanently handicapped. It was almost as bad as the effects of the Hiroshima and Nagasaki nuclear bombs – it was the century's worst tragedy connected to the leakage of poisonous gases.

In Bhopal there was also an Ananda Marga School in which about four hundred children were studying. The school was situated in the campus of Bharat Heavy Electricals Limited (BHEL), a public sector of the government. These four hundred children of the school came from different parts of the city. When the Union Carbide Gas leakage occurred the children were sleeping. The poisonous gas did not affect the students of the school, not any of them. The fatal gas did not even harm the family members of those children. Whereas the rest of the other people either died immediately or got damaged or handicapped, everyone connected to Ananda Marga remain unaffected. This was a big mystery to the people there, but with the theory of Microvita, it makes all clear.

There are two types of Microvita. One is Positive Microvita and the other is Negative Microvita. Negative Microvita cause diseases and destruction, but Positive Microvita helps in curing diseases and helps in overcoming the various different psychic and physical influences of Negative Microvita. The school children were singing BABA NAM KEVALAM daily in their school. The children were chanting Samgacchadvam, a 15,000 year-old Rgveda mantra. The school children were regularly doing meditation, sadhana hence all their cakras and nerve centers like the brain, heart, and all endocrine glands were so strong with Positive Microvita that any amount of Negative Microvita or any form of poisonous gas could not influence them.

The children were so strong with Positive Microvita that the Positive Microvita, the positive spiritual waves of the children, apparently even influenced the family members with whom they were living. Hence all their family members also survived the gas tragedy.

One who sings the LORD’s name is the devotee of BABA. The devotees are safe in HIS hands and there is nothing to worry about, as BABA is the controller of all the Positive Microvita. BABA’s grace actually is the Positive Microvita. Hence one should work hard to become HIS devotee by singing HIS name, i.e. BABA NAM KEVALAM and totally depend on HIM by surrendering to HIM.

> “*The majority of people* today do not know the technique of using Microvita. The 'Universal Entity sitting in one place has been using microvita to accelerate the spiritual growth of individuals in different celestial bodies in different ways.” - BABA

### 84. BABA WAS HAPPY WITH GROUP DISCUSSIONS

Every month we visited BABA for our monthly reporting. In the last days before HIS Physical Departure (Maha Prayan), BABA emphasized that we should read HIS books regularly. All of BABA’s many ideas were all well explained in HIS books. BABA told us to make a habit of reading HIS books everyday.

I was holding tests on philosophical questions amongst the workers of Delhi Sector. I started a debating system so that there would be discussions and debates on different philosophical areas. BABA appreciated the efforts.

BABA introduced two-group discussions. One group would support Ananda Marga ideology and the other group would come up with entirely negative and critical ideas against Ananda Marga. Five speakers were selected to speak for Ananda Marga and another five speakers were selected to speak against Ananda Marga. I was made the Chairperson to conduct the debate. The time for conducting the debate was 10.00-11.00 AM in the morning everyday during reporting sessions.

Everyday I reported to BABA the subjects of discussion and also about who were leading the opposition camps and who were standing up for the ideology. BABA wanted that the strongest persons should opt for the opposition to make it more difficult. Even so, the ordinary, simple-minded workers, due to their straightforward ideas defeated the opposition side and their intellectuality easily. The opposition group always came up with brilliant arguments but they appeared futile and counterproductive. Eventually, devotees who have love for the Supreme would win. Intellect has nothing to do with it – it all comes down to love and devotion eventually. Hence we should work hard to develop love and affection for BABA.

> 'The downtrodden *humanity* of this disgraced *world* is looking up to the eastern horizon, awaiting the advent of sadvipras (spiritual moralists) with earnest zeal and eagerness." - BABA

## 85. BABA ENCOURAGES ME TO PLAY GUITAR

In 1975 when I was working in Europe, I learned to play guitar in the Sweden Training Centre. On the very first day I learned some basic chords like Em, D, Am, G, etc. I started to play songs with the help of these chords. I practiced for one day and the next day I played in front of the trainees.

"It took us four or five years to play with the proper rhythm," they said. "And now in one day you're playing at the same level." It was BABA's grace that I learned to play so quickly. I played for many more days and learned many chords.

When I was in India in 1982, I was still playing the guitar. Indian villagers were singing BABA NAM KEVALAM to my guitar tunes. Many knew me as 'The Guitar Dada'. They were remembering me more often as 'The Guitar Dada' rather than by my own name. Such is the habit of the people in India. People get their name according to what they wear and what they do. This is a common habit in India: So I was called the guitar Dada, which of course I did not like. I was playing difficult Indian tunes on my guitar with the help of simple basic chords.

It was a quite difficult job to carry my guitar through the heavy crowds of the Indian trains and buses. Whenever I entered any train or bus with my guitar, people respected me, thinking that I was a musician. They made space for my guitar and accommodated it somehow. By BABA's grace I never felt any problems.

One day I decided that I would never play guitar anymore, and what I decided I usually followed strictly through with. During one reporting with when we all finished reporting, then we sang Prabhat Samgiit and sang kiirtan, then usually BABA would leave. But this time, BABA unexpectedly said in front of all the workers, "Do you all know one thing - Savitanandaji plays guitar very well." All the workers said, "Yes, BABA! He plays nicely, we also like him playing guitar." When BABA says something then naturally all the workers always support it.

Then I had no choice but to change my decision. I decided I would never touch the guitar again and now BABA was talking about how good I was with it. The next day I played Avarta Kiirtan (kiirtan facing six different alternating directions) on the guitar after finishing reporting. We usually sang Avarta Kiirtan without any instruments. Instruments were not allowed, but still I played kiirtan on the guitar because I knew BABA wanted me to play. I could play guitar once again. This was how BABA encouraged me to keep playing guitar. I still play guitar to even to-day, but only to sing HIS name in BABA NAM KEVALAM.

> "Fine arts lead human beings to the Transensory realm; hence Sadhakas should not discourage fine arts; rather they should support them, directly or indirectly." - BABA

## 86. BABA TRAINS US IN PRACAR (PROPAGATION)

Every month during reporting, BABA would often give us new programs. HIS planning and programs were so interesting, and sometimes they were just beyond our reach to understand or comprehension.

I shall narrate one old incident from the 1970's. At that time the Central Bureau of Investigation, the CBI police of India, were trying to estimate how many workers we had in the organization. Every month, many workers were traveling in orange dress, and it was an embarrassment for the Indian Intelligence. So they took one month to make a survey, counting all the workers traveling all over India. The Indian Intelligence informed all the Railway Police and local state police to count the number of Ananda Marga Swamiis (Workers) they saw.

We did not know what was going on behind our backs, but of course BABA knew everything. BABA started a program of transferring workers from one place to another. Before the worker reported at his place, another transfer and replacement was already waiting. In a month hundreds of workers were continuously transferred, posted again, transferred again, so workers had to move and travel all over India frequently. After one month the Indian Intelligence survey was completed. BABA also stopped the frequent transferring of the workers. When the final count of the member of workers of Ananda Marga was calculated, the central office of Indian Intelligence, the Indian Government and Russian Government were all surprised to know that Ananda Marga had more than ten thousand workers, working as volunteers without any payment. The Government of India could not manage such a big group of volunteers, what to say of any other government. The Indian Intelligence also knew that the workers lead a very hard life. How could a man like BABA get over ten thousands workers and give them no money? They had to undergo such hardship and still the workers were so happy. It was beyond their understanding.

Every month for one to two weeks BABA presided over the workers' reporting from 10.00 AM onwards and continued into the night with some breaks in between for lunch, dinner, and for individual sadhana.

BABA stayed at Lake Gardens in Calcutta. We also used to go to Tiljala. (It is about 5 kilometers from Lake Garden). Sometimes I used to reach a bit late because of my long morning meditations. Whenever I was late by an hour or so, BABA also was late, thus saving me the embarrassment. Mentally I used to thank BABA for not putting me in such an awkward situation. These were minor things but very touching, especially when one is filled with devotion and surrenderance to BABA.

One month, BABA gave us training on how to do pracar. We would all go out into the streets of Calcutta in groups and do pracar, speaking to people about meditation and social service programs etc., till evening. Every morning after having BABA's darshan, we would go out into the streets of Calcutta for pracar. This was an intense training for us, to teach us how to contact people when we work in the field. It was a very good, practical experience. Many got interested in Ananda Marga ideology and learnt meditation. This was how BABA established HIS spiritual ideology inspite of adverse situations.

> "The most valuable treasures on the path of human progress are honesty, simplicity and spirit. In no stratum of life should you be allowed the standard of honesty to deteriorate." - BABA

## 87. BRAHMA KAMAL FROM HIMALAYAS FOR BABA

One day General Secretary (GS) Dada told me to go to Ananda Nagar. "Go look after the brick industry there," he told me. I knew nothing about such things; I was interested mostly in spreading BABA's ideology, in doing kiirtan and meditation and conducting special classes and now GS Dada was giving me this duty to go look for bricks. I was frustrated but still, I could not refuse GS Dada's order, so I proceeded to Ananda Nagar. The brick manufacturing was well taken care of by the senior Dadas who had been living there. Hence I thought that there was no need for me to stay at Ananda Nagar. I had one month in hand, so I planned to go to the Himalayas to fetch some rare plants for BABA.

It was the months of August and September, and during those months the snow melts so that one may venture up to the higher altitudes of the Himalayas to fetch rare flowers like the Brahma Kamal etc. I took a train from Ananda Nagar and went to Delhi. From Delhi I went to Himachal Pradesh and from there I proceeded by bus to the higher altitudes of the Himalayas. I went up almost 15,000 feet. There I stayed in a village to travel further. From there, the only way to go higher in the mountains was on foot. I took a local man as a guide and we both started trekking up the Himalayas. We were able to climb up another 2000 feet. During other months the whole area would be snow filled, but now most of the snow had melted. I was looking for one plant called BRAHMA KAMAL (BRAHMA's LOTUS). Lotuses grow in water but this Brahma Kamal grows in the ice. All year round this Brahma Kamal remains encased in the ice, and only for a month or two when the snow melts, only then is the Brahma Kamal visible.

In the past, this Brahma Kamal was offered every year to Lord Shiva. I thought; let me offer a Brahma Kamal to my Lord Shiva – BABA for HE is the Lord of Lords. I picked up so many Brahma Kamal (Lotus) flowers for BABA and I put them all in an icebox so that they could survive in the same temperature. I also picked up some other rare plants for BABA along with.

One of the plants was Bhurj Patra (the bark of a certain kind of tree). If one writes on these Bhurj Patra then the writings can remain there for thousands of years, it's so durable. In the olden days all scriptures such as the Vedas were written on these Bhurj Patras (leaves). They are powerful antiseptic plates made of the bark of a plant hence they don't really get spoiled. I collected them and in a good number before starting my journey back to see BABA at Calcutta.

When I reached Calcutta, everybody told me that GS Dada was very angry with me for disobeying his order by not staying in Ananda Nagar to take care of the brick manufacturing unit. I felt I could do nothing for the brick industry, as it was already well administered. Thinking little of what I heard about Dada, I submitted the Brahma Kamal and all the other rare plants to my Acarya Asiimananda Avadhuta to be given to BABA. BABA first takes plant 'reporting' every day, before anything else, so before GS Dada had the chance to complain about me, it was reported to BABA that I had brought Brahma Kamals for HIM. BABA was so happy to know that I brought these. These Brahma Kamals were kept in a special glass house where the below zero temperatures could be constantly maintained. Now GS Dada could say nothing, because BABA was happy with me. We should work only to please BABA and none else. This was what I learned from this happening. Jai BABA!

> "To attain complete success in life, one must have mental equipoise, mental balance." - BABA

## 88. BABA'S ENLIGHTENING QUESTIONS

BABA always advised us to read HIS books regularly. I noticed one thing while I was in Jammu Kashmir in 1970. While I was in prison, I only had one of BABA's books – Subhasita Samgraha Part I. I read this book again and again for two years. Every time I read it, I felt as if I was reading it for the first time. Every time I found new, deeper meanings of each and every word. It was amazing, to see such deep knowledge contained in those words, right there in all of HIS books. I advise all readers to go through BABA's books and get the knowledge of BABA's philosophy. BABA spoke on almost every aspect of human life that you can think of. BABA gave guidance through HIS discourses to all of humanity for thousands of years to come. HIS every word, every sentence is such a huge revelation for humanity – one could take one sentence he wrote and write a voluminous book on it – indeed, this has already been done before. In the future I am sure that all the intellectuals will be taking BABA's books as the basic fundamental knowledge for all other efforts and pursuits.

One day BABA asked us a question about how to solve the water problem in Marathwada and Telangana; the drought hit areas of Central India. There are the Western Ghats, which get enormous amounts of rain during the monsoons that come from the Arabian Sea. On account of the higher Western Ghats, the rain falls on the western side of the ghats, which in turn drains all the rainwater back to the Arabian Sea. On the other side of the Western Ghats is a city, Pune (Poona). Marathwada and Telangana are in rain shadow areas due to the high Western Ghats. These areas have no water. So, BABA's question was how could one solve the water problem of the Marathwada and Telangana rain shadow areas? BABA gave us a day to think about it and the next day HE would listen to our solutions. We thought for the whole day and we all had different opinions.

The next day BABA came and asked us for the solution for the rain shadow areas. Many gave their replies but BABA was not satisfied. BABA asked me. Earlier, BABA told us that in the future water scarcity would be solved by water conservation. Planting trees and digging ponds will achieve it. These are steps to solve the water crisis in different parts of India as well as in the rest of the world. So I replied to BABA, "By digging many ponds and planting many trees, the scarcity of water in Marathwada and Telangana can be solved."

BABA said, "If there are no rains in those areas, then how can the ponds be filled and how can the trees grow?" So my reply was also not satisfying. We all surrendered to BABA and asked HIM for the right answer. BABA said, “It is very simple! When it rains on the other side of Western Ghat, preserve that water in dams, ponds and pools, then dig tunnels and send the water towards the other side of the Western Ghat, towards Pune, Marathwada and Telangana by use of canals and lift irrigation, then the whole rain shadow area will have plenty of water." Such a nice and a simple solution BABA gave to us.

BABA had so much knowledge; the Indian Government should have taken HIS ideas and applied them to develop the country. India would have become a very rich and prosperous country. Instead, the Indian Government listened to the Russian Government and kept BABA in jail, poisoned and tried to kill HIM because of HIS progressive ideas. All great personalities in the past have had to face enormous opposition due to their advanced ideas. This is history, and history repeated itself in BABA's case. BABA once said that one mistake HE made was that HE came ahead of HIS time. Because of this, HE had to suffer at the hands of the immoralists. Truly, BABA's persecution would be remembered for centuries as a black blemish upon Indian and world history.

> "It is a bigger thing to put one lesson into action than to hear many. You should put into action every lesson in your life." - BABA

# 89. BABA'S MORE INTERESTING QUESTIONS

One day BABA was giving a talk to a small gathering. Mostly they were sisters. BABA asked a question, "What will an Indian woman do when she gets money and what will a European woman do when she gets money?" Of course it was difficult for anybody to reply. Then BABA said, "The moment an Indian woman gets her hands on some surplus money she will look for a good sari and ornaments." All the sisters who were present there laughed. Even the men, who were present, also laughed because it was the men who provided the money for those saris and ornaments to be bought. Then BABA said, "The moment a European woman gets a good amount of money she will go and buy a car." There were some European sisters also sitting there. They also laughed at how BABA was pointing out their psychology.

Another day, it was workers 'reporting'. BABA asked one question about Andhra, the state from which I originally came from. BABA asked us, "What is Guntur famous for?" Guntur is a district in Andhra Pradesh. Even I couldn't reply. I may have come from the area but now that I thought about it, I didn't know what Guntur was famous for. BABA said, "Guntur is famous for ghee (pure clarified butter)." BABA said, "At the time of British rule, Guntur was exporting ghee to England and other countries". This was news to me. Later on, when I went to Andhra there I enquired about this matter. Guntur is to this day still exporting ghee. Such minute details BABA knew! There is nothing that BABA does not know. It was a great chance of enrichment of knowledge to be there interacting with HIM.

Another day, BABA was taking examination of the new Acaryas. BABA asked many questions. One question was about general knowledge, a very intriguing one. BABA asked one trainee, "Where is 'T' island?" The trainee could not reply. We also did not understand. We thought that this 'T' island might be somewhere near Australia or Indonesia, as there are many islands there. Nobody could answer BABA's question. When examination was over we asked BABA, "Where is 'T' Island?"

BABA said, "What is an Island? It is land surrounded by water. Hence it is called an island. So in the word WATER, 'T' is in the centre. The 'T' Island is in the word WATER." Such a question! Who can pass HIS exams with questions like that? But BABA finally passed and declared them to have passed the examination. HE is very kind always.

My acarya, Dada Asiimananda told me that one time he was walking with BABA when he had a very interesting question pop into his mind: what happened to disembodied minds after death? BABA replied to his mental query. "There are many minds waiting to receive a human structure," BABA said. "It is really very difficult to attain this human form. That is why everybody should spend maximum time trying to attain the Supreme Entity so that they do not waste their golden opportunity." Then BABA took out HIS stick and made a circle with it in the air, suddenly creating a rift in the middle of nowhere to show all the disembodied minds waiting to be born into a human structure. Dada saw so many, countless minds floating about in the air where BABA pointed, shaped like minidisks, some white, while others in the distance red and still others black. "The white ones are dominated by sattva guna (sentient, or saintly)," BABA said. "While the red ones by raja guna (mutative or active) and the black ones by tama (static, dull)." One should listen to BABA's wise words and make the most of this human form by utilizing it to the best of its capacity with kiirtan and meditation. We are here for a very short time. We should not waste it in petty pursuits.

> "Devotees are destined to get liberation whether they want it or not." - BABA

## 90. BABA'S ATTENTION TO KIIRTAN PARTIES

BABA emphasized two things before leaving HIS physical body:

Number one was that we should go through BABA'S books regularly and put those lessons into practice. BABA was also giving importance for doing more and more sadhana, but actually by reading HIS books the desire to devote more time in meditations comes naturally. HIS teachings emphasise on constant inner desire to realize the Supreme Self. Reading HIS books and doing sadhana is of prime importance.

Number two was that we should do maximum kiirtan, singing BABA NAM KEVALAM. By doing kiirtan, the LORD is attracted to come closer to us. The Lord is everywhere, all the time, but when kiirtan is done the Lord shifts HIS nucleus to the place where HIS devotees are singing HIS name. Hence kiirtan makes everyone holy because of HIS presence.

Every morning all of the workers would do three hours Akhanda Kiirtan and then would conduct Dharma Cakra (collective meditation). This created a good flow amongst the reporting session. BABA was also very happy to know about our routine. When BABA NAM KEVALAM is sung continuously for three hours, it is called Akhanda Kiirtan. Akhanda Kiirtan generates a tremendous spiritual flow; it establishes everyone present there in a very high state of spiritual bliss.

Whenever an Akhanda Kiirtan was held in any part of India or even any part of the world, BABA wanted report about it. BABA knows everything, yet BABA wanted everything to be reported to strengthen and establish the system which may continue for a very long time.

All the time BABA was planning and thinking about how to make the system continue effectively for thousands of years to come. HIS planning cannot be understood by ordinary minds.

BABA informed us that HE would start giving personal contact again to margiis, but only if they arrived singing kiirtan all the way from where they originated (at this time BABA was extremely busy and unfortunately only had the time to grant personal contacts to LFT's and acarayas). BABA called this program "Kiirtan Parikrama." This message was conveyed to all the margiis all over India.

Kiirtan parties started coming pouring in groups, singing kiirtan all through the streets, in trains and buses before finally finishing their long journeys. Then the report would be given to BABA that a kiirtan party had arrived from a particular place, and then they were granted personal contacts.

Once, a kiirtan party came while we were in the middle of a 'reporting' session. BABA said, "Kiirtan becomes more rhythmic by playing drums with rhythmic tunes. It seems that a kiirtan party has come from Mumbai, as they have special tunes and a special style of singing and beating of the drums." We went outside and saw that margiis had come from Mumbai singing kiirtan all the way from Mumbai to Calcutta, thousands of kilometers away. BABA gave personal contact to all of them.

> "I exist, my Lord exists, and my search for his glamour, my search for HIS grandeur, is never-ending." - BABA

# PART - XI

# BABA'S LAST DAYS ON THE EARTH

### 91. MEETING BABA IN THE HOSPITAL

It was in the month of October 1990 and BABA was still in the hospital when I went to see HIM. This time BABA was there for a second time. It was very rare that BABA changed the natural course of anything. Also, it is impossible to know how many people's bad samskaras BABA was carrying the burden of. HE did not want HIS children to suffer.

I went to see BABA in the hospital. This was my last personal meeting with HIM, but of course I didn't know that at that time. I thought that HIM would stay with us physically for a much longer time. But HE knew that it was my last personal meeting with HIM.

I entered BABA's room in the hospital and did Sastanga Pranam. BABA was on HIS bed with HIS PA Dada sitting nearby. PA Dada told BABA that I had come to have HIS Darshan. "A great pandit has come," he said. BABA kept me in the Global Microvita Steering Committee and I was also conducting regular studies, examinations and classes for all the Indian workers every month during 'reporting,' so in that context PA was telling BABA that a great pandit had come.

In front of BABA, we are all just lilliputs (dwarfs). BABA is the source of all our knowledge. But still, BABA jokingly added to the praise by saying, "Yes, a great pandit has come." BABA asked me to come closer and blessed me by keeping HIS hands on my head for a long time. These were the last blessings HE gave me. It was so soothing and so blissful. I was feeling so elevated for several days.

My posting at that time was in Gurukul (a university BABA founded) as the Principal of the Medical College at Ananda Nagar and I was also posted as the Superintendent of the Hospital in Ananda Nagar. After giving me blessings, BABA asked me personally, "Go through all my latest books and discourses and spread them all over the world." BABA blessed me again, and then I did one final Sastanga Pranam and left HIS room.

I remained in India for many years after BABA's Mahaprayan (Great Departure). I did not understand at the time why BABA was asking me to spread all HIS latest discourses until recently. Now after reading HIS biopsychology book, Microvita and PROUT books I have been trying to conduct seminars in the universities of Sweden, Germany, Poland, Holland, Finland, and Denmark etc. Perhaps BABA has a bigger plan to spread some of HIS ideas. Actually, only HE does all the work, but HE always gives all the credit to HIS sons and daughters. BABA is only helping us to become good. We are all moving from imperfection to perfection; keeping HIM as the goal of our lives, we should shun all our weaknesses and go ahead with our Mission.

> "Use Guru Mantra always before starting any activity. If you forget, do it after completing the action." - BABA

## 92. BABA'S 'VARABHAYA MUDRA' BLESSING

In India there are two major festivals in the months of September and October – Dushera and Diipavali. Both of these festivals are widely celebrated all over the country, and by Indians living abroad as well, by illuminating lights everywhere to indicate the victory of light over darkness. These festivals serve as symbols to remind one and all that the evil forces, however powerful they might be, would one day certainly meet their tragedy. These festivals started in the ancient day when criminal persons - "demons" - were punished or killed by great personalities like Lord Shiva or Lord Krishna. They brought great relief to the suffering humanity those days.

In Ananda Marga BABA also gave importance to these festivals, because the ideas behind them are good. "Remember that light, not darkness, is verily the truth in life," BABA once said. On these festival days BABA would give general darshan to many devotees who would turn up from all over India to celebrate. BABA also wrote some Prabhat Samgiit songs about these festivals.

Varabhaya Mudra was something that BABA would give invariably on the occasion of Dharma Maha Cakras. Every year these DMC's would be arranged in different places, but without exception they were organized on New Year's as well as on BABA's birthday in the month of May (Ananda Purnima). In the year 1990, however, BABA gave Varabhaya Mudra blessings on Dushera and Diipavali days as well in Calcutta.

One day BABA was telling a joke, "You know, I never miss any Dharma Maha Cakra." Of course BABA was the one who would conduct these DMC's, so HE was joking to make everyone laugh, but also to inspire others to attend. DMC's were so important – BABA's Mudra created such a vibrating spiritual flow – everyone felt that they had been given a new life, a new spirit. BABA knew the importance of these DMC's.

We did not know that these would be the last times that BABA would physically give us HIS Varabhaya Mudra blessings. How interesting it is that the last time BABA ever gave HIS mudra was during two days that HE would never otherwise give blessings and messages. It was one of HIS final messages to us, and also one of HIS most meaningful. BABA gave HIS blessings in both months of September and October 1990, and I was there on both occasions.

> "Always be prepared to *serve* the Ma'rga. For the sake of the ma'rga ideology do not be sparing, even of your life. Remember, in laying down one's life with a great ideal in mind, Moksa is inevitable. This is the reward of death in the war of Dharma." - BABA

### 93. BABA PREPARING TO LEAVE HIS BODY

Earlier BABA used to come everyday to preside over the workers' reporting, but now because of ill health, HE discontinued coming everyday. Only the organisational Heads were taking our reports and they were conveying them to BABA. BABA was listening to the reports from inside HIS room only. BABA was preparing the workers and the organization so that it could run in the absence of HIS physical presence smoothly.

People spend their whole lives thinking about how to establish themselves in their lifetimes, but great personalities like Lord Shiva, Lord Krishna and BABA Anandamurti think and work for the future, because their physical body is here on this planet for only a very short, limited time. But, once a proper system or Ideology is established, it would help and guide the suffering humanity for thousands of years.

The day before BABA's Mahaprayan (physical departure), BABA asked all the workers to assemble in HIS Quarters and then administered a special oath to all of us. The oath's inner meaning was that we would work and utilise all our energies for the betterment of the society till the last breath of our lives. BABA wanted us that we should work with the same speed for the all round development of society in HIS physical absence as we had been working during HIS physical presence. We were not aware that BABA was preparing the ground to leave HIS physical body. Had we gotten the hint, we would have done everything possible to beg HIM to stay in HIS physical body for a longer time. After taking our collective oath, BABA then recited a poem of Rabindranath Tagore's about how on the eve of HIS departure, HE gave clarion call to one and all to fight against the evil forces. The poem was also about how one should not mourn at the time of this Great Departure, but celebrate. Still none of us could understand HE was so clearly giving us.

BABA gave an indication to HIS PA that HE would leave HIS physical body, but BABA's PA refused to hear such things anymore from HIM. BABA changed the subject, but it is obvious that BABA was preparing PA's mind for the happening.

Later, BABA gave very clear hints as to who may be considered faithful to the Mission in the future. BABA told HIS PA that those who followed tour program and those who maintained financial integrity were the only trustworthy candidates of HIS Mission. This means that those who accumulate enormous wealth without the knowledge of the organisation, and those who make their own tour programs without the permission of the Central administration, can no longer be considered BABA's messengers. This was also an indication for the future of the Mission. After all, once BABA was not there physically, how could one know who was who and what was what? Who stood for HIM and who is working against the Mission?

The entire universe moves according to HIS will only. BABA's Mission shall be established as per HIS wishes, and not a single force on earth can check its progress.

> "Remember, until every single person in the universe accepts the A'nanda' Marga' ideology, you have no opportunity to rest." - BABA

### 94. BABA'S PHYSICAL DEPARTURE

In the month of May, BABA made a program all at once to proceed to Ananda Nagar, Pururlia, from Calcutta, West Bengal. One early morning BABA just got up and ordered everyone to make all the necessary travel arrangements to proceed by car to Ananda Nagar, Purulia.

Later we came to know that the day BABA was supposed to leave, two days after HE actually did, that is, BABA's quarters was attacked by Communist hooligans. Not only that, but there was also an attack on a white car just near HIS quarters – a car that looked just like BABA's car. The person in it was killed. The Communists attacked, assuming that BABA was still in the HIS quarters and that HE might be escaping by car. BABA's early trip thus exposed the conspiracy of the Communists.

Once HE arrived at Ananda Nagar, HIS mood completely changed. In Calcutta, BABA's mood was very serious but in Ananda Nagar HIS mood changed completely. We were also happy because we knew that such a happy mood was good for HIS health. But again BABA had to move to Calcutta.

In the month of October 1990 BABA was again admitted to the hospital in Calcutta. After this incident, I thought that the only way to make BABA happy was to do non-stop Akhanda Kiirtan. In the past I did one year non stop kiirtan in Sweden Training Center in the 70's. Kiirtan will make BABA happy so I mobilized all the workers and started an all day and all night Akhanda Kiirtan in the main hall of our Central Office in Calcutta. BABA was happy to know about it. Eventually BABA was released from the hospital and came back to HIS quarters, and still the Akhanda Kiirtan was going on.

AT AROUND 3 PM (15.00 HRS) ON OCTOBER 21^ST 1990 BABA LEFT US ALL.

BABA HAD ALREADY LEFT HIS PHYSICAL BODY. IT WAS BABA'S MAHAPRAYAN – HIS GREAT DEPARTURE.

BABA's body was preserved from the 21^st to the 26^th of October so that all the workers and margiis from all over the world could see HIS physical body. The kiirtan continued for all six days until cremation. The cremation was done within our Tiljala campus in Calcutta.

> "The one and the same Supreme Consciousness is being manifested as limitless individual entities, in countless mental receptacles, in countless minds." - BABA

### 95. BABA WAS EXTREMELY POSITIVE

In spite of all odds and struggle, opposition BABA was extremely positive all the time. HE always blessed us by saying "victory is yours". BABA had given us enough spirit to fight against evil forces. HE had full confidence that a real team, a trusted group of workers were already prepared to carry on the struggle against the Communist-backed workers who became traitors and turned against BABA'S Mission. And for what? For posts and positions? For funds accumulated from years of being in the center of the administration? For narrow geo and socio-sentiments? All petty, all narrow, all self-centered workers left Mission.

One of BABA's Missions was to create polarization. With BABA no longer physically present, it only makes sense that Ananda Marga would polarize between Universal, service-minded sentiments and narrow, selfish-minded sentiments. It all comes down to a clash of ideologies. This is the new phase in Ananda Marga.

I feel that BABA wanted to expedite this process of polarization. Rather, BABA always let things take their natural course. What has happened since HIS Mahaprayan is the natural course when you consider what had been going on behind the scenes for so many years – what will also be the natural course will be the victory of Universal Ideology over narrow sentiments, of light over darkness, of good over evil. BABA wants to show the whole world that this is the only truth. As BABA said when HE was released from jail, "You have heard of the victory of Dharma in the past, you are seeing the victory of Dharma in the present and you will see the victory of Dharma in the future."

GREAT IS BABA! WONDERFUL IS HIS PLAY!

> "I have already told you, HE cannot hate you - you may hate HIM if you so desire, but HE will never be able to hate you."
> - BABA

---

### 96. SUPPRESSION, OPPRESSION & RECOGNITION

BABA took HIS advent on this Earth with new progressive ideas. Humanity has been suffering for thousands of years under the exploitative rule of narrow sentiments, faiths, dogmas etc. HE came on this Earth to give a new way of life for every individual in the society.

BABA had to face opposition from the very beginning of HIS life. When BABA was a small child, he used to recite Lord Shiva's Dhyan Mantra very fluently. The priests were astonished to hear HIM and were curious to know what BABA might do in the future.

They hired the local astrologers to predict BABA's future. The astrologers came to the conclusion that BABA's name would be known all over the world. All the religions of the world would be vanished. They said, and there would remain only one Human religion, one Human society which HE would establish on this earth.

They planned and hired some hooligans to kill HIM just as HE was a small boy. They tied a gasoline-soaked cloth around his abdomen and set it on fire. Due to the smoke and heat, the attackers ran away, assuming that HE had died. BABA was only unconscious. On waking up, HE was able to walk all the way back to HIS parents, HIS insides being held in HIS hands. HE nearly died from the incident, but BABA was able to eventually recover. HE carried the scars with HIM for the rest of HIS life. This was only the beginning of HIS suppression – when BABA started Ananda Marga, it got worse.

In 1967 communists wanted to kill BABA and all the inmates at the Ananda Nagar Center situated in Purulia, West Bengal, India. An enormous mob was organized for the attack eventually killing five unarmed Dadas. They wanted to decimate Ananda Nagar and Ananda Marga once for all. BABA did a special mudra

and all the goons who came with their deadly weapons to kill all the inmates of Ananda Nagar took to their heels and ran away. BABA protected all by HIS special blessings.

The suppression on BABA and Ananda Marga continued, putting BABA in jail for long periods. The attempt to kill BABA in jail failed. He was released in spite of the opposition from Communist governments. The evil forces wanted to stop the spread of BABA's ideas and Ananda Marga by oppressing it, but they had only succeeded in helping it spread. Communists are still going on at their same old game, this time supporting expelled workers of Calcutta group. They are bound to fail one day.

In the 'recognition' phase, there is first a recognition of IISTA i.e. BABA, then comes the recognition of HIS ideology, which is for the all-round development of humanity. Thus, people will come to recognize HIS organization, Ananda Marga, which is the carrier of that Ideology.

When BABA was released from jail, BABA said that the organization would become very small once more, due to an internal emergency, and then afterwards the organization shall expand and would be spread all over the globe. This is the phase of recognition we are all passing through, no one should worry about it.

The only way to get through all these dramas is to hold on to HIS feet. One day BABA said, "Whenever people come nearer to me, I pretend to drive away." “Oh, BABA!” The margiis present there cried out, “If YOU drive us away, where shall we go? YOU are our only shelter, the ultimate abode."

Those who are real devotees hold on HIS lotus feet until their last breath, they are most intelligent devotees who depend only upon HIM, their future is well protected and safe for ever.

“If you move along the path of righteousness with the spirit of Universal humanity, victory is yours. The blessing of *Parama Purus'a* is always with you." - BABA

# 97. INDIA CAN'T RECOGNISE ITS OWN GREATNESS

In the year 1968, BABA went to the Philippines and had an official state reception there. Though, BABA was born in India, but was never given due respect or recognition, rather, met unkindly treatment all HIS life. HE was suppressed and oppressed in all possible ways by the then government of India and the state government of West Bengal.

The government of Philippines was busy in making all possible arrangements to receive HIM as a state guest. When BABA was about to leave India for a trip to Philippines, HE spoke at the airport about two great personalities: Rabindranath Tagore and Swami Vivekananda.

Swami Vivekananda tried to spread the philosophy of Advaita Vedanta (Non dualistic philosophy) but had to face severe opposition from dogmatic pundits of India. He was a victim of utter criticism even in the state of West Bengal where he was born. It was all the grace of Almighty and his Guru that he delivered historical speech in Chicago, USA in the World Religious Conference and the entire world gave him due recognition and ovation after this incidence Swami Vivekananda was recognized by the Indian people as a great personality.

The greatest poet of the age, Rabindranath Tagore wrote Giitanjali, profound devotional songs. But in India, his own country, the Hindu priests considered the verses of Giitanjali as anti-religious. Later, Rabindranath Tagore's poems were translated into English and many other languages. It was Giitanjali that brought him the greatest laurel of the Nobel Prize. He was the first Indian to be awarded with a Nobel Prize. He was then accepted as the Greatest Poet of the age by the people of his country.

Similar is the case with Ananda Marga which was banned in India, and BABA was put in jail in India but all the other countries

of the world gave due recognition to BABA and granted recognition to Ananda Marga as well. Ananda Marga (AMURT) is also a member of the United Nations as a Non Governmental Organisation. Even Proutist Universal, a socio-economic organization has also become a member of the United Nations as a Non Governmental Organisation.

The Indian government under the pressure of Russian and the government of West Bengal approached the United Nations not to recognize Ananda Marga. Contrary to it, the United Nations did not pay any attention to it by saying that it had considered the recognition of Ananda Marga with the UNO on the basis of the recognition of Ananda Marga in other countries of Africa, Europe, Japan, USA, Australia, and Canada. Indian government failed in their approach. Ananda Marga has expanded enormously so much that it is beyond the capacities of either Russian or Indian government to check its growth and spread world wide.

BABA once said, "The East will accept Ananda Marga due to its economic crises and West will accept Ananda Marga due to its psychic problems." The West is suffering from disastrous psychic problems. The only solution is to take up spirituality and universal ideas of Neo-Humanistic philosophy of Ananda Marga.

"The humans of today are possessed of spirited intellect and accomplished wisdom...

I call upon all enlightened people and say: 'Go ahead with courage. Humanity has been awaiting your establishment in the excellence and grandeur of glory.'" - BABA

### 98. BABA, THE NUCLEUS OF THE UNIVERSE

In 1981 I was working in South America, there I came to know one very interesting story from a Dada who met a world famous parapsychologist on a plane while traveling from Caracas, Venezuela (South America) to Miami, Florida, USA. When the flight took off, Dada started performing his meditation.

Suddenly a man introduced himself, saying that he was parapsychologist and gave him his card. A chat continued. The parapsychologist asked Dada as to what he was doing. Dada replied that he was meditating. The parapsychologist said that he was a consultant to NASA, which launches spaceships for America, as well as a consultant for Presidents of different countries who came to him to know about their future

"I felt a very powerful vibration on the plane just before," the parapsychologist told Dada. "So I got up to find its source and found you meditating." He asked if Dada had a picture of his Master he could see. Dada only had with him a special photograph of BABA in Varabhaya Mudra, which he could not show to everybody. Dada explained that to the parapsychologist.

"I don't need to see it," he replied. "I'll close my eyes and can feel the photo with my fingers for a few seconds." Dada agreed and took out the photo of BABA in Varabhaya Mudra. The parapsychologist put his hand to BABA's photo and remained silent for several seconds.

"All my life," he said, "I've been searching for the source on the earth from where all the events of the universe are getting controlled. At last, I have finally found it today when I felt BABA's photo." BABA was the source from which all the things were evolving and getting controlled. The parapsychologist also learnt meditation and started to meditate. Great is BABA!

*"Taraka Brahma* (Liberating God) forms a desire to come on earth with a specific mission of restoring Dharma (righteousness) by launching a ceaseless fight against all injustice and sin." - BABA

### 99. WHY BABA, KRISHNA & SHIVA CAME TO INDIA

LORD SHIVA, LORD KRISHNA and BABA ANANDAMURTI, are the universal personalities who took advent on this earth.

The blending of all the races and groups of people had not happened in any other part of the world as extensively as it has happened in India, although Canada, Europe, America etc. are now gradually approaching to that level. This blending process in India, however, started twelve thousand years ago. The Aryans migrated to India from Europe, Mongolians migrated to India from the East, Africans from the South and then there were the original inhabitants, the Austrics. All these four races blended because of the great work of Lord Shiva.

Lord Shiva came to India because India was the only place, heavily populated at the time, and it was also where racial tensions were reaching their peaks; they needed solutions to overcome their racial hatred. Even so, Lord Shiva did not stay only in India, but traveled all over the world and spread HIS universal ideas amongst all the human beings of that age.

Lord Krishna came to India because the vast majority of the population of earth was concentrated in India. India was divided into hundreds of kingdoms, and between each of these kingdoms there were selfish-minded infightings. Lord Krishna also had to come to India as the universal spirit could easily be awakened where the blending process of races and cultures had already been going on for several thousands of years. Lord Krishna continued and gave a push to the same process that Lord Shiva had started.

BABA Anandamurti also came to India due to many reasons. In India there is a widespread sense of inferiority, a country subjugated and exploited by foreign invaders for nearly one thousand of years. India is one of the most densely populated places on the earth at the same time. Indians are of very universal outlook, devotional oriented due to the influence of the Lord Shiva and Lord Krishna. Tantra originated in India; it was easier for the people of India to accept the tradition and culture, which was based on spirituality and morality.

India has many languages, cultures and faiths, weave a social synthesis due to the universal, spiritual touch of Lord Shiva, Krishna and BABA Anandamurti.

India is already set on the path of progress based on universal ideas. India has faith in Lord Shiva and Krishna. India could easily bring a change in the world because of its deep faith in spirituality and its conception of love, compassion, faith and ultimate trust in the Infinite Consciousness, the Supreme goal.

India has a great role to play to establish one human society. BABA knew ahead of time how little time HE had physically on this earth, so HE chose a place where HE could best sow the seeds, where there was a congenial environment for the universal plant to grow. These plants will grow and provide mankind with the fruits of peace for thousands of years to come.

India has blended the cultural matrix for thousands of years and stands for the spirit of universalism. Hence, BABA took birth in India, worked in India and made India as HIS working center. Let the entire world understand the noble task to establish universalism and the Mission of spirituality.

> "The time that the people of Bharat (India) had left over from work, they utilized in psychic and spiritual pursuits." - BABA

# 100. BABA'S ORGANISATION – ANANDA MARGA

BABA once said, "I have come on this earth to set right everything."

This entire universe is HIS Liila (Cosmic play). Now Tamoguna is dominating the earth. Tamoguna (static force), the crude material force is dominating, influencing and controlling the people of this planet. Good people, those controlled by Sattvaguna, are neglected and uncared but they are not united. They are helpless. BABA came on this Earth to unite all the good people, Sattvaguna dominated people, sentient dominated people and finally help them to establish a universal, cosmic society by bringing them on upper position and limelight.

Nowadays in different organizations or political parties or the governments, people like to occupy higher posts. Many eligible institutions were exploited in the past. There was a vast difference of high and low. But in Ananda Marga, the power is evenly distributed in all levels. Ananda Marga is based on the principle of "Atma Moks'artham jagat hitayaca" - Salvation to the Self and service to the humanity.

The real power exists with the lower levels because one is in direct contact with the people. The post or power in the higher levels is only to supervise that everything is followed according to the accepted system. But the actual materialization takes place in the lower levels. It means that BABA has shifted the power from higher to lower levels and the lower level power to higher levels. This is a big change in the conception of organization or power. This is the natural course.

Cosmic Mind is operating this universe because it is uni-purpose (to help all to develop physically, mentally and spiritually) and multi-lateral (creating the entire universe). Similarly Ananda Marga is also uni-purpose and a multi-dimensional organization guiding in all aspects of human life, like social, spiritual, economic, cultural, in fields of music, arts, education, literature, science, ecology, medicine etc. This is possible only when such a great personality like BABA is the founder of this noble Mission, Ananda Marga. HE will guide the Mission for all time to come.

BABA once said, “I have sown the seeds of struggle in the organization. It will grow by clash and cohesion.” The clashes between baser and higher thoughts remove the impurities of the individuals and thus take the whole organization to higher and higher levels constantly.

BABA once said that HE has considered almost all the constitutions and has picked up the best from amongst them to form the Ananda Marga. Hence Ananda Marga will suit the people of all countries even the plants and animal lives. Ananda Marga is the eternal path and will remain forever to guide all as it is based on fundamental cosmic principles.

> "Do not allow distinctions to crop up among Ananda Margiis. Unity should be maintained even at the risk of one's life. Do not under any circumstances allow individual interest to stand in the way of collective interest." - BABA

## 101. BABA AND THE UNIVERSE

The Supreme Father is the controller of the entire cosmological order. Whatever HE thinks is the law and whatever HE wishes has to happen.

One day one Margii was walking with BABA. He was thinking in his mind, "Why hasn't BABA established the Sadvipra Samaj as yet? If BABA wants HE can establish it within seconds. We are subjected to all these sufferings in absence of a Sadvipra Samaj."

Immediately BABA shot that Parama Purusa would act only at proper time. One will have to wait with patience and trust in HIM. Parama Purusa, if so desires can create several such universes in a matter of few seconds. Establishing Sadvipra Samaj is not a big problem for Parama Purusa.

Once one margii brother was concerned because he was not able to raise sufficient funds for publishing Ananda Marga books which was badly needed then. BABA spoke to him and assured him that no work of Ananda Marga whether it be publication of books, or running a school or a service project can suffer for want of money. The progress of Dharma can never be delayed for anything material. It is arrangement of the Parama Purusa that for any noble cause arrangement is made in advance for its materialization in HIS mind.

Once BABA told that Lord Buddha camped at Jamalpur, BABA's birth place. Many kings came to him with huge amount of gold and precious pearls and gems and offered them to Lord Buddha. As he was a Bhikshu i.e., someone who begs, so he couldn't deny the offers that would have hurt their feelings. So he accepted them all and finally while leaving the place Buddha ordered to bury them all in the foot of the hills near Jamalpur.

The margii brother curiously wanted to know the location where was such a huge quantities of the treasure was lying. BABA replied immediately that such worldly things may spoil many of the sadhakas so whatever is required the Parama Purusa would provide you and at the same time no progress in your pursuits would suffer in want of money. This is the greatest blessing of BABA that we feel at every step that money has never been a reason to cause a hindrance in our progress of materialization of a project.

> "He too feels the inner agony of despairing people in the core of HIS heart, and shows them the soothing path of enlightenment." - BABA

### 102. BABA’S MISSION ON 51 PLANETS OF UNIVERSE

This Universe is so vast. There are billions of galaxies, each of which contains billions of stars; each star is placed at a distance of several light years from each other. Each star has its own solar system with many many planets revolving around it. We have not yet known about all the planets and sub planets of this solar system. It is a known fact by now based upon scientific findings that there are several planets and other solar systems with human habitants.

We were amazed to learn from BABA directly that HE told us that in many many planets far from our solar system, there are people who are thousand times advanced people than us in all respects. They are developed in matters of science and technology, culture and arts, interestingly they are spiritually also highly developed. Such planets may be more than 50 in number. We may presume that BABA's Mission is already established in those planets.

Once I was performing meditation at the Sweden Training Center, where non-stop 24-hours kiirtan was continuously going on for over a year (365 days). A very pleasing spiritual vibration was created which others also equally felt. My meditation was very deep. I felt all of a sudden that my mind had left this physical body and moving somewhere very fast. I felt that I suddenly stopped near an unknown planet. There it looked as if there were nearly several million people lived on that planet. I could feel that all of them had only one objective in their minds i.e., to realize Param Purusa. One more speciality is that their thoughts were all similar and well known to each other. On different occasions BABA gave different informations about other planets and about the people living there.

I suppose that already on 51 planets spirituality has been established and now it is the turn of this earth that spirituality and Sadvipra Samaj shall be established very soon. This is the reason that BABA took HIS advent on this earth.

> "The existential nucleus of a single individual is directly linked to the *controlling* point of the Cosmic Nucleus or the cosmological *order... the* final and Supreme goal is to make one's individual existential nucleus coincide with the Cosmic Existential Nucleus." - BABA

# PART - XII

# BABA SAVIOUR & LOVING FATHER

### 103. BABA-TARAKA BRAHMA-SUPREME LIBERATOR

BABA is the Supreme Liberator. HE took advent on this earth for only one purpose, one motto i.e., to liberate all. Whosoever takes shelter in HIM can get rid of the influence of all the bondages (the Maya, Prakriti).

From the very inception of the civilization, man has been trying to ascend into higher states of Supreme Consciousness. The material influence was so deep on the primitive human mind. He became a natural worshipper, worshipping the sun, the moon, the fire, the stones or he worshipped imaginary gods or goddesses.

These practices could elevate the common man only to a very limited advancement in spiritual fields. In course of time man made some progress and spiritual scriptures in form of Vedas came up. These could not give satisfaction on spiritual level. The real thurst can be quenched by a spiritual realizations only. For that the Taraka Brahma as Lord Shiva, Lord Krshna and BABA took advents to lead the entire Human society on the path of spirituality.

Taraka Brahmas empowers, words with strong spiritual power and makes them SIDDHA MANTRAS (spiritual words for chanting). Whosoever repeats these Siddha Mantras can get liberated because these Mantras have come directly from HIM..

Lord Shiva gave some Siddha Mantras, Lord Krishna gave some Siddha Mantras and Lord Anandamurthi (BABA) also gave certain Siddha Mantras. These Siddha Mantras one can learn from HIS Representatives who are called Acaryas. One should learn Siddha Mantras. This is what is meant whenever someone is "initiated."

In the process of initiation, the Kula Kundalini is awakened by the power of Taraka Brahma. Acaryas are working all over the world, so no matter where one might live, one may get in touch with them. They are in every city. One simply needs the zeal and interest to learn and practice sincerely.

These Siddha Mantras are taught individually and have to be kept secret, but BABA gave one Siddha Mantra, which for the first time was permitted to utter collectively. This Siddha Mantra is BABA NAM KEVALAM. It is the eight-syllable Siddha Mantra. One may meditate on this mantra or sing it, collectively or individually.

There are so many gurus, so many masters. What is the difference between all these Gurus and Taraka Brahma?

1. Taraka Brahma is a born Guru, a born Master, is born liberated. Whereas other Gurus have their own Guru from whom they learn and practice, they are not born Masters. They also practice to get liberated. They are not fully established in Pure Consciousness, in the highest cakra, the Sahasrara, but Taraka Brahma is. HE is always in that

state of perfection, in trance, hence people used to think that Lord Shiva was using drugs, but that is not true, HE was in Samadhi, in ecstasy. Taraka Brahma is verily Jagad Guru – the Guru of the Universe.

2. Taraka Brahma leads a family life. He is not an ascetic saint like other Gurus. HE leads a normal life because HE has to set an example for family people, showing that when one is living a family life, one can achieve liberation as well. HE acts as much like an ordinary human being as possible, but HIS every action is superhuman. A very lucky few ever understand who HE is during HIS physical advent.

3. Taraka Brahma touches all aspects of human life like spirituality, philosophy, social problems, economic problems, culture, Arts, literature, music, etc. HE gives solutions for all problems. HE gives guidance for humanity to evolve physically, mentally and spiritually. Whereas other Gurus spend their whole lives covering basic spiritual topics, Taraka Brahma reaches deep into every sphere of human life.

4. Taraka Brahma comes with a Great Mission: to establish Dharma (righteousness). All of humanity is eventually polarized into two opposing camps because of HIS coming, and a fight between them is inevitable. Dharma always comes out victorious in the end, thus Taraka Brahma's advent signals the dawn of a new age of white peace.

5. Taraka Brahma teachings and ideas get materialized after HIS physical departure because they are meant for thousands of years; hence one lifetime is not enough for people to understand Taraka Brahma. Ordinary Gurus become popular and fall down also rapidly, their goals are short-term and for few people and few places and few years. Taraka Brahma remains in the hearts of all forever, so He guides all for thousands and thousands of years to come. HE IS FOR ALL PEOPLE, ALL THE TIME AND FOR ALL PLACES.

> "The Supreme Entity who controls this universe is *Parama Purusa*; HE is also the nucleus of the cosmological order." - BABA

---

## 104. BABA A FATHER & MASTER OF THE UNIVERSE

BABA as Taraka Brahma is the father of all. This entire creation is HIS psychic projection, HIS mental thought wave only. All human beings are HIS children. We are all one, as our father is ONE and so we belong to one universal family.

All the wealth of the world is concentrated in few hands and as a result, the majority of people are suffering. This is the root cause of all our economic problems – misutilization and over-accumulation of physical wealth. The only solution to the present economic crisis is to accept BABA's system of PROUT. The Supreme Father is the owner of the entire world, all its wealth. We, as HIS children, have to organize the wealth in a collective way, so that we may solve all our economic and social problems collectively. This is the only solution for the present crisis. As we have the right to live, so all plants and animals also have their own right to live. The Supreme Father is the Father of all the birds, animals and humans. HE is the Master of the universe; the Lord of all the Lords.

Whatever BABA did in HIS lifetime was just to create the spiritual waves for the future to be materialized. When Lord Shiva came, it took 2000 years for the people to believe and accept Lord Shiva as the Lord of Lords. When the Indus valley civilization and the Chinese civilization were built twenty-five generations later, only then did people could realize that Lord Shiva was the founder of the civilizations. Similarly, when Lord Krishna came, it took two hundred years for people to realize that it was Lord Krishna who built the peaceful society they were living in. Three generations after Lord Krishna's lifetime, people came to realize the greatness of Lord Krishna.

Now, BABA came on this earth. HE gave solutions for all the problems — social, economic, political, educational, cultural, spiritual etc. When people materialize HIS ideas, out of necessity, and when people benefit from HIS ideas, then maybe after twenty

years, or after two generations, people will start believing that BABA as the Lord of the universe.

# 106. SURRENDER TO BABA

BABA is the dearest and nearest one — the most beloved friend and guide. HE knows our mind, our heart, our intellect and our soul. So HE knows us better than we know ourselves. Why then should we think about ourselves? The best way to lead this life is to surrender unto HIM — a total surrender. The only thing a devotee is required to do.

BABA's ways are unique. HE would place things in HIS own ways. One day BABA asked some of the workers present there as to how many languages we knew. Usually in India, everyone knows at least three languages. BABA said, "It is very good, you should try to learn local languages because it will help in pracar (propagation). You should learn more languages for your convenience." Then BABA said, "You all know so many languages but I know only one language. Do you know what that language is?"

Then BABA said, "I know only one language and that is the language of the mind, the language of the heart. Whatever we think HE knows about it, HE is the knower of everything. This can be realized only when some one develops pure devotion for BABA.

One day BABA told us a story of three fishes. There were many ponds in a village. One day one fisherman put a net in one of the ponds and picked up many fishes in his net. He carried his catches merrily while walking towards his home, he told his plans to a friend that he would put the net in the nearby pond.

This was overheard by the leader fishes in that pond. She called a meeting of all the fishes. There lived one fish with thousand kinds of ideas and intellect, the second fish with a hundred intellects, still another fish was there which had only one idea or intellect. The fish having thousand kinds of intellect thought, "No matter let the fisherman come and try his net. I have thousands of ways to escape, I will never be caught in his

net." The second fish having hundred-intellect also thought, "I have one hundred intellects, so I have one hundred ways to escape from his net." It also decided to stay in the pond. The fish having one idea or intellect thought, "I have only one intellect, only one thought. This thought advises me to escape, as the fisherman is going to put the net tomorrow. I will be caught. I'll move into the pond where he already put his net so I won't get caught." So she jumped into the pond where the fisherman had already put his net. Thereby when the fisherman threw his net into the pond as per his plan both the fishes having thousand kinds of intellect and hundred kinds of intellect were caught along with other fishes. Only the fish who had one intellect could escape.

Similarly, those who have one pointed thought towards the Supreme will always escape from the bondages of mind (100 propensities and 1000 expressions) and would finally get salvation and attain the Supreme. Those who would apply hundred or thousand kinds of their intellect will remain stuck up in the net of the Maya or the Avidya (ignorance).

> "The light of new ideas shall certainly illumine every nook and corner of human habitation, in spite of the inroads of countless forces of *denunciation*, denigration and contempt." - BABA

### 107. BABA'S ADVENT TO GUIDE ALL

BABA came on this earth to make one realize that HE is with each and every individual and everything. The truth is that there is no difference between Parama Purusa, the Brahma and the Maha Sambhuti who alone is the Guru – the BABA. HE is the ultimate liberator.

One day, BABA was explaining about Lord Krishna and the Mahabharata. BABA asked us if we wanted to become a great hero like Arjuna of the Mahabharata or a great devotee like Sudhama, a childhood friend of Lord Krishna. Some expressed that they would like to become a great warrior, fighter like Arjuna and some wanted to become a great devotee like Sudhama. Some said that they would like to become both at the same time. BABA said, "Very intelligent." But then HE continued, "But do you know what I want you all to be? I want that you all should become neither Arjuna nor Sudhama - I want that you all should become Lord Krishna."

BABA wants us to realize that our own Unit Self is the reflection of that Supreme Self. It is simply a matter of realization. When the devotee reaches the status of "I am the Brahma" then he realizes the Supreme truth. BABA has come to make us realize this truth since HE is the THE LORD OF THE LORDS.

#### ANANDA NAGAR – A MODEL ECO VILLAGE

BABA created a model eco-village in the Purulia district of West Bengal, India, and lovingly named it "ANANDA NAGAR" (city of bliss). The place was once considered as most neglected and unfit to be developed was turned into a paradise and a model Master Unit. It has a very developed system of its own with a number of educational institutions, training centers, relief and welfare centers, developed fruit orchards, different kinds of plants, farming system. All the projects are unique in themselves.

This has become a model and an ideal place and seat of learning. A dry village of Purulia has now turned into a model eco-village where farming, animal husbandry, bee-keeping, cotton plantations, weaving industries and many other rural industries have been started to provide employment to the local tribal villages.

#### GURUKUL - NEO HUMANISTIC EDUCATIONAL CENTRE

In Ananda Nagar many faculties of education were opened to educate the local tribal people. The education system is to develop everyone physically, mentally, morally and spiritually. SAVIDHYAYE VIMUKTAYE is the motto - Education for expansion of mind, for liberation. Gurukul Education is based on Neo Humanistic ideals where a student develops all their mental faculties and becomes a universalistic personality by not giving any importance to geo, socio, lingual, religious sentiments.

If every village of India and the whole world is organized in the pattern of Ananda Nagar then all the social, economic and cultural problems of society will be solved. Ananda Nagar is a presentable Prout Project. Similar mini Prout Projects are called Master Units. These Master Units can be found all over the world, and more are being started all the time. They are a model solution for the present crises of humanity, given by BABA HIMSELF.
