### CHRONOLOGY OF BABA'S LIFE AND ACCOMPLISHMENTS

- **1921** - BABA is born on the full-moon Purnima day in the month of May.
- **1929** - Religious extremists attempt to murder BABA as just a young boy.
- **1939** - On the full moon of August BABA initiates HIS first disciple, Kalicharan, a notorious dacoit.
- **1930's and 40's** - BABA lives in Calcutta in contact with Subhash Chandra Bose who revolted against British rule. A file is sent every day from Jamalpur to Nehru about what BABA is up to.
- **1940's and 50's** - BABA continues to secretly initiate disciples.
- **1955** - At the request of his disciples, ANANDA MARGA PRACARAKA SAMGHA is formed. BABA starts granting acarayaship to different householders to spread the Mission.
- **1958** - RENAISSANCE UNIVERSAL, an intellectual forum, is founded.
- **1959** - BABA invents PROUT (a socio-economic theory) and soon after founds PROUTIST UNIVERSAL to propagate HIS theory.
- **1960's** - Ananda Marga spreads all over India and eventually into other countries, eventually gaining over two million practitioners by the end of the decade. PROUT also gains popularity and begins to convert many of the local Communists.
- **1962** - BABA founds the SEVA DHARMA MISSION, a monastic order, and begins initiating monks and nuns into Kapalika Sadhana, never before in history taught on such a scale.
- **1963** - BABA initiates a program of MASTER UNITS, self-sufficient eco-villages. The first Master Unit, ANANDA NAGAR (The City of Bliss) is built on a 400 square km piece of land in one of the least livable, most backward areas of India. Schools, universities, orphanages, spiritual centers and charitable hospitals started, and under BABA's agricultural guidance, Ananda Nagar turns into an oasis.
- **1967** - Communists attack Ananda Nagar and kill five unarmed monks.
- **1970** - BABA founds the ANANDA MARGA UNIVERSAL RELIEF TEAM (AMURT) to do relief work worldwide. It is granted United Nations observer status, and becomes one of the first UN NGO's of third world origin. Soon after BABA founds AMURTEL, specifically targeted to the needs of women worldwide.
- **1971** - BABA is arrested under the concocted charges of abetment to murder. Defectors of Ananda Marga, including all the organisational Heads and BABA's wife, with the support of the Communist government all denounce HIM in court.
- **1973** - In February, BABA is administered enough poison to kill a horse. HE goes into a coma for three days, survives, and then demands a judicial inquiry. When none is given, BABA starts a long fast on 1st of April that continues for over five years. This was the first of the attempts made on HIS life in prison.
- **1975** - Ananda Marga and all the organisations started by BABA are banned on 4th of July under martial law. All Ananda Marga centers and charitable activities are shut down and thousands of margiis are thrown in jail. Many are tortured and some are killed when they refuse to denounce BABA in court.
- **1977** - Martial law is repealed, Indira Gandhi loses power, and BABA is given a re-trial after being convicted to life imprisonment.
- **1978** - All false charges are thrown out and BABA is released from jail. Finally HE breaks the fast that continued on for five years, four months and two days.

- **1982** - BABA expounds a new philosophy called Neo-Humanism. On $30^{\text{th}}$ April, seventeen monks and nuns are surrounded on a bridge by Communist goondas and brutally murdered. On $14^{\text{th}}$ September, BABA composes HIS first song. By 1990, eight years later, HIS songs will have totaled 5018, more than doubling the previous world record set by Rabindranath Tagore (which took his whole life).
- **1986** - BABA invents a new theory of science called MICROVITA
- **1990** - On $7^{\text{th}}$ September, BABA founds Gurukul, a university in India.
- **$21^{\text{st}}$ October, 1990** - BABA leaves HIS physical body (Mahaprayan).

BABA's physical life only lasted sixty-nine years, but in that span, HE founded many worldwide organizations; dictated over two hundred books on spiritual philosophy; invented the theory of PROUT and went on to write twenty-one books on the subject; wrote four books on sociology and invented the theory of "the Social Cycle"; expounded the philosophy of Neo-Humanism, which to date has been installed in over one thousand schools; created a comprehensive curriculum that goes from the preschool to post-graduate level; initiated the Master Units program worldwide and built up Ananda Nagar from the ground; founded a university called Gurukul; started numerous plant and animal sanctuaries worldwide; wrote twenty-one books worth of short stories, as well as several other plays and dramas; wrote books on grammar for four different languages including English; dictated from 1985 to 1990 a twenty-six part unfinished encyclopedia on the Bengali language; authored a book on ayurvedic, yogic and herbal medicinal treatments; authored a book on yoga psychology and bio-psychology; wrote three books on farming in which HE invented the system of "water harvesting," which was used as the groundwork for the extremely successful Ananda Nagar project; invented the theory of Microvita, which has subsequently interested scientists worldwide, goading them to eventually found the Institute for Microvita Research after HIS Mahaprayan; wrote one book on the history of letters and phonetics as well as eight books on philology; composed 5018 songs in many different languages in the last eight years preceding HIS Mahaprayan; and finally, provided spiritual guidance and inspiration to countless thousands of people all across the world with HIS spiritual practices, with HIS love, and with HIS personal care and attention.

BABA's life is unparalleled.

### MEDITATION TO REALIZE BABA

BABA is always with you – you are never alone. BABA is the subject and we are HIS objects. HE is the creator and we are all created in HIS mind. HE is the Supreme Father, and we are all thus HIS mental children. One can realize BABA by doing meditation twice a day. The process for an introductory meditation is explained below. To learn a more advanced, personal technique, go to www.anandamarga.org to find the location of the nearest Ananda Marga center. Ananda Marga acaryas are always willing to teach meditation personally to anybody, free of charge.

PREPARATION: First wash the legs from the knees downwards, then the arms from the elbows towards the hands. Next, keep some water in the mouth while splashing the eyes at least twelve times. Spit out the water. This causes the "diver's reflex," which automatically relaxes the nervous system. Inhale water through the nose and spit it out through the mouth, repeat it four times.

UNIVERSALISM: All human beings are one family. BABA is the Supreme Father and we are all HIS children. Therefore, no narrow fissiparous tendency should ever be accepted. Anything that divides humanity directly opposes Universalism. Rather, try and encourage the idea that we are a part of One Human Society. Revolutionary marriages that are inter-national, inter-racial or inter-caste greatly help in breaking down the artificial barriers we create from person to person and place to place.

FOLLOW: Yama and Niyama, ten ancient moral universal principles. Follow these strictly for real spiritual and moral progress.

YAMA:

1) AHIMSA – Not to harm anybody
2) SATYA – Benevolent truthfulness
3) ASTEYA – Non-stealing
4) BRAHMACARYA – To perceive BRAHMA (God) in all expressions of the universe.
5) APARIGRAHA – Simple-living; the avoidance of unnecessary luxuries.

NIYAMA:

1) SHAOCA – Internal and external purity and cleanliness
2) SANTOSHA – Contentment and satisfaction; mental equilibrium
3) TAPAH – Selfless service. Think that BRAHMA has come into a form and you are serving HIM in that form.
4) SVADHYAYA – Try and keep good and uplifting company; group meditation once a week; reading spiritual books.
5) ISHVARA PRANIDHANA – Meditate and think that you are an inseparable part of the Supreme Consciousness (BABA).

KIIRTAN DANCE: Sing BABA NAM KEVALAM (Supreme Consciousness is all there is or love is all there is) while keeping the mind concentrated at the top of the head (the Sahasrara cakra). Hold the hands together at the level of the heart or raised above the head, imagining that you are reaching or surrendering to the Supreme. Alternately step by tapping the big toe of one foot behind the heel of the other while bending the knees. Touching the toe in this manner triggers a reaction in the pineal gland at the top of the head, causing the mind to relax. You can sing BABA NAM KEVALAM to almost any inspiring tune.

MEDITATION: Sit in a cross-legged position, close your eyes and think in your mind that the Supreme Consciousness (BABA) is all there – all around you and within you. You are one with That. While you are ideating on this feeling, repeat BABA NAM as you breathe in (always through the nose) and KEVALAM as you breathe out. Feel that you are becoming one with the Infinite Consciousness (BABA). Practice this twice a day for at least fifteen minutes. Try and repeat this mantra with every breath, twenty-four hours a day, whether you are working, driving, bathing, eating, etc. BABA NAM KEVALAM. This will help your meditation tremendously – it will in fact change your life.

BABA had given guarantee in HIS Supreme Command that one will get liberated in this life if one performs Sadhana (Meditation) twice a day. None could declare in the past in this way.

THE SUPREME COMMAND:

> Those who *perform* Sadhana twice a day regularly....
> Liberation is a sure guarantee....
> Lords command is also to follow Yama Niyama......
> It is the part and parcel of Sadhana to lead others along the path of righteousness - BABA.

AIM OF LIFE: Liberation of self and service to the suffering humanity. Do meditation for self-realization and do maximum service to the suffering humanity. Not only will this make your life blissful and full of fulfillment, but will really make your life count for something.

SERVICE: Save the money you would usually spend on habits such as tobacco or alcohol or the money you would usually spend on different luxuries and use it to help the poor and needy. Ananda Marga runs hundreds of social service projects worldwide that are in constant need of financial assistance.
